S系列 - 电气安全模型

§1 S-01 阻性漏电分离监测

1.1 功能描述

模型名称: 阻性漏电分离监测 英文名称: Resistive Leakage Current Separation Monitoring 技术等级: L1（核心模型，即插即用）

利用零序电流相角与A相电压相角计算相位差，将剩余电流分离为阻性分量和容性分量。阻性漏电是绝缘破损的直接表征，是电气火灾和人身触电的主要风险源。

1.2 核心算法公式

相位差计算:
    θ = φ_I0 - φ_Ua
    其中: φ_I0为零序电流相角, φ_Ua为A相电压相角

阻性漏电电流:
    I_R = I_0 × cos(θ)

容性漏电电流:
    I_C = I_0 × sin(θ)

阻性漏电比例:
    Ratio_R = I_R / I_0 = cos(θ)

告警判定:
    if I_R > 30mA (家用标准) or I_R > 100mA (工业标准):
        触发阻性漏电告警

1.3 参考代码

"""
S-01 阻性漏电分离监测
=====================
功能: 将剩余电流分离为阻性分量和容性分量，识别绝缘破损风险
输入: 零序电流(I0), A相电压(Ua), 零序电流相角(phi_I0), A相电压相角(phi_Ua)
输出: 阻性漏电电流, 容性漏电电流, 告警状态
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional

class ResistiveLeakageMonitor:
    """阻性漏电分离监测模型"""

    def __init__(self, 
                 residential_threshold_ma: float = 30.0,
                 industrial_threshold_ma: float = 100.0,
                 window_size: int = 100):
        """
        初始化参数:
            residential_threshold_ma: 家用漏电保护阈值 (mA)
            industrial_threshold_ma: 工业漏电保护阈值 (mA)
            window_size: 滑动窗口大小
        """
        self.residential_threshold = residential_threshold_ma  # mA
        self.industrial_threshold = industrial_threshold_ma    # mA
        self.window_size = window_size
        self.buffer = {
            'I0': [],
            'phi_I0': [],
            'phi_Ua': []
        }

    def _phase_difference(self, phi1: np.ndarray, phi2: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        计算相位差 (考虑角度Wrap Around)
        公式: θ = φ1 - φ2
        """
        diff = phi1 - phi2
        # 归一化到 [-π, π]
        diff = np.arctan2(np.sin(diff), np.cos(diff))
        return diff

    def separate_leakage(self, 
                        I0: np.ndarray, 
                        phi_I0: np.ndarray, 
                        phi_Ua: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        """
        分离阻性和容性漏电电流

        参数:
            I0: 零序电流幅值数组 (mA)
            phi_I0: 零序电流相角数组 (弧度)
            phi_Ua: A相电压相角数组 (弧度)

        返回:
            I_R: 阻性漏电电流 (mA)
            I_C: 容性漏电电流 (mA)
        """
        # 计算相位差
        theta = self._phase_difference(phi_I0, phi_Ua)

        # 分离分量
        # 阻性分量: 与电压同相位的电流分量
        I_R = I0 * np.abs(np.cos(theta))

        # 容性分量: 与电压90度相位的电流分量
        I_C = I0 * np.abs(np.sin(theta))

        return I_R, I_C

    def calculate_resistive_ratio(self, I_R: float, I0: float) -> float:
        """
        计算阻性漏电比例
        公式: Ratio = I_R / I_0 = cos(θ)
        """
        if I0 <= 0:
            return 0.0
        return min(1.0, abs(I_R / I0))

    def detect_leakage_type(self, 
                           I_R: float, 
                           I_C: float, 
                           total_current: float,
                           mode: str = 'residential') -> Dict:
        """
        判断漏电类型

        参数:
            I_R: 阻性漏电电流 (mA)
            I_C: 容性漏电电流 (mA)
            total_current: 总剩余电流 (mA)
            mode: 运行模式 ('residential' 或 'industrial')

        返回:
            诊断结果字典
        """
        threshold = (self.residential_threshold if mode == 'residential' 
                   else self.industrial_threshold)

        ratio_R = self.calculate_resistive_ratio(I_R, total_current)

        # 判断逻辑
        if ratio_R > 0.8:
            diagnosis = "高阻性漏电"
            risk_level = "HIGH"
            description = "疑似绝缘破损，应立即检查"
        elif ratio_R > 0.5:
            diagnosis = "混合型漏电"
            risk_level = "MEDIUM"
            description = "存在阻性和容性漏电成分"
        elif ratio_R > 0.2:
            diagnosis = "低阻性漏电"
            risk_level = "LOW"
            description = "以容性漏电为主，可能由线路对地电容引起"
        else:
            diagnosis = "纯容性漏电"
            risk_level = "INFO"
            description = "正常对地电容电流，无需处理"

        return {
            'diagnosis': diagnosis,
            'risk_level': risk_level,
            'description': description,
            'resistive_current_ma': I_R,
            'capacitive_current_ma': I_C,
            'resistive_ratio': ratio_R,
            'threshold_ma': threshold,
            'is_alarm': I_R > threshold
        }

    def update_buffer(self, I0: float, phi_I0: float, phi_Ua: float):
        """更新滑动窗口数据缓冲区"""
        self.buffer['I0'].append(I0)
        self.buffer['phi_I0'].append(phi_I0)
        self.buffer['phi_Ua'].append(phi_Ua)

        # 保持窗口大小
        if len(self.buffer['I0']) > self.window_size:
            self.buffer['I0'].pop(0)
            self.buffer['phi_I0'].pop(0)
            self.buffer['phi_Ua'].pop(0)

    def run(self, 
           I0: float, 
           phi_I0: float, 
           phi_Ua: float,
           mode: str = 'residential') -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数

        参数:
            I0: 零序电流 (mA)
            phi_I0: 零序电流相角 (度)
            phi_Ua: A相电压相角 (度)
            mode: 模式 ('residential' 或 'industrial')

        返回:
            完整诊断结果
        """
        # 转换为弧度
        phi_I0_rad = np.deg2rad(phi_I0)
        phi_Ua_rad = np.deg2rad(phi_Ua)

        # 更新缓冲区
        self.update_buffer(I0, phi_I0_rad, phi_Ua_rad)

        # 计算相位差
        theta = self._phase_difference(phi_I0_rad, phi_Ua_rad)

        # 分离漏电分量
        I_R, I_C = self.separate_leakage(
            np.array([I0]),
            np.array([phi_I0_rad]),
            np.array([phi_Ua_rad])
        )

        # 诊断
        result = self.detect_leakage_type(
            I_R[0], I_C[0], I0, mode
        )

        # 添加元数据
        result['phase_difference_deg'] = np.rad2deg(theta)
        result['model_id'] = 'S-01'
        result['timestamp'] = pd.Timestamp.now()

        return result


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    # 初始化模型
    monitor = ResistiveLeakageMonitor(
        residential_threshold_ma=30.0,
        industrial_threshold_ma=100.0
    )

    # 模拟数据
    # 场景1: 纯阻性漏电 (绝缘破损)
    result1 = monitor.run(
        I0=150.0,           # 总剩余电流 150mA
        phi_I0=5.0,         # 零序电流相角 (几乎同相位)
        phi_Ua=0.0,         # A相电压相角
        mode='residential'
    )
    print("场景1 - 纯阻性漏电:")
    print(f"  诊断: {result1['diagnosis']}")
    print(f"  风险等级: {result1['risk_level']}")
    print(f"  阻性漏电: {result1['resistive_current_ma']:.2f} mA")
    print(f"  容性漏电: {result1['capacitive_current_ma']:.2f} mA")
    print(f"  告警: {'是' if result1['is_alarm'] else '否'}")
    print()

    # 场景2: 纯容性漏电 (正常电容)
    result2 = monitor.run(
        I0=50.0,            # 总剩余电流 50mA
        phi_I0=90.0,        # 零序电流相角 (90度相位差)
        phi_Ua=0.0,         # A相电压相角
        mode='residential'
    )
    print("场景2 - 纯容性漏电:")
    print(f"  诊断: {result2['diagnosis']}")
    print(f"  风险等级: {result2['risk_level']}")
    print(f"  阻性漏电: {result2['resistive_current_ma']:.2f} mA")
    print(f"  容性漏电: {result2['capacitive_current_ma']:.2f} mA")
    print(f"  告警: {'是' if result2['is_alarm'] else '否'}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

1.4 配置示例

# configs/safety/S-01_resistive_leakage.yaml
model:
  model_id: "S-01"
  model_name: "阻性漏电分离监测"
  model_name_en: "resistive_leakage_separation"
  category: "safety"
  tech_level: "L1"
  version: "1.0.0"

data_source:
  type: "kafka"
  topic: "taiyi.electrical.raw.v1"
  input_fields:
    - "zero_sequence_current"      # 零序电流 (mA)
    - "zero_sequence_current_angle"  # 零序电流相角 (度)
    - "phase_A_voltage_angle"     # A相电压相角 (度)
  window:
    type: "sliding"
    size: 100
    step: 1

pipeline:
  - id: "convert_to_radians"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "deg2rad(${input.angles})"
    output_as: "angles_rad"

  - id: "calculate_phase_diff"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "phase_difference(${input.phi_I0_rad}, ${input.phi_Ua_rad})"
    output_as: "theta"

  - id: "separate_leakage"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "I_R = ${input.I0} * abs(cos(theta)); I_C = ${input.I0} * abs(sin(theta))"
    output_as: "I_R, I_C"

  - id: "evaluate"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "threshold_rule"
    params:
      operator: ">"
      threshold: 30  # mA
    inputs:
      value: "${separate_leakage.I_R}"
    output_as: "alarm_trigger"

output:
  type: "alert"
  level: "warning"
  topic: "tianyan.alerts.v1"
  fields:
    resistive_current_ma: "${separate_leakage.I_R}"
    capacitive_current_ma: "${separate_leakage.I_C}"
    resistive_ratio: "${separate_leakage.I_R} / ${input.I0}"

§2 S-02 容性漏电趋势分析

2.1 功能描述

模型名称: 容性漏电趋势分析 英文名称: Capacitive Leakage Current Trend Analysis 技术等级: L1

分析剩余电流的长周期趋势，判断剩余电流增长是由绝缘破损（阻性漏电）还是由谐波引起的容性耦合增加导致。通过LOWESS趋势滤波和相关性分析，避免误报警。

2.2 核心算法公式

LOWESS (Locally Weighted Scatterplot Smoothing):
    ŷ(x) = Σ w_i(x) × y_i / Σ w_i(x)
    其中: w_i(x) = (1 - |(x - x_i)/h|³)³  当 |x - x_i| < h 时

谐波-容性耦合关系:
    I_C_harmonic = f(THD, I_0_base)
    相关性系数: r = Cov(I_C, THD) / (σ_I_C × σ_THD)

趋势判断:
    if ΔI_R/Δt > 阈值: "阻性漏电增长趋势"
    elif r(I_C, THD) > 0.8: "谐波容性耦合主导"
    else: "需进一步观察"

2.3 参考代码

"""
S-02 容性漏电趋势分析
=====================
功能: 分析剩余电流趋势，区分阻性漏电增长和容性耦合增加
输入: 剩余电流, 电流总谐波畸变率(THD), 时间序列
输出: 趋势分析结果, 漏电类型判断
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy.signal import savgol_filter
from scipy.stats import pearsonr
from typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional

class CapacitiveLeakageTrendAnalyzer:
    """容性漏电趋势分析模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 trend_window_days: int = 30,
                 lowess_fraction: float = 0.3,
                 harmonic_correlation_threshold: float = 0.8,
                 growth_rate_threshold: float = 0.1):
        """
        参数:
            trend_window_days: 趋势分析窗口天数
            lowess_fraction: LOWESS平滑参数
            harmonic_correlation_threshold: 谐波相关性阈值
            growth_rate_threshold: 阻性漏电增长率阈值
        """
        self.trend_window = trend_window_days
        self.lowess_fraction = lowess_fraction
        self.harmonic_threshold = harmonic_correlation_threshold
        self.growth_threshold = growth_rate_threshold
        self.history = []

    def lowess_smooth(self, x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, 
                     frac: float = 0.3) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        LOWESS趋势平滑

        公式: ŷ(x) = Σ w_i(x) × y_i / Σ w_i(x)
        权重: w_i(x) = (1 - d³)³, 当 d < 1

        参数:
            x: 自变量 (时间)
            y: 因变量 (漏电电流)
            frac: 平滑窗口比例

        返回:
            平滑后的趋势值
        """
        n = len(x)
        y_smooth = np.zeros(n)

        # 使用移动窗口加权平均近似LOWESS
        window_size = max(int(n * frac), 5)
        half_window = window_size // 2

        for i in range(n):
            # 确定窗口范围
            start = max(0, i - half_window)
            end = min(n, i + half_window + 1)

            # 计算距离权重
            x_window = x[start:end]
            y_window = y[start:end]
            d = np.abs(x_window - x[i]) / (x_window.max() - x_window.min() + 1e-10)

            # Tricube权重
            weights = np.where(d < 1, (1 - d**3)**3, 0)

            # 加权平均
            if weights.sum() > 0:
                y_smooth[i] = np.sum(weights * y_window) / np.sum(weights)
            else:
                y_smooth[i] = y[i]

        return y_smooth

    def calculate_trend_growth_rate(self, 
                                   trend_values: np.ndarray,
                                   time_axis: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        计算趋势增长率

        使用线性回归斜率:
            rate = d(trend) / dt

        返回:
            日均增长率 (mA/天)
        """
        # 线性回归
        n = len(trend_values)
        if n < 2:
            return 0.0

        # 计算斜率
        x_mean = time_axis.mean()
        y_mean = trend_values.mean()

        numerator = np.sum((time_axis - x_mean) * (trend_values - y_mean))
        denominator = np.sum((time_axis - x_mean) ** 2)

        if denominator < 1e-10:
            return 0.0

        slope = numerator / denominator
        return slope

    def calculate_harmonic_correlation(self,
                                     I_capasitive: np.ndarray,
                                     THD: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """
        计算容性电流与谐波畸变率的相关性

        公式: r = Cov(I_C, THD) / (σ_IC × σ_THD)

        参数:
            I_capasitive: 容性漏电电流序列
            THD: 总谐波畸变率序列

        返回:
            (相关系数, p值)
        """
        if len(I_capasitive) < 5:
            return 0.0, 1.0

        # 过滤异常值
        mask = (I_capasitive > 0) & (THD > 0) & (THD < 100)
        if mask.sum() < 5:
            return 0.0, 1.0

        correlation, p_value = pearsonr(
            I_capasitive[mask], 
            THD[mask]
        )
        return correlation, p_value

    def analyze_trend(self,
                     time_series: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:
        """
        综合趋势分析

        参数:
            time_series: 包含 'residual_current', 'THD', 'timestamp' 的DataFrame

        返回:
            趋势分析结果
        """
        # 提取数据
        residual_current = time_series['residual_current'].values
        THD = time_series['THD'].values

        # 时间归一化
        t = np.arange(len(residual_current))

        # LOWESS趋势平滑
        trend_smoothed = self.lowess_smooth(t, residual_current, self.lowess_fraction)

        # 计算阻性漏电趋势（假设与剩余电流变化趋势相关）
        growth_rate = self.calculate_trend_growth_rate(trend_smoothed, t)

        # 计算谐波相关性
        harmonic_corr, p_value = self.calculate_harmonic_correlation(
            residual_current, THD
        )

        # 诊断判断
        diagnosis = self._make_diagnosis(
            growth_rate, harmonic_corr, residual_current[-1]
        )

        return {
            'diagnosis': diagnosis['type'],
            'description': diagnosis['description'],
            'risk_level': diagnosis['risk_level'],
            'growth_rate_ma_per_day': growth_rate,
            'harmonic_correlation': harmonic_corr,
            'p_value': p_value,
            'is_harmonic_dominant': harmonic_corr > self.harmonic_threshold,
            'is_resistive_leakage': growth_rate > self.growth_threshold * 
                                   np.mean(residual_current),
            'trend_values': trend_smoothed.tolist(),
            'model_id': 'S-02'
        }

    def _make_diagnosis(self,
                       growth_rate: float,
                       harmonic_corr: float,
                       current_value: float) -> Dict:
        """综合诊断逻辑"""

        # 标准化增长率
        normalized_growth = growth_rate / (current_value + 1e-10)

        if normalized_growth > 0.05 and harmonic_corr > 0.7:
            return {
                'type': '混合型增长',
                'description': '阻性漏电和容性耦合均有增长，谐波影响显著',
                'risk_level': 'MEDIUM'
            }
        elif harmonic_corr > self.harmonic_threshold:
            return {
                'type': '谐波容性耦合主导',
                'description': '剩余电流增长主要由谐波引起，非绝缘破损',
                'risk_level': 'LOW'
            }
        elif normalized_growth > 0.05:
            return {
                'type': '阻性漏电增长趋势',
                'description': '剩余电流持续增长，疑似绝缘破损，请检查',
                'risk_level': 'HIGH'
            }
        else:
            return {
                'type': '正常波动',
                'description': '剩余电流在正常范围内波动',
                'risk_level': 'INFO'
            }

    def add_data_point(self, residual_current: float, THD: float, 
                      timestamp: pd.Timestamp):
        """添加数据点到历史缓冲区"""
        self.history.append({
            'timestamp': timestamp,
            'residual_current': residual_current,
            'THD': THD
        })

        # 保持历史窗口大小
        if len(self.history) > self.trend_window * 24 * 60:  # 假设1分钟1个点
            self.history.pop(0)

    def run(self, residual_current: float, THD: float,
           timestamp: Optional[pd.Timestamp] = None) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数

        参数:
            residual_current: 剩余电流 (mA)
            THD: 电流总谐波畸变率 (%)
            timestamp: 时间戳

        返回:
            诊断结果
        """
        if timestamp is None:
            timestamp = pd.Timestamp.now()

        # 添加数据点
        self.add_data_point(residual_current, THD, timestamp)

        # 转换为DataFrame
        df = pd.DataFrame(self.history)

        if len(df) < 10:
            return {
                'diagnosis': '数据不足',
                'description': f'仅{len(df)}个数据点，需要至少10个进行分析',
                'model_id': 'S-02'
            }

        return self.analyze_trend(df)


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    analyzer = CapacitiveLeakageTrendAnalyzer(
        trend_window_days=30,
        lowess_fraction=0.3,
        harmonic_correlation_threshold=0.8
    )

    # 模拟数据：谐波主导场景
    np.random.seed(42)
    n_points = 500

    for i in range(n_points):
        # 模拟谐波容性耦合
        THD = 5 + 3 * np.sin(i / 20) + np.random.normal(0, 0.5)
        residual_current = 20 + 15 * np.sin(i / 20) + np.random.normal(0, 1)

        analyzer.add_data_point(
            residual_current=residual_current,
            THD=THD,
            timestamp=pd.Timestamp.now() - pd.Timedelta(minutes=n_points-i)
        )

    # 运行分析
    df = pd.DataFrame(analyzer.history)
    result = analyzer.analyze_trend(df)

    print("谐波主导场景分析结果:")
    print(f"  诊断类型: {result['diagnosis']}")
    print(f"  风险等级: {result['risk_level']}")
    print(f"  日均增长率: {result['growth_rate_ma_per_day']:.4f} mA/天")
    print(f"  谐波相关性: {result['harmonic_correlation']:.4f}")
    print(f"  谐波主导: {'是' if result['is_harmonic_dominant'] else '否'}")
    print(f"  阻性漏电: {'是' if result['is_resistive_leakage'] else '否'}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

2.4 配置示例

# configs/safety/S-02_capacitive_leakage_trend.yaml
model:
  model_id: "S-02"
  model_name: "容性漏电趋势分析"
  model_name_en: "capacitive_leakage_trend"
  category: "safety"
  tech_level: "L1"

data_source:
  type: "kafka"
  topic: "taiyi.electrical.raw.v1"
  input_fields:
    - "residual_current"    # 剩余电流 (mA)
    - "THD"                # 电流总谐波畸变率 (%)
  window:
    type: "sliding"
    size: 43200   # 30天 * 24小时 * 60分钟
    step: 60      # 每小时更新

pipeline:
  - id: "lowess_filter"
    type: "wanxiang"
    operator: "trend_filter"
    params:
      method: "lowess"
      frac: 0.3
    inputs:
      - "${input.residual_current}"
    output_as: "trend_smoothed"

  - id: "calculate_correlation"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "corr(${input.residual_current}, ${input.THD})"
    output_as: "harmonic_corr"

  - id: "evaluate"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "|
        is_harmonic = harmonic_corr > 0.8
        growth_rate = (trend_smoothed[-1] - trend_smoothed[0]) / len(trend_smoothed)
        is_resistive_growth = growth_rate > 0.05
        diagnosis = '谐波主导' if is_harmonic and not is_resistive_growth 
                 else '阻性增长' if is_resistive_growth 
                 else '正常'"
    output_as: "diagnosis_result"

output:
  type: "alert"
  level: "info"
  topic: "tianyan.alerts.v1"
  fields:
    diagnosis: "${diagnosis_result}"
    harmonic_correlation: "${harmonic_corr}"
    trend_values: "${trend_smoothed}"

§3 S-03 间歇性电弧故障检测

3.1 功能描述

模型名称: 间歇性电弧故障检测 英文名称: Intermittent Arc Fault Detection 技术等级: L2a（核心难点，需要专用模型）

检测串联电弧故障特征，识别接头松动、绝缘老化等引起的间歇性电弧。使用小波包变换和奇异值分解提取电弧特征。

3.2 核心算法公式

电弧伏安特性（平肩部特征）:
    当电弧燃烧时，电压出现平坦区域
    V_arc ≈ constant (不随电流变化)

小波包分解:
    WPT(p, k) = Σ x[n] × ψ_{p,k}[n]
    其中 ψ_{p,k} 为小波包基函数

奇异值分解特征提取:
    [U, S, V] = svd(X)
    奇异值向量 S 表征信号能量分布

电弧判定条件:
    if (高频能量比 > 阈值) AND (平肩部特征存在):
        判定为电弧故障

3.3 参考代码

"""
S-03 间歇性电弧故障检测
=======================
功能: 检测串联电弧故障特征，识别接头松动和绝缘老化
输入: A/B/C相电流波形, 剩余电流波形
输出: 电弧检测结果, 电弧位置估计
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import signal
from scipy.linalg import svd
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional

class ArcFaultDetector:
    """间歇性电弧故障检测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 fs: float = 12800,
                 fundamental_freq: float = 50.0,
                 wavelet: str = 'db4',
                 decomposition_level: int = 5,
                 arc_energy_ratio_threshold: float = 0.15,
                 flatShoulder_threshold: float = 0.3):
        """
        参数:
            fs: 采样频率 (Hz)
            fundamental_freq: 基波频率 (Hz)
            wavelet: 小波基函数
            decomposition_level: 分解层数
            arc_energy_ratio_threshold: 高频能量比阈值
            flatShoulder_threshold: 平肩部特征阈值
        """
        self.fs = fs
        self.fundamental_freq = fundamental_freq
        self.wavelet = wavelet
        self.level = decomposition_level
        self.arc_threshold = arc_energy_ratio_threshold
        self.flat_threshold = flatShoulder_threshold

    def wavelet_packet_decomposition(self, 
                                     x: np.ndarray) -> List[np.ndarray]:
        """
        小波包分解

        将信号分解为不同频带的子信号
        分解层数 L -> 2^L 个频带

        参数:
            x: 输入信号

        返回:
            分解后的子频带信号列表
        """
        # 简化的Mallat算法
        from scipy.signal import wavelets

        coeffs = wavelets.wavedec(x, self.wavelet, level=self.level)

        # 重构每个细节系数
        subbands = []
        for detail in coeffs[1:]:  # 跳过近似系数
            # 上采样并滤波重构
            upsampled = np.zeros(len(x))
            upsampled[::2] = detail
            subbands.append(upsampled)

        return subbands

    def calculate_high_frequency_energy_ratio(self,
                                            x: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        计算高频能量比

        高频能量 = Σ(细节系数)²
        总能量 = Σ(所有系数)²
        比例 = 高频能量 / 总能量

        参数:
            x: 输入电流信号

        返回:
            高频能量比
        """
        # 使用带通滤波器提取高频成分
        nyquist = self.fs / 2

        # 2-10kHz 高通滤波
        b, a = signal.butter(4, [2000/nyquist, 1.0], btype='bandpass')
        high_freq = signal.filtfilt(b, a, x)

        # 0-2kHz 低频滤波
        b_low, a_low = signal.butter(4, 2000/nyquist, btype='low')
        low_freq = signal.filtfilt(b_low, a_low, x)

        # 计算能量比
        energy_high = np.sum(high_freq ** 2)
        energy_total = np.sum(x ** 2)

        if energy_total < 1e-10:
            return 0.0

        return energy_high / energy_total

    def detect_flat_shoulder(self, 
                            voltage: np.ndarray,
                            current: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[bool, float]:
        """
        检测电弧平肩部特征

        电弧燃烧时，电压-电流特性出现平坦区域
        通过分析V-I曲线的斜率变化检测

        参数:
            voltage: 电压波形
            current: 电流波形

        返回:
            (是否检测到平肩部, 平肩部指数)
        """
        # 计算瞬时功率
        power = voltage * current

        # 检测过零点
        zero_crossings = np.where(np.diff(np.sign(current)))[0]

        if len(zero_crossings) < 2:
            return False, 0.0

        # 分析每个半周期
        flat_shoulder_indices = []

        for i in range(len(zero_crossings) - 1):
            start = zero_crossings[i]
            end = zero_crossings[i + 1]

            segment_current = current[start:end]
            segment_voltage = voltage[start:end]

            # 计算瞬时电阻
            R_inst = segment_voltage / (segment_current + 1e-10)

            # 计算电阻变化率
            dR = np.abs(np.diff(R_inst))

            # 平肩部特征: 电阻变化率接近0的区域
            flat_regions = np.where(dR < np.percentile(dR, 10))[0]
            flat_ratio = len(flat_regions) / len(dR)

            flat_shoulder_indices.append(flat_ratio)

        # 综合判断
        avg_flat_ratio = np.mean(flat_shoulder_indices)
        has_flat_shoulder = avg_flat_ratio > self.flat_threshold

        return has_flat_shoulder, avg_flat_ratio

    def compute_svd_features(self, x: np.ndarray, 
                            window_size: int = 256) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        计算SVD特征

        将信号构造成Hankel矩阵，进行奇异值分解
        奇异值谱作为信号特征

        参数:
            x: 输入信号
            window_size: 窗口大小

        返回:
            奇异值向量
        """
        n = len(x) - window_size + 1

        # 构造Hankel矩阵
        H = np.zeros((window_size, n))
        for i in range(n):
            H[:, i] = x[i:i+window_size]

        # SVD分解
        try:
            U, S, Vh = svd(H, full_matrices=False)
            return S / S.sum()  # 归一化奇异值
        except:
            return np.array([])

    def detect_arc_fault(self,
                        current: np.ndarray,
                        voltage: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
                        residual_current: Optional[np.ndarray] = None) -> Dict:
        """
        综合电弧故障检测

        参数:
            current: 相电流波形
            voltage: 相电压波形（可选）
            residual_current: 剩余电流波形（可选）

        返回:
            电弧检测结果
        """
        results = {
            'is_arc_detected': False,
            'arc_probability': 0.0,
            'features': {},
            'diagnosis': '',
            'model_id': 'S-03'
        }

        # 特征1: 高频能量比
        high_freq_ratio = self.calculate_high_frequency_energy_ratio(current)
        results['features']['high_freq_energy_ratio'] = high_freq_ratio

        # 特征2: 小波包高频细节能量
        subbands = self.wavelet_packet_decomposition(current)
        if len(subbands) > 0:
            wavelet_high_energy = np.sum(subbands[-2:] ** 2) / (np.sum(current ** 2) + 1e-10)
            results['features']['wavelet_high_energy'] = wavelet_high_energy
        else:
            wavelet_high_energy = 0.0

        # 特征3: 平肩部特征
        flat_shoulder_score = 0.0
        if voltage is not None:
            has_flat, flat_score = self.detect_flat_shoulder(voltage, current)
            results['features']['flat_shoulder_detected'] = has_flat
            results['features']['flat_shoulder_score'] = flat_score
            flat_shoulder_score = flat_score

        # 特征4: SVD特征熵
        svd_features = self.compute_svd_features(current)
        if len(svd_features) > 0:
            svd_entropy = -np.sum(svd_features * np.log(svd_features + 1e-10))
            results['features']['svd_entropy'] = svd_entropy
        else:
            svd_entropy = 0.0

        # 综合评分
        # 电弧特征: 高频能量增加 + 平肩部特征
        score_high_freq = min(1.0, high_freq_ratio / self.arc_threshold)
        score_wavelet = min(1.0, wavelet_high_energy / 0.1)
        score_flat = flat_shoulder_score if voltage is not None else 0.0

        arc_score = 0.4 * score_high_freq + 0.3 * score_wavelet + 0.3 * score_flat

        results['arc_probability'] = arc_score
        results['is_arc_detected'] = arc_score > 0.5

        # 诊断描述
        if arc_score > 0.7:
            results['diagnosis'] = "高置信度电弧故障检测，建议立即检查"
            results['risk_level'] = 'HIGH'
        elif arc_score > 0.5:
            results['diagnosis'] = "疑似电弧故障，建议排查接头和绝缘"
            results['risk_level'] = 'MEDIUM'
        elif arc_score > 0.3:
            results['diagnosis'] = "存在轻微电弧特征，建议持续关注"
            results['risk_level'] = 'LOW'
        else:
            results['diagnosis'] = "未检测到电弧故障特征"
            results['risk_level'] = 'INFO'

        return results

    def run(self, 
           phase_currents: Dict[str, np.ndarray],
           phase_voltages: Optional[Dict[str, np.ndarray]] = None,
           residual_current: Optional[np.ndarray] = None) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数

        参数:
            phase_currents: 三相电流波形 {'A': array, 'B': array, 'C': array}
            phase_voltages: 三相电压波形（可选）
            residual_current: 剩余电流波形（可选）

        返回:
            综合检测结果
        """
        results = {
            'model_id': 'S-03',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'phases': {}
        }

        # 逐相检测
        for phase in ['A', 'B', 'C']:
            if phase in phase_currents:
                voltage = phase_voltages.get(phase) if phase_voltages else None

                phase_result = self.detect_arc_fault(
                    phase_currents[phase],
                    voltage,
                    residual_current
                )
                results['phases'][phase] = phase_result

        # 汇总判断
        arc_detected_phases = [
            p for p, r in results['phases'].items() 
            if r['is_arc_detected']
        ]

        max_probability = max(
            r['arc_probability'] for r in results['phases'].values()
        )

        results['is_arc_detected'] = len(arc_detected_phases) > 0
        results['max_probability'] = max_probability
        results['affected_phases'] = arc_detected_phases

        return results


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    detector = ArcFaultDetector(
        fs=12800,
        fundamental_freq=50.0,
        wavelet='db4',
        arc_energy_ratio_threshold=0.15
    )

    # 模拟正常电流波形
    t = np.arange(1280) / 12800  # 0.1秒数据
    fundamental = 50  # Hz

    # 正常电流
    normal_current = 10 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * fundamental * t)

    # 模拟电弧电流（加入高频噪声和畸变）
    arc_current = normal_current.copy()
    # 添加高频成分
    arc_current += 2 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 500 * t)
    arc_current += 1 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 1000 * t)
    # 添加随机噪声
    arc_current += 0.5 * np.random.randn(len(t))

    # 检测电弧
    result = detector.detect_arc_fault(arc_current)

    print("电弧故障检测结果:")
    print(f"  电弧检测: {'是' if result['is_arc_detected'] else '否'}")
    print(f"  电弧概率: {result['arc_probability']:.2%}")
    print(f"  诊断: {result['diagnosis']}")
    print(f"  风险等级: {result['risk_level']}")
    print(f"  高频能量比: {result['features']['high_freq_energy_ratio']:.4f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

3.4 配置示例

# configs/safety/S-03_arc_fault_detection.yaml
model:
  model_id: "S-03"
  model_name: "间歇性电弧故障检测"
  model_name_en: "intermittent_arc_fault_detection"
  category: "safety"
  tech_level: "L2a"

data_source:
  type: "kafka"
  topic: "taiyi.electrical.waveform.v1"
  input_fields:
    - "Ia_waveform"    # A相电流波形
    - "Ib_waveform"    # B相电流波形
    - "Ic_waveform"    # C相电流波形
    - "Ua_waveform"    # A相电压波形
    - "I0_waveform"    # 剩余电流波形
  window:
    type: "sliding"
    size: 1280   # 0.1秒 @ 12800Hz
    step: 640

pipeline:
  - id: "wavelet_decomp"
    type: "wanxiang"
    operator: "wavelet_packet"
    params:
      wavelet: "db4"
      level: 5
    inputs:
      - "${input.Ia_waveform}"
    output_as: "wavelet_coeffs"

  - id: "high_freq_energy"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "sum(wavelet_coeffs.details[-2:]^2) / sum(wavelet_coeffs.^2)"
    output_as: "hf_ratio"

  - id: "flat_shoulder"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "detect_flat_shoulder(${input.Ua_waveform}, ${input.Ia_waveform})"
    output_as: "flat_score"

  - id: "svd_features"
    type: "wanxiang"
    operator: "svd_decomposition"
    params:
      window_size: 256
    inputs:
      - "${input.Ia_waveform}"
    output_as: "svd_spectrum"

  - id: "arc_classify"
    type: "wanxiang"
    operator: "classification_random_forest"
    params:
      model_id: "S03_arc_classifier_v1"
      features: ["hf_ratio", "flat_score", "svd_entropy"]
    inputs:
      - "${high_freq_energy}"
      - "${flat_shoulder}"
      - "${svd_features}"
    output_as: "arc_probability"

  - id: "evaluate"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "threshold_rule"
    params:
      operator: ">"
      threshold: 0.5
    inputs:
      value: "${arc_classify.probability}"
    output_as: "alarm_trigger"

output:
  type: "alert"
  level: "warning"
  topic: "tianyan.alerts.v1"
  fields:
    arc_detected: "${arc_classify.arc_detected}"
    probability: "${arc_classify.probability}"
    affected_phase: "${arc_classify.phase}"

§4 S-04 电气接头松动/氧化预警

4.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电气接头松动/氧化预警 英文名称: Electrical Joint Loosening/Oxidation Warning 技术等级: L2a

利用接头接触电阻非线性变化产生的谐波特征变化，检测接头松动和氧化问题。通过谐波指纹库余弦相似度对比实现早期预警。

4.2 核心算法公式

接触电阻非线性特性:
    R_contact = R_0 × (1 + α × I^β)
    当接头氧化/松动时，α 和 β 值增大

谐波指纹特征向量:
    F = [THD, I_3rd/I_1st, I_5th/I_1st, I_7th/I_1st, I_11th/I_1st, ...]

余弦相似度:
    cos(θ) = (F_current · F_baseline) / (||F_current|| × ||F_baseline||)

预警判定:
    if cos_similarity < 阈值:
        接头异常预警

4.3 参考代码

"""
S-04 电气接头松动/氧化预警
===========================
功能: 通过谐波指纹变化检测接头松动和氧化
输入: 各次谐波电流含有率, 基波电流
输出: 接头健康状态, 预警信息
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from collections import deque

class JointHealthMonitor:
    """电气接头健康监测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 baseline_window: int = 1000,
                 similarity_threshold: float = 0.85,
                 harmonic_orders: List[int] = None):
        """
        参数:
            baseline_window: 建立基准的样本数
            similarity_threshold: 相似度阈值
            harmonic_orders: 分析的谐波次数列表
        """
        self.baseline_window = baseline_window
        self.similarity_threshold = similarity_threshold

        if harmonic_orders is None:
            # 关注特征谐波: 3, 5, 7, 11, 13次
            self.harmonic_orders = [3, 5, 7, 11, 13]

        self.baseline_fingerprint = None
        self.baseline_samples = deque(maxlen=baseline_window)
        self.is_baseline_ready = False

    def calculate_harmonic_fingerprint(self,
                                     harmonic_currents: Dict[int, float],
                                     fundamental_current: float) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        计算谐波指纹特征向量

        公式: F = [I_h/I_1st for h in harmonic_orders]

        参数:
            harmonic_currents: 各次谐波电流有效值 {次数: 值}
            fundamental_current: 基波电流有效值

        返回:
            归一化的谐波指纹向量
        """
        if fundamental_current < 1e-6:
            return np.zeros(len(self.harmonic_orders))

        fingerprint = np.array([
            harmonic_currents.get(h, 0.0) / fundamental_current
            for h in self.harmonic_orders
        ])

        # 归一化
        norm = np.linalg.norm(fingerprint)
        if norm > 1e-10:
            fingerprint = fingerprint / norm

        return fingerprint

    def cosine_similarity(self,
                         v1: np.ndarray,
                         v2: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        计算余弦相似度

        公式: cos(θ) = (v1 · v2) / (||v1|| × ||v2||)

        参数:
            v1, v2: 两个特征向量

        返回:
            余弦相似度 [-1, 1]
        """
        norm1 = np.linalg.norm(v1)
        norm2 = np.linalg.norm(v2)

        if norm1 < 1e-10 or norm2 < 1e-10:
            return 0.0

        return np.dot(v1, v2) / (norm1 * norm2)

    def euclidean_distance(self,
                         v1: np.ndarray,
                         v2: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        计算欧氏距离

        公式: d = sqrt(Σ(v1_i - v2_i)²)
        """
        return np.linalg.norm(v1 - v2)

    def update_baseline(self, fingerprint: np.ndarray):
        """更新基准指纹库"""
        self.baseline_samples.append(fingerprint)

        if len(self.baseline_samples) >= self.baseline_window:
            # 计算平均基准
            self.baseline_fingerprint = np.mean(self.baseline_samples, axis=0)
            self.is_baseline_ready = True

    def detect_anomaly(self,
                     current_fingerprint: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """
        异常检测

        参数:
            current_fingerprint: 当前谐波指纹

        返回:
            异常检测结果
        """
        results = {
            'is_anomaly': False,
            'similarity': 0.0,
            'distance': 0.0,
            'health_score': 100.0,
            'diagnosis': '',
            'model_id': 'S-04'
        }

        if not self.is_baseline_ready:
            results['diagnosis'] = '基准数据不足，正在学习'
            return results

        # 计算相似度
        similarity = self.cosine_similarity(
            current_fingerprint, 
            self.baseline_fingerprint
        )
        results['similarity'] = similarity

        # 计算距离
        distance = self.euclidean_distance(
            current_fingerprint,
            self.baseline_fingerprint
        )
        results['distance'] = distance

        # 健康评分 (相似度转化的百分制)
        results['health_score'] = similarity * 100

        # 异常判定
        if similarity < self.similarity_threshold:
            results['is_anomaly'] = True

            # 分析异常类型
            if similarity < 0.5:
                results['diagnosis'] = '严重异常：谐波指纹显著偏离，可能存在接头松动或氧化'
                results['risk_level'] = 'HIGH'
            elif similarity < 0.7:
                results['diagnosis'] = '中度异常：谐波特征变化明显，建议检查接头'
                results['risk_level'] = 'MEDIUM'
            else:
                results['diagnosis'] = '轻度异常：谐波指纹略有变化，持续关注'
                results['risk_level'] = 'LOW'
        else:
            results['diagnosis'] = '正常：谐波指纹与基准一致'
            results['risk_level'] = 'INFO'

        return results

    def analyze_trend(self,
                     fingerprints_history: List[np.ndarray],
                     time_windows: int = 100) -> Dict:
        """
        趋势分析

        检测谐波指纹的长期漂移

        参数:
            fingerprints_history: 历史指纹列表
            time_windows: 分析窗口大小

        返回:
            趋势分析结果
        """
        if len(fingerprints_history) < time_windows:
            return {'trend': '数据不足'}

        recent = fingerprints_history[-time_windows:]
        old = fingerprints_history[-2*time_windows:-time_windows]

        # 计算指纹漂移
        if len(old) > 0:
            drift = np.mean([
                self.cosine_similarity(r, o)
                for r, o in zip(recent, old)
            ])

            # 漂移检测
            if drift < 0.8:
                trend = '下降趋势 - 接头状态恶化'
            elif drift < 0.95:
                trend = '轻微下降 - 需关注'
            else:
                trend = '稳定'
        else:
            drift = 1.0
            trend = '稳定'

        return {
            'trend': trend,
            'drift_coefficient': drift,
            'estimated_degradation_rate': 1 - drift
        }

    def run(self,
           harmonic_currents: Dict[int, float],
           fundamental_current: float,
           update_baseline: bool = True) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数

        参数:
            harmonic_currents: 各次谐波电流有效值
            fundamental_current: 基波电流有效值
            update_baseline: 是否更新基准

        返回:
            完整诊断结果
        """
        # 计算当前指纹
        fingerprint = self.calculate_harmonic_fingerprint(
            harmonic_currents, 
            fundamental_current
        )

        # 检测异常
        results = self.detect_anomaly(fingerprint)

        # 更新基准（仅在正常状态下）
        if update_baseline and not results['is_anomaly']:
            self.update_baseline(fingerprint)

        # 添加指纹到历史
        if not hasattr(self, 'fingerprint_history'):
            self.fingerprint_history = []
        self.fingerprint_history.append(fingerprint)

        # 趋势分析
        if len(self.fingerprint_history) >= 200:
            results['trend_analysis'] = self.analyze_trend(
                self.fingerprint_history
            )

        results['model_id'] = 'S-04'
        results['timestamp'] = pd.Timestamp.now()

        return results


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    monitor = JointHealthMonitor(
        baseline_window=100,
        similarity_threshold=0.85
    )

    # 模拟正常状态的谐波数据
    normal_harmonics = {
        1: 100.0,   # 基波
        3: 3.0,    # 3次谐波 (3%)
        5: 5.0,    # 5次谐波 (5%)
        7: 2.5,    # 7次谐波 (2.5%)
        11: 1.5,   # 11次谐波 (1.5%)
        13: 1.0    # 13次谐波 (1%)
    }

    # 建立基准
    print("建立基准数据...")
    for i in range(100):
        noise = {k: v * (1 + np.random.normal(0, 0.05)) 
                for k, v in normal_harmonics.items()}
        monitor.run(noise, noise[1], update_baseline=True)

    # 模拟氧化后的谐波变化
    oxidized_harmonics = {
        1: 100.0,
        3: 8.0,    # 3次谐波增加
        5: 12.0,   # 5次谐波增加
        7: 6.0,    # 7次谐波增加
        11: 3.0,
        13: 2.0
    }

    # 检测氧化
    result = monitor.run(oxidized_harmonics, oxidized_harmonics[1])

    print("\n氧化检测结果:")
    print(f"  是否异常: {'是' if result['is_anomaly'] else '否'}")
    print(f"  相似度: {result['similarity']:.4f}")
    print(f"  健康评分: {result['health_score']:.2f}")
    print(f"  诊断: {result['diagnosis']}")
    print(f"  风险等级: {result['risk_level']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

4.4 配置示例

# configs/safety/S-04_joint_health.yaml
model:
  model_id: "S-04"
  model_name: "电气接头松动/氧化预警"
  model_name_en: "joint_loosening_oxidation_warning"
  category: "safety"
  tech_level: "L2a"

data_source:
  type: "kafka"
  topic: "taiyi.electrical.harmonic.v1"
  input_fields:
    - "fundamental_current"    # 基波电流 (A)
    - "I_harmonic_3"           # 3次谐波电流
    - "I_harmonic_5"           # 5次谐波电流
    - "I_harmonic_7"           # 7次谐波电流
    - "I_harmonic_11"          # 11次谐波电流
    - "I_harmonic_13"          # 13次谐波电流
  window:
    type: "sliding"
    size: 100
    step: 1

pipeline:
  - id: "calc_fingerprint"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "|
        F = [
          ${input.I_harmonic_3} / ${input.fundamental_current},
          ${input.I_harmonic_5} / ${input.fundamental_current},
          ${input.I_harmonic_7} / ${input.fundamental_current},
          ${input.I_harmonic_11} / ${input.fundamental_current},
          ${input.I_harmonic_13} / ${input.fundamental_current}
        ]
        normalize(F)"
    output_as: "fingerprint"

  - id: "cosine_similarity"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "cosine_similarity(fingerprint, qianzhi.get_baseline('joint_fingerprint'))"
    output_as: "similarity"

  - id: "evaluate"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "threshold_rule"
    params:
      operator: "<"
      threshold: 0.85
    inputs:
      value: "${cosine_similarity}"
    output_as: "alarm_trigger"

output:
  type: "alert"
  level: "warning"
  topic: "tianyan.alerts.v1"
  fields:
    health_score: "${cosine_similarity} * 100"
    similarity: "${cosine_similarity}"
    fingerprint: "${fingerprint}"

§5 S-05 中性线过载热保护

5.1 功能描述

模型名称: 中性线过载热保护 英文名称: Neutral Conductor Overload Thermal Protection 技术等级: L1（纯计算模型）

计算三相不平衡系统中性线电流，基于热量累积模型评估过载风险，实现反时限热保护功能。

5.2 核心算法公式

中性线电流计算（三相三线制不平衡系统）:
    I_N = √(I_a² + I_b² + I_c² + 2×I_a×I_b×cos(120°-φ_ab) + ...)

简化计算（考虑三相相位差120°）:
    I_N = √(I_a² + I_b² + I_c² - I_a×I_b - I_b×I_c - I_c×I_a)

热量累积模型（反时限特性）:
    dQ/dt = I²(t) × R - Q_loss
    τ × d(I_norm)/dt = I²(t)/I_rated² - I_norm

跳闸时间:
    t_trip = τ / (I²/I_rated² - 1.1²)

5.3 参考代码

"""
S-05 中性线过载热保护
======================
功能: 计算中性线电流，评估过载热风险
输入: 三相电流 Ia, Ib, Ic
输出: 中性线电流, 热状态, 告警/跳闸判定
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional
from collections import deque

class NeutralOverloadProtector:
    """中性线过载热保护模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 neutral_rated_current: float = 100.0,
                 thermal_time_constant: float = 3600.0,
                 alarm_ratio: float = 0.8,
                 trip_ratio: float = 1.0,
                 sampling_period: float = 1.0):
        """
        参数:
            neutral_rated_current: 中性线额定电流 (A)
            thermal_time_constant: 热时间常数 (秒)
            alarm_ratio: 告警阈值比例 (额定电流的百分比)
            trip_ratio: 跳闸阈值比例
            sampling_period: 采样周期 (秒)
        """
        self.I_rated = neutral_rated_current
        self.tau = thermal_time_constant
        self.alarm_threshold = alarm_ratio * neutral_rated_current
        self.trip_threshold = trip_ratio * neutral_rated_current
        self.dt = sampling_period

        # 热状态缓存
        self.thermal_state = 0.0
        self.history = deque(maxlen=86400)  # 保存24小时历史

    def calculate_neutral_current(self,
                                  I_a: float,
                                  I_b: float,
                                  I_c: float,
                                  phi_ab: float = 120.0,
                                  phi_bc: float = 120.0,
                                  phi_ca: float = 120.0) -> float:
        """
        计算中性线电流

        对于三相三线制不平衡系统:
        I_N = √(I_a² + I_b² + I_c² + 2×I_a×I_b×cos(φ_ab) + ...)

        参数:
            I_a, I_b, I_c: 三相电流有效值 (A)
            phi_ab, phi_bc, phi_ca: 各相间相位角 (度)

        返回:
            中性线电流有效值 (A)
        """
        # 转换为弧度
        phi_ab_rad = np.deg2rad(phi_ab)
        phi_bc_rad = np.deg2rad(phi_bc)
        phi_ca_rad = np.deg2rad(phi_ca)

        # 使用简化公式（假设三相相位差120°）
        # I_N = √(I_a² + I_b² + I_c² - I_a×I_b - I_b×I_c - I_c×I_a)
        # 这适用于平衡三相系统中性线电流为0的情况

        # 通用公式
        I_N_sq = (I_a**2 + I_b**2 + I_c**2 + 
                  2 * I_a * I_b * np.cos(phi_ab_rad) +
                  2 * I_b * I_c * np.cos(phi_bc_rad) +
                  2 * I_c * I_a * np.cos(phi_ca_rad))

        return np.sqrt(max(0, I_N_sq))

    def update_thermal_state(self, I_N: float) -> float:
        """
        更新热状态

        使用热量累积模型:
        τ × d(I_norm)/dt = I²(t)/I_rated² - I_norm

        参数:
            I_N: 当前中性线电流

        返回:
            当前热状态 (标幺值，1.0表示额定温度)
        """
        # 归一化电流平方
        I_sq_norm = (I_N / self.I_rated) ** 2

        # 热累积微分方程离散化
        # dI_norm/dt = (I² - I_norm) / τ
        dI_norm = (I_sq_norm - self.thermal_state) * (self.dt / self.tau)

        self.thermal_state += dI_norm

        # 热状态不能低于0（冷却后）
        self.thermal_state = max(0.0, self.thermal_state)

        return self.thermal_state

    def calculate_trip_time(self, I_N: float) -> float:
        """
        计算跳闸时间（反时限特性）

        公式: t_trip = τ / (I²/I_rated² - 1.1²)

        参数:
            I_N: 当前中性线电流

        返回:
            预计跳闸时间 (秒)，inf表示不会跳闸
        """
        I_ratio_sq = (I_N / self.I_rated) ** 2

        # 避免除以零
        denominator = I_ratio_sq - 1.1**2

        if denominator <= 0:
            return float('inf')

        return self.tau / denominator

    def evaluate_protection(self, I_N: float) -> Dict:
        """
        保护评估

        参数:
            I_N: 当前中性线电流

        返回:
            保护评估结果
        """
        # 更新热状态
        thermal = self.update_thermal_state(I_N)

        # 计算跳闸时间
        trip_time = self.calculate_trip_time(I_N)

        # 判断状态
        I_ratio = I_N / self.I_rated

        if I_ratio > 1.1:
            status = 'TRIP'
            level = 'critical'
            action = '立即跳闸'
        elif thermal > 1.0:
            status = 'TRIP_PENDING'
            level = 'high'
            action = f'热过载，将在{trip_time:.0f}秒后跳闸'
        elif I_ratio > self.alarm_threshold / self.I_rated:
            status = 'ALARM'
            level = 'warning'
            action = f'中性线电流超过{int(self.alarm_threshold)}A警戒线'
        elif I_ratio > 0.6:
            status = 'WARNING'
            level = 'info'
            action = '中性线电流偏高，建议关注'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            level = 'info'
            action = '中性线运行正常'

        return {
            'status': status,
            'level': level,
            'action': action,
            'neutral_current': I_N,
            'thermal_state': thermal,
            'thermal_percent': thermal * 100,
            'current_ratio': I_ratio,
            'rated_current': self.I_rated,
            'trip_time_remaining': trip_time if trip_time < float('inf') else None,
            'alarm_threshold': self.alarm_threshold,
            'trip_threshold': self.trip_threshold
        }

    def run(self, I_a: float, I_b: float, I_c: float) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数

        参数:
            I_a, I_b, I_c: 三相电流有效值 (A)

        返回:
            完整保护结果
        """
        # 计算中性线电流
        I_N = self.calculate_neutral_current(I_a, I_b, I_c)

        # 保护评估
        results = self.evaluate_protection(I_N)

        # 记录历史
        self.history.append({
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'I_a': I_a,
            'I_b': I_b,
            'I_c': I_c,
            'I_N': I_N,
            'thermal': results['thermal_state']
        })

        # 添加模型标识
        results['model_id'] = 'S-05'
        results['timestamp'] = pd.Timestamp.now()

        # 添加历史统计
        if len(self.history) >= 100:
            recent_currents = [h['I_N'] for h in list(self.history)[-100:]]
            results['avg_neutral_current'] = np.mean(recent_currents)
            results['max_neutral_current'] = np.max(recent_currents)

        return results

    def get_thermal_curve(self, I_ratios: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        获取热特性曲线（用于绘图）

        参数:
            I_ratios: 电流标幺值数组

        返回:
            稳态热状态数组
        """
        return I_ratios ** 2


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    protector = NeutralOverloadProtector(
        neutral_rated_current=100.0,
        thermal_time_constant=3600.0,
        alarm_ratio=0.8,
        trip_ratio=1.0
    )

    # 模拟三相不平衡场景
    # A相: 80A, B相: 60A, C相: 40A
    I_a = 80.0
    I_b = 60.0
    I_c = 40.0

    result = protector.run(I_a, I_b, I_c)

    print("中性线过载保护评估:")
    print(f"  A相电流: {result.get('I_a', I_a):.1f} A")
    print(f"  B相电流: {result.get('I_b', I_b):.1f} A")
    print(f"  C相电流: {result.get('I_c', I_c):.1f} A")
    print(f"  中性线电流: {result['neutral_current']:.1f} A")
    print(f"  额定电流: {result['rated_current']:.1f} A")
    print(f"  电流比例: {result['current_ratio']:.2%}")
    print(f"  热状态: {result['thermal_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  状态: {result['status']}")
    print(f"  级别: {result['level']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")

    if result.get('avg_neutral_current'):
        print(f"  100点平均中性线电流: {result['avg_neutral_current']:.1f} A")
        print(f"  100点最大中性线电流: {result['max_neutral_current']:.1f} A")

    # 计算不同电流比例下的跳闸时间
    print("\n跳闸时间曲线:")
    for ratio in [1.0, 1.2, 1.5, 2.0]:
        trip_t = protector.calculate_trip_time(ratio * 100)
        if trip_t < float('inf'):
            print(f"  {ratio:.1f}倍额定: {trip_t/60:.1f} 分钟")
        else:
            print(f"  {ratio:.1f}倍额定: 不会跳闸")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

5.4 配置示例

# configs/safety/S-05_neutral_overload.yaml
model:
  model_id: "S-05"
  model_name: "中性线过载热保护"
  model_name_en: "neutral_overload_thermal_protection"
  category: "safety"
  tech_level: "L1"

data_source:
  type: "kafka"
  topic: "taiyi.electrical.raw.v1"
  input_fields:
    - "Ia_rms"    # A相电流有效值 (A)
    - "Ib_rms"    # B相电流有效值 (A)
    - "Ic_rms"    # C相电流有效值 (A)
  window:
    type: "sliding"
    size: 1
    step: 1

config:
  neutral_rated_current: 100.0    # 中性线额定电流 (A)
  thermal_time_constant: 3600.0   # 热时间常数 (秒)
  alarm_ratio: 0.8               # 告警阈值 (额定电流比例)
  trip_ratio: 1.0                # 跳闸阈值

pipeline:
  - id: "calc_neutral_current"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "|
        I_N_sq = Ia^2 + Ib^2 + Ic^2 
               - Ia*Ib - Ib*Ic - Ic*Ia
        sqrt(max(0, I_N_sq))"
    output_as: "I_N"

  - id: "update_thermal"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "|
        thermal = thermal_state + (I_N^2/I_rated^2 - thermal_state) * dt/tau
        max(0, thermal)"
    output_as: "thermal_state"

  - id: "evaluate"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "threshold_rule"
    params:
      thresholds:
        - {value: 110, level: "critical"}
        - {value: 100, level: "high"}
        - {value: 80, level: "warning"}
    inputs:
      value: "${I_N}"
    output_as: "protection_level"

output:
  type: "alert"
  level: "${protection_level}"
  topic: "tianyan.alerts.v1"
  fields:
    neutral_current_A: "${calc_neutral_current.I_N}"
    thermal_state_percent: "${update_thermal.thermal_state} * 100"
    status: "${protection_level}"
    rated_current: "${config.neutral_rated_current}"

§6 S-06 三相不平衡热损评估

6.1 功能描述

模型名称: 三相不平衡热损评估 英文名称: Three-Phase Unbalance Thermal Loss Assessment 技术等级: L1

基于负序电流不平衡度计算电机额外转子发热损耗，评估三相不平衡对设备的热损伤。

6.2 核心算法公式

负序电流不平衡度:
    ε_I2 = I_2 / I_1 × 100%

电机额外热损耗:
    P_loss = 3 × I_2² × R_r × K
    其中: I_2为负序电流, R_r为转子电阻, K为集肤效应系数

等效降容计算:
    Capacity_reduction = ε_I2² / 100 × 降容系数

6.3 参考代码

"""
S-06 三相不平衡热损评估
========================
功能: 评估三相不平衡引起的电机热损耗
输入: 负序电流不平衡度, 电机参数
输出: 热损耗, 等效降容比例
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Optional

class UnbalanceLossEvaluator:
    """三相不平衡热损评估模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rotor_resistance: float = 0.1,
                 skin_effect_factor: float = 1.5,
                 base_degradation_factor: float = 0.72):
        """
        参数:
            rotor_resistance: 转子电阻 (Ω)
            skin_effect_factor: 集肤效应系数
            base_degradation_factor: 基准降容系数 (不平衡度100%时的降容比例)
        """
        self.R_r = rotor_resistance
        self.K = skin_effect_factor
        self.base_factor = base_degradation_factor

    def calculate_negative_sequence_current(self,
                                           I_a: float,
                                           I_b: float,
                                           I_c: float,
                                           phi_a: float = 0.0,
                                           phi_b: float = -120.0,
                                           phi_c: float = 120.0) -> float:
        """
        计算负序电流

        对称分量法:
        I_2 = (1/3) × [I_a + I_b∠240° + I_c∠120°]

        参数:
            I_a, I_b, I_c: 三相电流有效值
            phi_a, phi_b, phi_c: 三相相角

        返回:
            负序电流有效值 (A)
        """
        # 复数形式
        I_a_c = I_a * np.exp(1j * np.deg2rad(phi_a))
        I_b_c = I_b * np.exp(1j * np.deg2rad(phi_b))
        I_c_c = I_c * np.exp(1j * np.deg2rad(phi_c))

        # 对称分量变换
        alpha = np.exp(1j * 2 * np.pi / 3)  # 120°旋转因子

        I_0 = (I_a_c + I_b_c + I_c_c) / 3
        I_1 = (I_a_c + alpha**2 * I_b_c + alpha * I_c_c) / 3
        I_2 = (I_a_c + alpha * I_b_c + alpha**2 * I_c_c) / 3

        return abs(I_2)

    def calculate_unbalance_factor(self,
                                  I_2: float,
                                  I_1: float) -> float:
        """
        计算负序电流不平衡度

        公式: ε_I2 = I_2 / I_1 × 100%
        """
        if I_1 < 1e-6:
            return 0.0
        return (I_2 / I_1) * 100

    def calculate_thermal_loss(self,
                               I_2: float) -> float:
        """
        计算电机额外热损耗

        公式: P_loss = 3 × I_2² × R_r × K

        参数:
            I_2: 负序电流有效值 (A)

        返回:
            额外热损耗 (W)
        """
        return 3 * (I_2 ** 2) * self.R_r * self.K

    def calculate_capacity_reduction(self,
                                    unbalance_factor: float) -> float:
        """
        计算等效降容比例

        经验公式:
        Capacity_reduction = ε_I2² × base_factor / 100

        参数:
            unbalance_factor: 负序不平衡度 (%)

        返回:
            降容比例 (0-1)
        """
        return (unbalance_factor ** 2) * self.base_factor / 10000

    def evaluate(self,
                I_a: float,
                I_b: float,
                I_c: float,
                rated_power: float = 100.0,
                rated_current: float = 100.0) -> Dict:
        """
        综合评估

        参数:
            I_a, I_b, I_c: 三相电流有效值 (A)
            rated_power: 电机额定功率 (kW)
            rated_current: 电机额定电流 (A)

        返回:
            评估结果
        """
        # 计算正序电流（近似为三相电流平均值）
        I_1 = np.mean([I_a, I_b, I_c])

        # 计算负序电流
        I_2 = self.calculate_negative_sequence_current(I_a, I_b, I_c)

        # 计算不平衡度
        unbalance = self.calculate_unbalance_factor(I_2, I_1)

        # 计算热损耗
        thermal_loss_w = self.calculate_thermal_loss(I_2)
        thermal_loss_kw = thermal_loss_w / 1000

        # 计算降容比例
        capacity_reduction = self.calculate_capacity_reduction(unbalance)

        # 年化损耗估算
        annual_loss_kwh = thermal_loss_kw * 8760 * 0.7  # 假设70%负载率

        # 判断等级
        if unbalance > 10:
            level = 'critical'
            action = '严重不平衡，立即检查'
        elif unbalance > 5:
            level = 'warning'
            action = '不平衡超标，建议调整负载'
        elif unbalance > 2:
            level = 'info'
            action = '轻微不平衡，可接受'
        else:
            level = 'info'
            action = '三相平衡，运行良好'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-06',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'unbalance_factor_percent': unbalance,
            'negative_sequence_current_A': I_2,
            'positive_sequence_current_A': I_1,
            'thermal_loss_W': thermal_loss_w,
            'thermal_loss_kW': thermal_loss_kw,
            'capacity_reduction_percent': capacity_reduction * 100,
            'equivalent_power_loss_kW': rated_power * capacity_reduction,
            'annual_estimate_kWh': annual_loss_kwh,
            'annual_loss_cost_yuan': annual_loss_kwh * 0.8,  # 假设电价0.8元/kWh
            'level': level,
            'action': action
        }

    def run(self, I_a: float, I_b: float, I_c: float,
           rated_power: float = 100.0) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        return self.evaluate(I_a, I_b, I_c, rated_power)


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    evaluator = UnbalanceLossEvaluator(
        rotor_resistance=0.1,
        skin_effect_factor=1.5
    )

    # 场景1: 严重不平衡
    # A相: 100A, B相: 70A, C相: 50A
    result = evaluator.run(100, 70, 50, rated_power=100)

    print("三相不平衡热损评估:")
    print(f"  A相电流: 100 A")
    print(f"  B相电流: 70 A")
    print(f"  C相电流: 50 A")
    print(f"  负序不平衡度: {result['unbalance_factor_percent']:.2f}%")
    print(f"  负序电流: {result['negative_sequence_current_A']:.2f} A")
    print(f"  正序电流: {result['positive_sequence_current_A']:.2f} A")
    print(f"  额外热损耗: {result['thermal_loss_W']:.2f} W")
    print(f"  等效降容: {result['capacity_reduction_percent']:.2f}%")
    print(f"  年化损耗估算: {result['annual_estimate_kWh']:.0f} kWh")
    print(f"  年化损失电费: ¥{result['annual_loss_cost_yuan']:.0f}")
    print(f"  风险等级: {result['level']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§7 S-07 瞬时过电压累积冲击

7.1 功能描述

模型名称: 瞬时过电压累积冲击 英文名称: Transient Overvoltage Cumulative Impact 技术等级: L1（纯计算模型）

评估瞬时过电压事件对电气设备的累积损伤效应，考虑峰值电压、持续时间和发生频率。

7.2 核心算法公式

冲击能量累积:
    E_impulse = Σ(V_peak × t_duration × K_factor)

等效老化小时数:
    t_equivalent = E_impulse / V_rated × Life_constant

累计损伤度:
    D = t_equivalent / T_design

7.3 参考代码

"""
S-07 瞬时过电压累积冲击
=======================
功能: 评估瞬时过电压对设备的累积损伤
输入: 过电压峰值, 持续时间, 发生频率
输出: 冲击能量, 等效老化, 损伤度
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List
from collections import deque

class TransientOvervoltageAssessor:
    """瞬时过电压累积冲击评估模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_voltage: float = 220.0,
                 design_lifetime_hours: float = 200000.0,
                 life_constant: float = 1e6,
                 alarm_threshold: float = 0.3,
                 critical_threshold: float = 0.7):
        """
        参数:
            rated_voltage: 设备额定电压 (V)
            design_lifetime_hours: 设计寿命 (小时)
            life_constant: 寿命常数
            alarm_threshold: 告警阈值 (损伤度比例)
            critical_threshold: 严重阈值
        """
        self.V_rated = rated_voltage
        self.T_design = design_lifetime_hours
        self.Life_K = life_constant
        self.alarm_threshold = alarm_threshold
        self.critical_threshold = critical_threshold

        # 事件历史
        self.event_history = deque(maxlen=10000)
        self.daily_summary = deque(maxlen=365)

    def calculate_impulse_energy(self,
                                 V_peak: float,
                                 t_duration: float,
                                 K_factor: float = 1.0) -> float:
        """
        计算单次冲击能量

        参数:
            V_peak: 峰值电压 (V)
            t_duration: 持续时间 (秒)
            K_factor: 冲击系数
        """
        return V_peak * t_duration * K_factor

    def calculate_equivalent_hours(self, energy: float) -> float:
        """
        计算等效老化小时数

        公式: t_equivalent = energy / V_rated * Life_constant
        """
        return (energy / self.V_rated) * self.Life_K

    def calculate_damage_ratio(self, t_equivalent: float) -> float:
        """
        计算损伤度

        公式: D = t_equivalent / T_design
        """
        return t_equivalent / self.T_design

    def classify_overvoltage_event(self, V_peak: float) -> str:
        """
        分类过电压事件等级
        """
        ratio = V_peak / self.V_rated

        if ratio > 2.0:
            return 'CATASTROPHIC'  # 灾难性
        elif ratio > 1.5:
            return 'CRITICAL'      # 严重
        elif ratio > 1.3:
            return 'HIGH'          # 高
        elif ratio > 1.1:
            return 'MEDIUM'        # 中等
        else:
            return 'LOW'           # 轻微

    def record_event(self, V_peak: float, t_duration: float,
                    timestamp: pd.Timestamp = None) -> Dict:
        """
        记录并处理一次过电压事件
        """
        if timestamp is None:
            timestamp = pd.Timestamp.now()

        # 计算冲击能量
        energy = self.calculate_impulse_energy(V_peak, t_duration)

        # 计算等效老化
        eq_hours = self.calculate_equivalent_hours(energy)

        # 分类
        severity = self.classify_overvoltage_event(V_peak)

        event = {
            'timestamp': timestamp,
            'V_peak': V_peak,
            't_duration': t_duration,
            'energy': energy,
            'eq_hours': eq_hours,
            'severity': severity,
            'V_ratio': V_peak / self.V_rated
        }

        self.event_history.append(event)

        # 更新日统计
        self._update_daily_summary(timestamp, eq_hours, severity)

        return event

    def _update_daily_summary(self, timestamp: pd.Timestamp,
                             eq_hours: float, severity: str):
        """更新日统计"""
        today = timestamp.date()

        if not self.daily_summary or self.daily_summary[-1]['date'] != today:
            self.daily_summary.append({
                'date': today,
                'event_count': 1,
                'total_eq_hours': eq_hours,
                'severities': [severity]
            })
        else:
            last = self.daily_summary[-1]
            last['event_count'] += 1
            last['total_eq_hours'] += eq_hours
            last['severities'].append(severity)

    def calculate_cumulative_damage(self) -> float:
        """
        计算累计损伤度
        """
        total_eq_hours = sum(e['eq_hours'] for e in self.event_history)
        return self.calculate_damage_ratio(total_eq_hours)

    def run(self, V_peak: float, t_duration: float = 0.001) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 记录事件
        event = self.record_event(V_peak, t_duration)

        # 计算累计损伤
        cumulative_damage = self.calculate_cumulative_damage()

        # 获取最近统计
        recent_events = list(self.event_history)[-100:]
        today_events = self.daily_summary[-1] if self.daily_summary else None

        # 评估状态
        if cumulative_damage > self.critical_threshold:
            status = 'CRITICAL'
            action = '设备老化严重，建议检修或更换'
        elif cumulative_damage > self.alarm_threshold:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '累积损伤上升，关注设备状态'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '瞬时过电压影响在可控范围'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-07',
            'timestamp': event['timestamp'],
            'current_event': event,
            'cumulative_damage_percent': cumulative_damage * 100,
            'total_events': len(self.event_history),
            'recent_100_events_energy': sum(e['energy'] for e in recent_events),
            'today_event_count': today_events['event_count'] if today_events else 0,
            'today_eq_hours': today_events['total_eq_hours'] if today_events else 0,
            'status': status,
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    assessor = TransientOvervoltageAssessor(
        rated_voltage=220.0,
        design_lifetime_hours=200000.0
    )

    # 模拟多次过电压事件
    events = [
        (380, 0.002),   # 380V冲击，持续2ms
        (450, 0.001),   # 450V冲击，持续1ms
        (350, 0.005),   # 350V冲击，持续5ms
        (520, 0.0005),  # 520V冲击，持续0.5ms
    ]

    for V, t in events:
        result = assessor.run(V, t)
        print(f"过电压事件: {V}V, {t*1000:.1f}ms")
        print(f"  严重等级: {result['current_event']['severity']}")
        print(f"  冲击能量: {result['current_event']['energy']:.4f}")
        print(f"  等效老化: {result['current_event']['eq_hours']:.2f} 小时")
        print(f"  累计损伤: {result['cumulative_damage_percent']:.4f}%")
        print()

    print(f"总事件数: {result['total_events']}")
    print(f"状态: {result['status']}")
    print(f"建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

7.4 配置示例

# configs/safety/S-07_transient_overvoltage.yaml
model:
  model_id: "S-07"
  model_name: "瞬时过电压累积冲击"
  category: "safety"
  tech_level: "L1"

data_source:
  type: "kafka"
  topic: "taiyi.electrical.raw.v1"
  input_fields:
    - "V_peak"        # 峰值电压 (V)
    - "t_duration"   # 持续时间 (s)
  window:
    type: "event"
    trigger: "V_peak > V_rated * 1.1"

config:
  rated_voltage: 220.0
  design_lifetime_hours: 200000.0
  alarm_threshold: 0.3
  critical_threshold: 0.7

pipeline:
  - id: "classify_event"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "|
        ratio = V_peak / rated_voltage
        case(
          ratio > 2.0, 'CATASTROPHIC',
          ratio > 1.5, 'CRITICAL',
          ratio > 1.3, 'HIGH',
          ratio > 1.1, 'MEDIUM',
          'LOW'
        )"
    output_as: "severity"

  - id: "calc_energy"
    type: "builtin"
    operator: "expression_eval"
    params:
      expression: "V_peak * t_duration * 1.0"
    output_as: "impulse_energy"

  - id: "update_damage"
    type: "stateful"
    operator: "cumulative_sum"
    params:
      decay: 0.9999  # 轻微衰减
    inputs:
      value: "${calc_energy}"
    output_as: "cumulative_energy"

output:
  type: "alert"
  topic: "tianyan.alerts.v1"
  fields:
    severity: "${classify_event.severity}"
    damage_percent: "${update_damage.cumulative_energy} / design_lifetime * 100"

§8 S-08 电机轴承/转子断条初兆

8.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电机轴承/转子断条初兆 英文名称: Motor Bearing/Rotor Bar Fault Early Detection 技术等级: L2b（需离线训练模型）

通过振动频谱分析和电流特征识别，提前发现电机轴承磨损和转子断条故障。

8.2 核心算法公式

轴承故障特征频率:
    BPFO = (n/2) × f_r × (1 - d/D × cos(β))
    BPFI = (n/2) × f_r × (1 + d/D × cos(β))
    FTF  = (1/2) × f_r × (1 - d/D × cos(β))

转子断条特征 (电流频谱):
    f_broken = (1 ± 2k) × f_s, k = 1,2,3,...
    边带幅值比 = (I_sideband / I_fundamental) × 100%

振动RMS趋势:
    RMS_trend = Σ(a_i²)^0.5 / N

8.3 参考代码

"""
S-08 电机轴承/转子断条初兆检测
=============================
功能: 通过振动和电流特征检测电机早期故障
输入: 振动信号, 电流信号, 电机参数
输出: 故障概率, 故障类型, 严重程度
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional
from scipy import signal
from scipy.fft import fft, fftfreq

class MotorFaultDetector:
    """电机故障初兆检测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 motor_poles: int = 4,
                 rated_speed: float = 1500.0,
                 shaft_diameter: float = 0.05,
                 ball_diameter: float = 0.02,
                 contact_angle: float = 15.0,
                 num_balls: int = 8):
        """
        参数:
            motor_poles: 极数
            rated_speed: 额定转速 (RPM)
            shaft_diameter: 轴承节圆直径 (m)
            ball_diameter: 滚动体直径 (m)
            contact_angle: 接触角 (度)
            num_balls: 滚动体数量
        """
        self.p = motor_poles
        self.f_r = rated_speed / 60  # 转频 Hz
        self.d = shaft_diameter
        self.D = ball_diameter
        self.beta = np.deg2rad(contact_angle)
        self.n = num_balls

        # 基准数据
        self.baseline_rated_power = None
        self.baseline_current_spectrum = None

    def calculate_bearing_frequencies(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        计算轴承故障特征频率

        BPFO: 外圈故障频率
        BPFI: 内圈故障频率
        FTF: 保持架故障频率
        BSF: 滚动体故障频率
        """
        # 转频
        fr = self.f_r

        # 特征频率计算
        BPFO = (self.n / 2) * fr * (1 - self.d / self.D * np.cos(self.beta))
        BPFI = (self.n / 2) * fr * (1 + self.d / self.D * np.cos(self.beta))
        FTF  = (1 / 2) * fr * (1 - self.d / self.D * np.cos(self.beta))
        BSF  = (self.D / (2 * self.d)) * fr * (1 - (self.d / self.D * np.cos(self.beta))**2)

        return {
            'BPFO': BPFO,  # 外圈
            'BPFI': BPFI,  # 内圈
            'FTF': FTF,    # 保持架
            'BSF': BSF     # 滚动体
        }

    def calculate_rotor_bar_frequencies(self, f_s: float = 50.0) -> list:
        """
        计算转子断条特征频率

        f_broken = (1 ± 2k) × f_s
        """
        frequencies = []
        for k in range(1, 4):
            frequencies.append(f_s * (1 + 2 * k))
            frequencies.append(f_s * (1 - 2 * k))
        return frequencies

    def extract_spectrum(self, data: np.ndarray, fs: float) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        """
        提取频谱

        返回: (频率, 幅值)
        """
        N = len(data)
        yf = np.abs(fft(data))[:N//2]
        xf = fftfreq(N, 1/fs)[:N//2]
        return xf, yf

    def detect_bearing_fault(self, vibration: np.ndarray, fs: float,
                           threshold: float = 0.15) -> Dict:
        """
        检测轴承故障

        参数:
            vibration: 振动信号
            fs: 采样频率
            threshold: 检测阈值
        """
        # 提取频谱
        freq, amplitude = self.extract_spectrum(vibration, fs)

        # 获取特征频率
        fault_freqs = self.calculate_bearing_frequencies()

        # 检测各频率成分的能量
        detection_results = {}
        for fault_type, target_freq in fault_freqs.items():
            # 在目标频率附近搜索峰值
            bandwidth = 0.1 * target_freq
            mask = (freq > target_freq - bandwidth) & (freq < target_freq + bandwidth)

            if np.any(mask):
                peak_value = np.max(amplitude[mask])
                avg_value = np.mean(amplitude[mask])
                snr = 10 * np.log10(peak_value / (avg_value + 1e-10))

                detection_results[fault_type] = {
                    'frequency': target_freq,
                    'peak_amplitude': peak_value,
                    'snr_db': snr,
                    'detected': snr > threshold
                }

        # 综合判断
        detected_faults = [k for k, v in detection_results.items() if v['detected']]

        if detected_faults:
            severity = max([detection_results[f]['snr_db'] for f in detected_faults])
            if severity > 20:
                level = 'critical'
            elif severity > 12:
                level = 'warning'
            else:
                level = 'info'
        else:
            level = 'normal'
            severity = 0

        return {
            'fault_type': 'bearing',
            'detected': len(detected_faults) > 0,
            'specific_faults': detected_faults,
            'details': detection_results,
            'severity_db': severity,
            'level': level
        }

    def detect_rotor_bar_fault(self, current: np.ndarray, fs: float,
                              f_s: float = 50.0,
                              threshold: float = 0.05) -> Dict:
        """
        检测转子断条故障

        参数:
            current: 电流信号
            fs: 采样频率
            f_s: 电网频率
            threshold: 检测阈值
        """
        # 提取频谱
        freq, amplitude = self.extract_spectrum(current, fs)

        # 基波频率的边带
        bar_freqs = self.calculate_rotor_bar_frequencies(f_s)

        # 检测边带能量
        detection_results = []
        total_sideband_power = 0
        fundamental_power = amplitude[np.argmin(np.abs(freq - f_s))] if np.any(np.abs(freq - f_s) < 1) else 1

        for target_freq in bar_freqs:
            bandwidth = 1.0  # 1Hz带宽
            mask = (freq > target_freq - bandwidth) & (freq < target_freq + bandwidth)

            if np.any(mask):
                peak_value = np.max(amplitude[mask])
                sideband_ratio = peak_value / (fundamental_power + 1e-10)
                detection_results.append({
                    'frequency': target_freq,
                    'amplitude': peak_value,
                    'ratio': sideband_ratio,
                    'detected': sideband_ratio > threshold
                })
                if sideband_ratio > threshold:
                    total_sideband_power += peak_value

        # 综合边带比
        sideband_ratio_total = total_sideband_power / (fundamental_power + 1e-10)

        return {
            'fault_type': 'rotor_bar',
            'detected': sideband_ratio_total > threshold,
            'sideband_ratio_percent': sideband_ratio_total * 100,
            'details': detection_results,
            'severity': sideband_ratio_total * 100,
            'level': 'critical' if sideband_ratio_total > 0.15 else 'warning' if sideband_ratio_total > 0.05 else 'normal'
        }

    def run(self, vibration: np.ndarray, current: np.ndarray,
           fs: float = 5120.0) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 检测轴承故障
        bearing_result = self.detect_bearing_fault(vibration, fs)

        # 检测转子断条故障
        rotor_result = self.detect_rotor_bar_fault(current, fs)

        # 综合评估
        faults = []
        if bearing_result['detected']:
            faults.append(('bearing', bearing_result['level']))
        if rotor_result['detected']:
            faults.append(('rotor_bar', rotor_result['level']))

        if not faults:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '电机运行正常，未检测到明显故障特征'
        else:
            max_level = max([f[1] for f in faults], default='normal')
            if max_level == 'critical':
                status = 'CRITICAL'
                action = f'检测到严重故障: {[f[0] for f in faults if f[1] == \"critical\"]}'
            elif max_level == 'warning':
                status = 'WARNING'
                action = f'检测到早期故障征兆: {[f[0] for f in faults if f[1] == \"warning\"]}'
            else:
                status = 'INFO'
                action = '检测到轻微异常，建议持续关注'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-08',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'bearing_detection': bearing_result,
            'rotor_bar_detection': rotor_result,
            'faults_detected': len(faults) > 0,
            'fault_summary': faults,
            'status': status,
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    detector = MotorFaultDetector(
        motor_poles=4,
        rated_speed=1500.0
    )

    # 模拟轴承外圈故障特征频率
    fault_freqs = detector.calculate_bearing_frequencies()
    print("轴承故障特征频率:")
    for fault_type, freq in fault_freqs.items():
        print(f"  {fault_type}: {freq:.2f} Hz")

    # 模拟转子断条特征频率
    bar_freqs = detector.calculate_rotor_bar_frequencies(50.0)
    print(f"\n转子断条特征频率: {[f'{f:.1f}' for f in bar_freqs]} Hz")

    # 模拟检测结果
    print("\n模拟检测结果:")
    print("  检测到: 轴承外圈 (BPFO) 故障特征")
    print("  信噪比: 15.3 dB")
    print("  等级: warning")
    print("  建议: 计划检修，更换轴承")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§9 S-09 变压器直流偏磁监测

9.1 功能描述

模型名称: 变压器直流偏磁监测 英文名称: Transformer DC Bias Monitoring 技术等级: L2a（需在线校准）

监测变压器直流偏磁现象，评估对变压器寿命的影响。

9.2 核心算法公式

直流分量检测:
    I_DC = (1/N) × Σ(i_k)

偏磁程度评估:
    Bias_index = |I_DC| / I_rated × 100%

额外损耗计算:
    P_DC_loss = I_DC² × R_eq

励磁电流特征:
    I_ex = √(I_peak² - I_fund²)

9.3 参考代码

"""
S-09 变压器直流偏磁监测
=======================
功能: 监测变压器直流偏磁，评估对绝缘老化的影响
输入: 励磁电流波形, 变压器参数
输出: 偏磁程度, 额外损耗, 风险评估
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Optional
from scipy.fft import fft, fftfreq

class DCBiasMonitor:
    """变压器直流偏磁监测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_power: float = 1000.0,
                 rated_voltage: float = 10.0,
                 rated_current: float = 57.7,
                 equivalent_resistance: float = 0.5,
                 alarm_threshold: float = 5.0,
                 critical_threshold: float = 10.0):
        """
        参数:
            rated_power: 额定容量 (kVA)
            rated_voltage: 额定电压 (kV)
            rated_current: 额定电流 (A)
            equivalent_resistance: 等效电阻 (Ω)
            alarm_threshold: 告警阈值 (偏磁电流百分比)
            critical_threshold: 严重阈值
        """
        self.S_rated = rated_power * 1000  # VA
        self.U_rated = rated_voltage * 1000  # V
        self.I_rated = rated_current
        self.R_eq = equivalent_resistance
        self.alarm_threshold = alarm_threshold
        self.critical_threshold = critical_threshold

        # 统计缓存
        self.dc_history = []
        self.loss_history = []

    def extract_dc_component(self, current: np.ndarray, fs: float) -> float:
        """
        提取直流分量

        参数:
            current: 电流波形
            fs: 采样频率

        返回:
            直流分量 (A)
        """
        return np.mean(current)

    def extract_peak_current(self, current: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """
        提取峰值电流

        返回: (正峰值, 负峰值)
        """
        return np.max(current), np.min(current)

    def calculate_excitation_current(self, current: np.ndarray, fs: float) -> float:
        """
        计算励磁电流特征

        I_ex = √(I_peak² - I_fund²)
        """
        N = len(current)
        yf = np.abs(fft(current))[:N//2]

        # 基波频率附近
        f_base = 50.0
        df = fs / N
        idx_base = int(f_base / df)
        bandwidth = 5

        mask = (np.arange(len(yf)) > idx_base - bandwidth) & \
               (np.arange(len(yf)) < idx_base + bandwidth)

        I_fund = np.max(yf[mask]) * 2 / N
        I_peak = np.max(np.abs(current))

        I_ex = np.sqrt(max(0, I_peak**2 - I_fund**2))
        return I_ex

    def calculate_bias_index(self, I_DC: float) -> float:
        """
        计算偏磁指数

        Bias_index = |I_DC| / I_rated × 100%
        """
        return abs(I_DC) / self.I_rated * 100

    def calculate_dc_loss(self, I_DC: float) -> float:
        """
        计算直流导致的额外损耗

        P_DC_loss = I_DC² × R_eq
        """
        return (I_DC ** 2) * self.R_eq

    def evaluate_asymmetry(self, current: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """
        评估波形不对称性
        """
        I_pos_peak = np.max(current)
        I_neg_peak = abs(np.min(current))

        asymmetry = (I_pos_peak - I_neg_peak) / (I_pos_peak + I_neg_peak + 1e-10) * 100

        return {
            'positive_peak': I_pos_peak,
            'negative_peak': I_neg_peak,
            'asymmetry_percent': asymmetry,
            'asymmetric': abs(asymmetry) > 5
        }

    def run(self, current: np.ndarray, fs: float = 5120.0) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 提取直流分量
        I_DC = self.extract_dc_component(current, fs)

        # 计算偏磁指数
        bias_index = self.calculate_bias_index(I_DC)

        # 计算额外损耗
        dc_loss = self.calculate_dc_loss(I_DC)

        # 评估不对称性
        asymmetry = self.evaluate_asymmetry(current)

        # 历史统计
        self.dc_history.append(I_DC)
        self.loss_history.append(dc_loss)

        # 状态判断
        if bias_index > self.critical_threshold or abs(asymmetry['asymmetry_percent']) > 20:
            status = 'CRITICAL'
            action = '严重直流偏磁，建议立即检查直流源'
        elif bias_index > self.alarm_threshold or abs(asymmetry['asymmetry_percent']) > 10:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '检测到直流偏磁，建议排查原因'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '直流分量在允许范围内'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-09',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'dc_component_A': I_DC,
            'bias_index_percent': bias_index,
            'dc_loss_W': dc_loss,
            'asymmetry': asymmetry,
            'status': status,
            'action': action,
            'dc_history_avg': np.mean(self.dc_history[-100:]) if len(self.dc_history) > 0 else 0,
            'dc_history_max': np.max(self.dc_history[-100:]) if len(self.dc_history) > 0 else 0
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    monitor = DCBiasMonitor(
        rated_power=1000.0,
        rated_voltage=10.0,
        rated_current=57.7
    )

    # 模拟: 带有直流偏置的励磁电流
    fs = 5120.0
    t = np.arange(0, 0.1, 1/fs)
    f = 50.0

    # 正常电流 + 直流偏置
    I_DC_offset = 2.5  # 2.5A直流偏置
    current = 80 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t) + I_DC_offset + np.random.randn(len(t)) * 0.5

    result = monitor.run(current, fs)

    print("变压器直流偏磁监测:")
    print(f"  直流分量: {result['dc_component_A']:.3f} A")
    print(f"  偏磁指数: {result['bias_index_percent']:.2f}%")
    print(f"  额外损耗: {result['dc_loss_W']:.2f} W")
    print(f"  正峰值: {result['asymmetry']['positive_peak']:.2f} A")
