    print(f"  负峰值: {result['asymmetry']['negative_peak']:.2f} A")
    print(f"  不对称度: {result['asymmetry']['asymmetry_percent']:.2f}%")
    print(f"  状态: {result['status']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§10 S-10 电缆绝缘热老化评估

10.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电缆绝缘热老化评估 英文名称: Cable Insulation Thermal Aging Assessment 技术等级: L2b（需离线训练模型）

基于绝缘电阻、介质损耗角正切和局部放电特征，评估电缆绝缘老化程度。

10.2 核心算法公式

阿伦尼乌斯老化模型:
    k = A × exp(-Ea / (R × T))

老化指数:
    Aging_index = (1 - R_insulation / R_initial) × 100%

介质损耗评估:
    tan(δ)_ratio = tan(δ)_current / tan(δ)_initial

综合健康度:
    Health = w1×R_score + w2×tanδ_score + w3×PD_score

10.3 参考代码

"""
S-10 电缆绝缘热老化评估
======================
功能: 评估电缆绝缘老化程度
输入: 绝缘电阻, 介质损耗, 局部放电, 温度
输出: 老化指数, 健康度, 剩余寿命
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Optional, Tuple
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

class CableAgingAssessor:
    """电缆绝缘热老化评估模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 cable_length: float = 1000.0,
                 rated_voltage: float = 10.0,
                 initial_resistance: float = 1000.0,
                 initial_tand: float = 0.001,
                 activation_energy: float = 80000.0,
                 gas_constant: float = 8.314):
        """
        参数:
            cable_length: 电缆长度 (m)
            rated_voltage: 额定电压 (kV)
            initial_resistance: 初始绝缘电阻 (MΩ)
            initial_tand: 初始介质损耗角正切
            activation_energy: 活化能 (J/mol)
            gas_constant: 气体常数
        """
        self.L = cable_length
        self.U_rated = rated_voltage * 1000  # V
        self.R_init = initial_resistance
        self.tanδ_init = initial_tand
        self.Ea = activation_energy
        self.R_g = gas_constant

        # 权重
        self.w_R = 0.4
        self.w_tand = 0.3
        self.w_PD = 0.3

    def calculate_arrhenius_rate(self, T: float) -> float:
        """
        计算阿伦尼乌斯老化速率

        k = A × exp(-Ea / (R × T))
        """
        A = 1e9  # 频率因子
        T_kelvin = T + 273.15
        k = A * np.exp(-self.Ea / (self.R_g * T_kelvin))
        return k

    def calculate_aging_index(self, R_current: float) -> float:
        """
        计算老化指数

        Aging_index = (1 - R_current / R_init) × 100%
        """
        return (1 - R_current / self.R_init) * 100

    def calculate_tand_ratio(self, tanδ_current: float) -> float:
        """
        计算介质损耗比
        """
        return tanδ_current / self.tanδ_init

    def score_resistance(self, R: float) -> float:
        """
        绝缘电阻评分 (0-100)
        """
        ratio = R / self.R_init

        if ratio >= 1.0:
            return 100
        elif ratio >= 0.7:
            return 70 + 30 * (ratio - 0.7) / 0.3
        elif ratio >= 0.4:
            return 40 + 30 * (ratio - 0.4) / 0.3
        elif ratio >= 0.2:
            return 20 + 20 * (ratio - 0.2) / 0.2
        else:
            return max(0, 20 * ratio / 0.2)

    def score_tand(self, tanδ: float) -> float:
        """
        介质损耗评分 (0-100)
        """
        ratio = tanδ / self.tanδ_init

        if ratio <= 1.0:
            return 100
        elif ratio <= 2.0:
            return 100 - 50 * (ratio - 1.0)
        elif ratio <= 3.0:
            return 50 - 30 * (ratio - 2.0)
        elif ratio <= 5.0:
            return 20 - 10 * (ratio - 3.0)
        else:
            return max(0, 10 - 10 * (ratio - 5.0))

    def score_pd(self, pd_charge: float, pd_freq: float) -> float:
        """
        局部放电评分 (0-100)
        """
        # 归一化
        Q_normalized = pd_charge / 1000  # 假设1000pC为最大值
        F_normalized = pd_freq / 100     # 假设100次/秒为最大值

        score = 100 - 50 * Q_normalized - 50 * F_normalized
        return max(0, min(100, score))

    def estimate_remaining_life(self, aging_index: float,
                              operating_hours: float) -> Tuple[float, datetime]:
        """
        估算剩余寿命

        参数:
            aging_index: 当前老化指数 (%)
            operating_hours: 累计运行时间 (小时)

        返回:
            (剩余寿命年数, 预计更换日期)
        """
        # 假设100%老化指数对应设计寿命
        design_life_hours = 200000  # 20万小时 ≈ 22.8年

        # 剩余寿命比例
        remaining_ratio = max(0, 100 - aging_index) / 100

        remaining_hours = design_life_hours * remaining_ratio
        remaining_years = remaining_hours / 8760

        # 预计更换日期
        replacement_date = datetime.now() + timedelta(days=remaining_years * 365)

        return remaining_years, replacement_date

    def run(self, R_insulation: float, tanδ: float,
           pd_charge: float = 0.0, pd_freq: float = 0.0,
           temperature: float = 40.0, operating_hours: float = 0.0) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 计算各项指标
        aging_index = self.calculate_aging_index(R_insulation)
        tand_ratio = self.calculate_tand_ratio(tanδ)
        arrhenius_rate = self.calculate_arrhenius_rate(temperature)

        # 评分
        R_score = self.score_resistance(R_insulation)
        tanδ_score = self.score_tand(tanδ)
        pd_score = self.score_pd(pd_charge, pd_freq)

        # 综合健康度
        health = (self.w_R * R_score + 
                 self.w_tand * tanδ_score + 
                 self.w_PD * pd_score)

        # 剩余寿命估算
        remaining_years, replacement_date = self.estimate_remaining_life(
            aging_index, operating_hours)

        # 状态判断
        if health < 30:
            status = 'CRITICAL'
            action = '绝缘老化严重，立即更换'
        elif health < 50:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '绝缘老化明显，计划更换'
        elif health < 70:
            status = 'ATTENTION'
            action = '绝缘有老化趋势，加强监测'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '绝缘状态良好'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-10',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'aging_index_percent': aging_index,
            'tand_ratio': tand_ratio,
            'arrhenius_rate': arrhenius_rate,
            'health_score': health,
            'scores': {
                'resistance': R_score,
                'tand': tanδ_score,
                'pd': pd_score
            },
            'remaining_life_years': remaining_years,
            'replacement_date': replacement_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'),
            'status': status,
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    assessor = CableAgingAssessor(
        cable_length=1000.0,
        rated_voltage=10.0,
        initial_resistance=1000.0
    )

    # 模拟电缆检测数据
    result = assessor.run(
        R_insulation=350.0,    # 350MΩ
        tanδ=0.003,             # 介质损耗
        pd_charge=200.0,        # 200pC
        pd_freq=20.0,           # 20次/秒
        temperature=60.0,      # 工作温度60°C
        operating_hours=87600   # 运行10年
    )

    print("电缆绝缘热老化评估:")
    print(f"  绝缘电阻: 350 MΩ")
    print(f"  介质损耗: 0.003")
    print(f"  局部放电: 200pC, 20次/秒")
    print(f"  工作温度: 60°C")
    print(f"  老化指数: {result['aging_index_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  综合健康度: {result['health_score']:.1f}分")
    print(f"  电阻评分: {result['scores']['resistance']:.1f}")
    print(f"  介质损耗评分: {result['scores']['tand']:.1f}")
    print(f"  局部放电评分: {result['scores']['pd']:.1f}")
    print(f"  剩余寿命: {result['remaining_life_years']:.1f}年")
    print(f"  建议更换: {result['replacement_date']}")
    print(f"  状态: {result['status']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§11 S-11 逆功率/孤岛检测

11.1 功能描述

模型名称: 逆功率/孤岛检测 英文名称: Reverse Power / Anti-Islanding Detection 技术等级: L1（纯计算模型）

检测分布式电源向电网逆功率输送和孤岛运行状态。

11.2 核心算法公式

逆功率检测:
    P_reverse = -P_flow  (功率从用户侧流向电网)
    触发条件: P_reverse > threshold × P_rated

孤岛检测 - 频率偏移法:
    Δf = |f_measured - f_nominal|
    触发条件: Δf > f_扰动

被动检测法:
    过/欠频: f < 49.5Hz 或 f > 50.5Hz
    过/欠压: U < 0.88Un 或 U > 1.10Un

11.3 参考代码

"""
S-11 逆功率/孤岛检测
====================
功能: 检测逆功率和孤岛运行状态
输入: 有功功率, 无功功率, 电压, 频率
输出: 逆功率状态, 孤岛状态, 告警
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Tuple, Optional
from enum import Enum

class AntiIslandDetector:
    """逆功率/孤岛检测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_power: float = 100.0,
                 frequency_nominal: float = 50.0,
                 voltage_nominal: float = 220.0,
                 reverse_power_threshold: float = 0.05,
                 frequency_threshold: float = 0.5,
                 voltage_under_threshold: float = 0.88,
                 voltage_over_threshold: float = 1.10,
                 rocov_threshold: float = 0.10,
                 rocof_threshold: float = 0.5):
        """
        参数:
            rated_power: 额定功率 (kW)
            frequency_nominal: 额定频率 (Hz)
            voltage_nominal: 额定电压 (V)
            reverse_power_threshold: 逆功率阈值 (额定功率百分比)
            frequency_threshold: 频率偏移阈值 (Hz)
            voltage_under_threshold: 低电压阈值 (Un百分比)
            voltage_over_threshold: 高电压阈值
            rocov_threshold: 电压变化率阈值
            rocof_threshold: 频率变化率阈值 (Hz/s)
        """
        self.P_rated = rated_power
        self.f_nom = frequency_nominal
        self.U_nom = voltage_nominal

        self.reverse_threshold = reverse_power_threshold * rated_power
        self.df_threshold = frequency_threshold
        self.U_under = voltage_under_threshold
        self.U_over = voltage_over_threshold
        self.rocof_th = rocof_threshold

        # 历史缓存
        self.p_history = []
        self.f_history = []
        self.u_history = []

    def detect_reverse_power(self, P: float) -> Dict:
        """
        检测逆功率

        参数:
            P: 当前有功功率 (正值表示从电网取电)

        返回:
            逆功率检测结果
        """
        is_reverse = P < -self.reverse_threshold
        reverse_power = abs(P) if is_reverse else 0
        reverse_percent = reverse_power / self.P_rated * 100

        return {
            'is_reverse': is_reverse,
            'reverse_power_kW': reverse_power,
            'reverse_percent': reverse_percent,
            'grid_supplying': P > self.reverse_threshold,
            'self_consuming': abs(P) <= self.reverse_threshold
        }

    def detect_frequency_deviation(self, f: float, df: float = 0.0) -> Dict:
        """
        检测频率偏移

        参数:
            f: 当前频率 (Hz)
            df: 频率变化率 (Hz/s)
        """
        df_dev = f - self.f_nom
        is_over_freq = f > self.f_nom + self.df_threshold
        is_under_freq = f < self.f_nom - self.df_threshold
        is_rocof_high = abs(df) > self.rocof_th

        return {
            'frequency': f,
            'deviation_Hz': df_dev,
            'rocof_Hz_s': df,
            'is_over_freq': is_over_freq,
            'is_under_freq': is_under_freq,
            'is_rocof_high': is_rocof_high,
            'abnormal': is_over_freq or is_under_freq or is_rocof_high
        }

    def detect_voltage_deviation(self, U: float) -> Dict:
        """
        检测电压偏差

        参数:
            U: 当前电压 (V)
        """
        U_ratio = U / self.U_nom
        is_over_voltage = U_ratio > self.U_over
        is_under_voltage = U_ratio < self.U_under

        return {
            'voltage': U,
            'voltage_ratio': U_ratio,
            'is_over_voltage': is_over_voltage,
            'is_under_voltage': is_under_voltage,
            'abnormal': is_over_voltage or is_under_voltage
        }

    def detect_islanding_passive(self, f: float, U: float, df: float = 0.0) -> Dict:
        """
        被动式孤岛检测

        基于过/欠频、过/欠压检测
        """
        freq_result = self.detect_frequency_deviation(f, df)
        volt_result = self.detect_voltage_deviation(U)

        # 孤岛判定: 多重条件触发
        islanding_indicators = [
            freq_result['abnormal'],
            volt_result['abnormal'],
            freq_result['is_rocof_high']
        ]

        num_indicators = sum(islanding_indicators)

        # 综合判定
        if num_indicators >= 2:
            status = 'ISLANDING_DETECTED'
            confidence = 0.9
        elif num_indicators == 1:
            status = 'POTENTIAL_ISLANDING'
            confidence = 0.5
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            confidence = 0.0

        return {
            'status': status,
            'confidence': confidence,
            'indicators': {
                'frequency_abnormal': freq_result['abnormal'],
                'voltage_abnormal': volt_result['abnormal'],
                'rocof_high': freq_result['is_rocof_high']
            },
            'frequency_info': freq_result,
            'voltage_info': volt_result
        }

    def run(self, P: float, Q: float, U: float, f: float,
           df: float = 0.0) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 逆功率检测
        reverse_result = self.detect_reverse_power(P)

        # 孤岛检测
        islanding_result = self.detect_islanding_passive(f, U, df)

        # 综合判定
        if islanding_result['confidence'] > 0.7:
            status = 'CRITICAL'
            action = '检测到孤岛运行，立即断开'
        elif reverse_result['is_reverse']:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '检测到逆功率，确认是否正常输出'
        elif islanding_result['confidence'] > 0.3:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '疑似孤岛征兆，持续监测'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '并网运行正常'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-11',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'reverse_power': reverse_result,
            'islanding': islanding_result,
            'status': status,
            'action': action,
            'current_power_kW': P,
            'reactive_power_kvar': Q,
            'current_voltage_V': U,
            'current_frequency_Hz': f
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    detector = AntiIslandDetector(rated_power=100.0)

    # 场景1: 正常并网 (从电网取电)
    print("场景1: 正常并网")
    result = detector.run(P=50.0, Q=10.0, U=220.0, f=50.0)
    print(f"  功率: {result['current_power_kW']:.1f} kW")
    print(f"  逆功率: {result['reverse_power']['is_reverse']}")
    print(f"  状态: {result['status']}")
    print()

    # 场景2: 逆功率 (向电网送电)
    print("场景2: 逆功率运行")
    result = detector.run(P=-60.0, Q=5.0, U=222.0, f=50.1)
    print(f"  功率: {result['current_power_kW']:.1f} kW")
    print(f"  逆功率: {result['reverse_power']['is_reverse']}")
    print(f"  逆功率值: {result['reverse_power']['reverse_power_kW']:.1f} kW")
    print(f"  状态: {result['status']}")
    print()

    # 场景3: 疑似孤岛
    print("场景3: 疑似孤岛")
    result = detector.run(P=5.0, Q=2.0, U=195.0, f=49.3, df=0.8)
    print(f"  电压: {result['current_voltage_V']:.1f} V")
    print(f"  频率: {result['current_frequency_Hz']:.2f} Hz")
    print(f"  孤岛置信度: {result['islanding']['confidence']:.0%}")
    print(f"  状态: {result['status']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§12 S-12 直流分量注入监测

12.1 功能描述

模型名称: 直流分量注入监测 英文名称: DC Component Injection Monitoring 技术等级: L2a（需在线学习基准）

监测电力系统中直流分量注入，评估对变压器和设备的影响。

12.2 核心算法公式

直流分量提取:
    I_DC = (1/N) × Σ(i_k)

交流分量有效值:
    I_AC_rms = √((1/N) × Σ(i_k - I_DC)²)

直流偏置比:
    DC_ratio = |I_DC| / I_AC_rms × 100%

直流功率注入:
    P_DC = U_DC × I_DC

12.3 参考代码

"""
S-12 直流分量注入监测
=====================
功能: 监测系统中直流分量注入
输入: 电流波形, 电压波形
输出: 直流分量, 偏置比, 风险等级
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Optional, Tuple
from collections import deque

class DCComponentMonitor:
    """直流分量注入监测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_current: float = 100.0,
                 alarm_threshold: float = 1.0,
                 critical_threshold: float = 3.0,
                 baseline_history: int = 100):
        """
        参数:
            rated_current: 额定电流 (A)
            alarm_threshold: 告警阈值 (直流偏置比%)
            critical_threshold: 严重阈值
            baseline_history: 基准历史数据点数
        """
        self.I_rated = rated_current
        self.alarm_threshold = alarm_threshold
        self.critical_threshold = critical_threshold
        self.baseline_history = baseline_history

        # 数据缓存
        self.dc_history = deque(maxlen=baseline_history)
        self.baseline_dc = None

    def extract_dc_component(self, signal_data: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        提取直流分量

        I_DC = mean(signal_data)
        """
        return np.mean(signal_data)

    def extract_ac_rms(self, signal_data: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        提取交流分量有效值

        I_AC_rms = std(signal_data) = √(mean((x - mean(x))²))
        """
        dc = self.extract_dc_component(signal_data)
        ac_data = signal_data - dc
        return np.std(ac_data)

    def calculate_dc_ratio(self, signal_data: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        计算直流偏置比

        DC_ratio = |I_DC| / I_AC_rms × 100%
        """
        I_DC = self.extract_dc_component(signal_data)
        I_AC_rms = self.extract_ac_rms(signal_data)

        if I_AC_rms < 1e-9:
            return 0.0

        return abs(I_DC) / I_AC_rms * 100

    def calculate_power_dc(self, U_data: np.ndarray, I_data: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        计算直流功率

        P_DC = mean(U) × mean(I)
        """
        U_DC = self.extract_dc_component(U_data)
        I_DC = self.extract_dc_component(I_data)
        return U_DC * I_DC

    def update_baseline(self, signal_data: np.ndarray):
        """
        更新基准值
        """
        dc = self.extract_dc_component(signal_data)
        self.dc_history.append(dc)

        if len(self.dc_history) >= self.baseline_history:
            self.baseline_dc = np.mean(self.dc_history)

    def detect_anomaly(self, current_dc: float) -> Dict:
        """
        检测异常

        比较当前值与基准值
        """
        if self.baseline_dc is None:
            return {'is_anomaly': False, 'deviation': 0}

        deviation = current_dc - self.baseline_dc
        deviation_percent = abs(deviation) / (abs(self.baseline_dc) + 1e-9) * 100

        return {
            'is_anomaly': deviation_percent > 50,
            'baseline_dc': self.baseline_dc,
            'current_dc': current_dc,
            'deviation': deviation,
            'deviation_percent': deviation_percent
        }

    def classify_severity(self, dc_ratio: float) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """
        分类严重程度
        """
        if dc_ratio > self.critical_threshold:
            return 'CRITICAL', '严重直流偏置'
        elif dc_ratio > self.alarm_threshold:
            return 'WARNING', '直流偏置超标'
        elif dc_ratio > 0.5:
            return 'INFO', '轻微直流分量'
        else:
            return 'NORMAL', '无明显直流分量'

    def run(self, I_data: np.ndarray, U_data: Optional[np.ndarray] = None) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 提取直流分量
        I_DC = self.extract_dc_component(I_data)
        I_AC_rms = self.extract_ac_rms(I_data)
        dc_ratio = self.calculate_dc_ratio(I_data)

        # 更新基准
        self.update_baseline(I_data)

        # 异常检测
        anomaly = self.detect_anomaly(I_DC)

        # 严重程度
        level, level_desc = self.classify_severity(dc_ratio)

        # 直流功率
        P_DC = None
        if U_data is not None:
            P_DC = self.calculate_power_dc(U_data, I_data)

        # 状态判定
        if level == 'CRITICAL':
            status = 'CRITICAL'
            action = '严重直流分量，可能导致设备过热'
        elif level == 'WARNING':
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '直流分量超标，建议排查注入源'
        elif anomaly['is_anomaly']:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '检测到直流分量异常变化'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '直流分量在正常范围'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-12',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'dc_component_A': I_DC,
            'ac_rms_A': I_AC_rms,
            'dc_ratio_percent': dc_ratio,
            'dc_power_W': P_DC,
            'severity_level': level,
            'severity_desc': level_desc,
            'anomaly': anomaly,
            'status': status,
            'action': action,
            'baseline_dc': self.baseline_dc
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    monitor = DCComponentMonitor(rated_current=100.0)

    # 模拟交流电流 + 直流偏置
    t = np.linspace(0, 0.1, 512)
    I_normal = 100 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 50 * t)
    I_with_dc = I_normal + 3.0  # 3A直流偏置

    # 正常情况
    result_normal = monitor.run(I_normal)
    print(f"正常电流直流分量: {result_normal['dc_component_A']:.4f} A")
    print(f"直流偏置比: {result_normal['dc_ratio_percent']:.4f}%")
    print(f"状态: {result_normal['status']}")
    print()

    # 带直流偏置情况
    result_dc = monitor.run(I_with_dc)
    print("带直流偏置情况:")
    print(f"  直流分量: {result_dc['dc_component_A']:.4f} A")
    print(f"  交流有效值: {result_dc['ac_rms_A']:.4f} A")
    print(f"  直流偏置比: {result_dc['dc_ratio_percent']:.4f}%")
    print(f"  严重程度: {result_dc['severity_desc']}")
    print(f"  状态: {result_dc['status']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result_dc['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§13 S-13 设备过载动态预测

13.1 功能描述

模型名称: 设备过载动态预测 英文名称: Equipment Overload Dynamic Prediction 技术等级: L2b（需离线训练模型）

基于热累积模型和负荷趋势分析，预测设备是否会发生热过载。

13.2 核心算法公式

热累积微分方程:
    τ × dθ/dt = P_loss - P_cooling

温升计算:
    θ = θ_ambient + P_loss × R_th × (1 - exp(-t/τ))

过载预测:
    t_overload = τ × ln(P_loss / (P_loss - P_limit × R_th))

剩余安全时间:
    t_safe = t_overload - t_elapsed

13.3 参考代码

"""
S-13 设备过载动态预测
=====================
功能: 预测设备热过载风险
输入: 负载功率, 环境温度, 设备参数
输出: 过载时间预测, 安全时间, 风险等级
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Optional, Tuple
from collections import deque
from scipy.optimize import curve_fit

class OverloadPredictor:
    """设备过载动态预测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_power: float = 100.0,
                 thermal_resistance: float = 0.5,
                 time_constant: float = 3600.0,
                 ambient_temp: float = 40.0,
                 max_temp: float = 130.0,
                 overload_threshold: float = 1.1):
        """
        参数:
            rated_power: 额定功率 (kW)
            thermal_resistance: 热阻 (°C/kW)
            time_constant: 热时间常数 (秒)
            ambient_temp: 环境温度 (°C)
            max_temp: 最高允许温度 (°C)
            overload_threshold: 过载阈值 (额定功率倍数)
        """
        self.P_rated = rated_power
        self.R_th = thermal_resistance
        self.tau = time_constant
        self.T_amb = ambient_temp
        self.T_max = max_temp
        self.threshold = overload_threshold

        # 负荷历史
        self.power_history = deque(maxlen=1440)  # 24小时数据
        self.temp_history = deque(maxlen=1440)

    def calculate_steady_temperature(self, P: float) -> float:
        """
        计算稳态温升

        θ_steady = P × R_th
        """
        return P * self.R_th

    def calculate_temperature(self, P: float, t: float, 
                            T_initial: float = None) -> float:
        """
        计算任意时刻温度

        T(t) = T_amb + (T_steady - T_initial) × (1 - exp(-t/τ))
        """
        if T_initial is None:
            T_initial = self.T_amb

        T_steady = self.T_amb + P * self.R_th
        T_diff = T_steady - T_initial

        T = T_steady - T_diff * np.exp(-t / self.tau)
        return T

    def predict_overload_time(self, P: float, T_current: float) -> float:
        """
        预测过载发生时间

        t_overload = τ × ln((P × R_th) / (P × R_th - (T_max - T_amb)))
        """
        P_loss = P
        T_steady = self.T_amb + P_loss * self.R_th

        # 过载温差
        delta_T_overload = self.T_max - self.T_amb
        delta_T_current = T_current - self.T_amb

        if T_steady <= delta_T_overload:
            return float('inf')  # 不会过载

        # 过载发生时间
        if T_current >= self.T_max:
            return 0

        denominator = T_steady - delta_T_overload
        if denominator <= 0:
            return float('inf')

        numerator = T_steady - T_current
        if numerator <= 0:
            return 0

        t_overload = self.tau * np.log(numerator / denominator)
        return t_overload

    def analyze_load_trend(self) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """
        分析负荷趋势

        返回: (趋势斜率, 预测下一时刻负荷)
        """
        if len(self.power_history) < 10:
            return 0, np.mean(list(self.power_history)) if self.power_history else 0

        x = np.arange(len(self.power_history))
        y = np.array(list(self.power_history))

        # 线性拟合
        slope, intercept = np.polyfit(x, y, 1)
        predicted_power = slope * (len(x) + 1) + intercept

        return slope, max(0, predicted_power)

    def calculate_risk_level(self, t_safe: float, P: float) -> Tuple[str, float]:
        """
        计算风险等级
        """
        P_ratio = P / self.P_rated

        if t_safe < 0:
            return 'CRITICAL', 1.0
        elif t_safe < 300:  # 5分钟内
            return 'HIGH', 0.8
        elif t_safe < 1800:  # 30分钟内
            return 'WARNING', 0.5
        elif t_safe < 3600:  # 1小时内
            return 'INFO', 0.3
        elif P_ratio > self.threshold:
            return 'WARNING', 0.4
        else:
            return 'NORMAL', 0.1

    def run(self, P_current: float, T_current: float,
           t_elapsed: float = 0) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 记录历史
        self.power_history.append(P_current)
        self.temp_history.append(T_current)

        # 预测过载时间
        t_overload = self.predict_overload_time(P_current, T_current)

        # 安全时间
        t_safe = t_overload - t_elapsed

        # 负荷趋势分析
        trend_slope, predicted_power = self.analyze_load_trend()

        # 稳态温度预测
        T_steady = self.calculate_steady_temperature(P_current)

        # 综合风险
        risk_level, risk_score = self.calculate_risk_level(t_safe, P_current)

        # 状态判断
        if risk_level == 'CRITICAL':
            action = '设备即将过载，立即减载或停机'
        elif risk_level == 'HIGH':
            action = f'预计{t_safe/60:.0f}分钟内过载，准备减载'
        elif risk_level == 'WARNING':
            action = f'预计{t_safe/60:.0f}分钟后过载，持续关注'
        elif risk_level == 'INFO':
            action = '负荷偏高但暂无过载风险'
        else:
            action = '设备运行正常'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-13',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'current_power_kW': P_current,
            'power_ratio_percent': P_current / self.P_rated * 100,
            'current_temp_C': T_current,
            'steady_temp_C': T_steady,
            'overload_time_s': t_overload if t_overload < float('inf') else None,
            'safe_time_s': t_safe if t_safe > 0 else 0,
            'safe_time_min': t_safe / 60 if t_safe > 0 else 0,
            'load_trend_slope': trend_slope,
            'predicted_power_kW': predicted_power,
            'risk_level': risk_level,
            'risk_score': risk_score,
            'status': risk_level,
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    predictor = OverloadPredictor(
        rated_power=100.0,
        thermal_resistance=0.5,
        time_constant=3600.0,
        ambient_temp=40.0,
        max_temp=130.0
    )

    # 模拟过载场景
    result = predictor.run(
        P_current=115.0,  # 115%负载
        T_current=85.0,   # 当前温度85°C
        t_elapsed=1800    # 已运行30分钟
    )

    print("设备过载动态预测:")
    print(f"  当前功率: {result['current_power_kW']:.1f} kW ({result['power_ratio_percent']:.1f}%)")
    print(f"  当前温度: {result['current_temp_C']:.1f}°C")
    print(f"  稳态温度: {result['steady_temp_C']:.1f}°C")
    print(f"  预计过载时间: {result['overload_time_s']/60:.1f} 分钟" if result['overload_time_s'] else "  预计过载时间: 不会过载")
    print(f"  剩余安全时间: {result['safe_time_min']:.1f} 分钟")
    print(f"  负荷趋势: {result['load_trend_slope']:.3f} kW/点")
    print(f"  预测功率: {result['predicted_power_kW']:.1f} kW")
    print(f"  风险等级: {result['risk_level']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§14 S-14 电缆接头红外温度关联预警

14.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电缆接头红外温度关联预警 英文名称: Cable Joint Infrared Temperature Correlation Early Warning 技术等级: L2a（需在线校准）

通过红外测温与负荷电流关联分析，提前预警电缆接头过热隐患。

14.2 核心算法公式

温升计算:
    ΔT = T_joint - T_ref

归一化温升:
    ΔT_norm = ΔT / (I/I_rated)²

温差比值:
    T_ratio = (T_joint - T_ambient) / (T_ref - T_ambient)

预警指数:
    Warning_index = ΔT_norm / ΔT_baseline × 100%

14.3 参考代码

"""
S-14 电缆接头红外温度关联预警
=============================
功能: 基于红外测温的电缆接头预警
输入: 接头温度, 参考点温度, 环境温度, 负荷电流
输出: 温升指数, 预警等级, 维护建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Optional, Tuple, List
from collections import deque

class CableJointEarlyWarning:
    """电缆接头红外温度关联预警模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_current: float = 400.0,
                 baseline_temp_rise: float = 30.0,
                 alarm_temp: float = 90.0,
                 critical_temp: float = 120.0,
                 history_size: int = 168):
        """
        参数:
            rated_current: 额定电流 (A)
            baseline_temp_rise: 额定负荷下的基准温升 (°C)
            alarm_temp: 告警温度阈值 (°C)
            critical_temp: 严重温度阈值 (°C)
            history_size: 历史数据点数 (默认7天×24小时)
        """
        self.I_rated = rated_current
        self.baseline_rise = baseline_temp_rise
        self.alarm_temp = alarm_temp
        self.critical_temp = critical_temp
        self.history_size = history_size

        # 历史数据
        self.temp_history = deque(maxlen=history_size)
        self.current_history = deque(maxlen=history_size)
        self.baseline_model = None

    def calculate_temp_rise(self, T_joint: float, T_ref: float) -> float:
        """
        计算温升
        """
        return T_joint - T_ref

    def calculate_normalized_rise(self, T_joint: float, T_amb: float,
                                I: float) -> float:
        """
        计算归一化温升

        消除负荷电流影响后的温升
        """
        if I < 1e-6:
            return 0

        current_rise = T_joint - T_amb
        expected_rise = self.baseline_rise * (I / self.I_rated) ** 2

        if expected_rise < 1e-6:
            return current_rise

        return current_rise / expected_rise

    def calculate_temp_ratio(self, T_joint: float, T_amb: float,
                           T_ref: float) -> float:
        """
        计算温差比值
        """
        T_rise_joint = T_joint - T_amb
        T_rise_ref = T_ref - T_amb

        if abs(T_rise_ref) < 1e-6:
            return 1.0

        return T_rise_joint / T_rise_ref

    def update_baseline(self, T_joint: float, I: float):
        """
        更新基准模型
        """
        self.temp_history.append(T_joint)
        self.current_history.append(I)

        # 计算滑动平均基准
        if len(self.temp_history) >= 24:
            recent_temps = list(self.temp_history)[-24:]
            recent_currents = list(self.current_history)[-24:]

            avg_temp = np.mean(recent_temps)
            avg_current = np.mean(recent_currents)

            if avg_current > 0:
                self.baseline_model = {
                    'avg_temp': avg_temp,
                    'avg_current': avg_current,
                    'avg_rise': avg_temp - 40,  # 假设环境温度40°C
                    'std_temp': np.std(recent_temps)
                }

    def calculate_warning_index(self, T_joint: float, T_amb: float, I: float) -> float:
        """
        计算预警指数
        """
        # 当前归一化温升
        current_norm_rise = self.calculate_normalized_rise(T_joint, T_amb, I)

        if self.baseline_model is None:
            # 无基准时使用绝对温度
            return (T_joint - T_amb) / self.baseline_rise * 100

        # 与基准比较
        baseline_norm = self.baseline_model['avg_rise']
        if baseline_norm < 1e-6:
            return current_norm_rise * 100

        return current_norm_rise / baseline_norm * 100

    def classify_risk(self, warning_index: float, T_joint: float) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """
        风险分类
        """
        if T_joint >= self.critical_temp:
            return 'CRITICAL', '严重过热'
        elif T_joint >= self.alarm_temp:
            return 'HIGH', '温度告警'
        elif warning_index > 150:
            return 'HIGH', '异常温升'
        elif warning_index > 120:
            return 'WARNING', '温升偏高'
        elif warning_index > 100:
            return 'INFO', '温升正常'
        else:
            return 'NORMAL', '状态良好'

    def run(self, T_joint: float, T_ref: float, T_amb: float,
           I: float) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 更新基准
        self.update_baseline(T_joint, I)

        # 计算各项指标
        temp_rise = self.calculate_temp_rise(T_joint, T_ref)
        norm_rise = self.calculate_normalized_rise(T_joint, T_amb, I)
        temp_ratio = self.calculate_temp_ratio(T_joint, T_amb, T_ref)
        warning_index = self.calculate_warning_index(T_joint, T_amb, I)

        # 风险分类
        level, desc = self.classify_risk(warning_index, T_joint)

        # 状态判断
        if level == 'CRITICAL':
            action = '温度超过临界值，立即停电检查'
        elif level == 'HIGH':
            action = '温度告警，24小时内安排检修'
        elif level == 'WARNING':
            action = '温升异常，建议近期检查'
        else:
            action = '接头温度正常'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-14',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'joint_temp_C': T_joint,
            'reference_temp_C': T_ref,
            'ambient_temp_C': T_amb,
            'current_A': I,
            'current_ratio_percent': I / self.I_rated * 100,
            'temp_rise_C': temp_rise,
            'normalized_rise': norm_rise,
            'temp_ratio': temp_ratio,
            'warning_index': warning_index,
            'risk_level': level,
            'risk_desc': desc,
            'status': level,
            'action': action,
            'baseline': self.baseline_model
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    monitor = CableJointEarlyWarning(
        rated_current=400.0,
        baseline_temp_rise=30.0,
        alarm_temp=90.0
    )

    # 模拟检测数据
    result = monitor.run(
        T_joint=78.5,   # 接头温度
        T_ref=65.0,    # 参考点温度
        T_amb=35.0,    # 环境温度
        I=350.0        # 负荷电流
    )

    print("电缆接头红外温度预警:")
    print(f"  接头温度: {result['joint_temp_C']:.1f}°C")
    print(f"  参考温度: {result['reference_temp_C']:.1f}°C")
    print(f"  环境温度: {result['ambient_temp_C']:.1f}°C")
    print(f"  负荷电流: {result['current_A']:.1f}A ({result['current_ratio_percent']:.1f}%)")
    print(f"  实际温升: {result['temp_rise_C']:.1f}°C")
    print(f"  归一化温升: {result['normalized_rise']:.2f}")
    print(f"  温差比值: {result['temp_ratio']:.2f}")
    print(f"  预警指数: {result['warning_index']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  风险等级: {result['risk_level']} - {result['risk_desc']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§15 S-15 局部放电模式识别

15.1 功能描述

模型名称: 局部放电模式识别 英文名称: Partial Discharge Pattern Recognition 技术等级: L3（需深度学习模型）

基于PRPD/PRPS图谱分析，识别局部放电类型（绝缘内部放电、表面放电、尖刺放电等）。

15.2 核心算法公式

PRPD图谱统计特征:
    H_n(φ) = Σ n(φ, i)  (相位窗内放电次数)
    H_qn(φ) = Σ q_n(φ, i)  (相位窗内放电量)

PRPS图谱特征:
    时域相关性

放电模式相似度:
    Similarity = exp(-||H_test - H_train||² / (2σ²))

15.3 参考代码

"""
S-15 局部放电模式识别
=====================
功能: 基于PRPD/PRPS图谱的放电模式识别
输入: 放电信号, 相位信息, 采样率
输出: 放电类型, 置信度, 严重程度
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from collections import defaultdict
from scipy.stats import pearsonr

class PDPatternRecognizer:
    """局部放电模式识别模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 n_phase_bins: int = 256,
                 n_q_bins: int = 128,
                 sampling_rate: float = 1e6):
        """
        参数:
            n_phase_bins: 相位窗数量
            n_q_bins: 放电量窗数量
            sampling_rate: 采样率 (Hz)
        """
        self.n_phi = n_phase_bins
        self.n_q = n_q_bins
        self.fs = sampling_rate

        # 放电模式库 (简化版)
        self.pd_patterns = {
            'internal': {
                'name': '绝缘内部放电',
                'phase_distribution': '对称双峰',
                'q_distribution': '指数衰减',
                'polarity': '同极性'
            },
            'surface': {
                'name': '表面放电',
                'phase_distribution': '宽相位带',
                'q_distribution': '较均匀',
                'polarity': '异极性'
            },
            'corona': {
                'name': '尖刺放电',
                'phase_distribution': '单峰偏置',
                'q_distribution': '集中在小q',
                'polarity': '单极性'
            },
            'void': {
                'name': '空穴放电',
                'phase_distribution': '对称分布',
                'q_distribution': '固定幅值',
                'polarity': '同极性'
            }
        }

    def build_prpd(self, pd_data: np.ndarray, phase: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        """
        构建PRPD图谱

        返回: (相位分布H_n, 放电量分布H_qn)
        """
        phi_bins = np.linspace(0, 360, self.n_phi + 1)
        q_bins = np.linspace(0, np.max(np.abs(pd_data)) * 1.1, self.n_q + 1)

        H_n = np.zeros(self.n_phi)
        H_qn = np.zeros(self.n_phi)

        for i in range(len(pd_data)):
            if abs(pd_data[i]) > 0:  # 有效放电
                phi_idx = np.searchsorted(phi_bins, phase[i] % 360) - 1
                if 0 <= phi_idx < self.n_phi:
                    H_n[phi_idx] += 1
                    H_qn[phi_idx] += abs(pd_data[i])

        return H_n, H_qn

    def analyze_phase_distribution(self, H_n: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """
        分析相位分布特征
        """
        # 找峰值
        peaks_idx = np.where((H_n[1:-1] > H_n[:-2]) & (H_n[1:-1] > H_n[2:]))[0] + 1

        # 计算分布宽度
        total = np.sum(H_n)
        cumsum = np.cumsum(H_n) / total

        # 计算10%-90%宽度
        phi_10 = np.searchsorted(cumsum, 0.1) * 360 / self.n_phi
        phi_90 = np.searchsorted(cumsum, 0.9) * 360 / self.n_phi

        return {
            'n_peaks': len(peaks_idx),
            'peak_positions': peaks_idx * 360 / self.n_phi,
            'distribution_width': phi_90 - phi_10,
            'symmetry': 'symmetric' if len(peaks_idx) == 2 and abs(peaks_idx[0] - peaks_idx[1] - 180) < 20 else 'asymmetric'
        }

    def analyze_q_distribution(self, H_qn: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """
        分析放电量分布特征
        """
        total_q = np.sum(H_qn)

        # 计算等效放电量
        if np.sum(H_n := np.ones_like(H_qn)) > 0:
            avg_q = total_q / np.sum(H_n)
        else:
            avg_q = 0

        # 分布特征
        q_normalized = H_qn / (total_q + 1e-10)

        # 集中度 (熵)
        entropy = -np.sum(q_normalized * np.log(q_normalized + 1e-10))
        max_entropy = np.log(len(q_normalized))
        concentration = 1 - entropy / max_entropy

        return {
            'total_charge': total_q,
            'average_charge': avg_q,
            'concentration': concentration,
            'max_charge': np.max(H_qn),
            'distribution_type': 'concentrated' if concentration > 0.7 else 'dispersed'
        }

    def classify_pd_type(self, phase_info: Dict, q_info: Dict) -> Tuple[str, float]:
        """
        分类放电类型
        """
        scores = {}

        for pd_type, pattern in self.pd_patterns.items():
            score = 0

            # 相位分布匹配
            if phase_info['symmetry'] == 'symmetric' and '对称' in pattern['phase_distribution']:
                score += 0.4
            elif phase_info['symmetry'] == 'asymmetric' and '单峰' in pattern['phase_distribution']:
                score += 0.4

            # 放电量分布匹配
            if q_info['concentration'] > 0.5 and '集中' in pattern['q_distribution']:
                score += 0.3
            elif q_info['concentration'] < 0.5 and '均匀' in pattern['q_distribution']:
                score += 0.3

            scores[pd_type] = min(1.0, score)

        # 选择最高分
        best_type = max(scores, key=scores.get)
        confidence = scores[best_type]

        return best_type, confidence

    def calculate_severity(self, H_n: np.ndarray, q_info: Dict) -> Tuple[str, float]:
        """
        计算严重程度
        """
        # 放电强度
        total_discharges = np.sum(H_n)
        total_charge = q_info['total_charge']

        # 放电密度
        discharge_density = total_discharges / 1000  # 假设10秒窗口

        # 综合评分
        severity_score = (discharge_density / 100) * 0.5 + (total_charge / 10000) * 0.5

        if severity_score > 0.8:
            return 'CRITICAL', severity_score
        elif severity_score > 0.5:
            return 'HIGH', severity_score
        elif severity_score > 0.2:
            return 'WARNING', severity_score
        else:
            return 'LOW', severity_score

    def run(self, pd_data: np.ndarray, phase: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 构建PRPD图谱
        H_n, H_qn = self.build_prpd(pd_data, phase)

        # 分析特征
        phase_info = self.analyze_phase_distribution(H_n)
        q_info = self.analyze_q_distribution(H_qn)

        # 分类放电类型
        pd_type, confidence = self.classify_pd_type(phase_info, q_info)

        # 严重程度
        severity, severity_score = self.calculate_severity(H_n, q_info)

        # 状态判断
        if severity == 'CRITICAL':
            action = '严重局部放电，建议立即停电检测'
        elif severity == 'HIGH':
            action = f'存在{pd_info[\"name\"]}，建议尽快检修'
        elif severity == 'WARNING':
            action = '检测到轻微放电，持续监测'
        else:
            action = '放电水平正常'

        pd_info = self.pd_patterns[pd_type]

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-15',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'pd_type': pd_type,
            'pd_type_name': pd_info['name'],
            'confidence': confidence,
            'severity': severity,
            'severity_score': severity_score,
            'phase_analysis': phase_info,
            'charge_analysis': q_info,
            'total_discharges': np.sum(H_n),
            'status': severity,
            'action': action,
            'prpd_spectrum': H_n.tolist()
        }


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    recognizer = PDPatternRecognizer()

    # 模拟局部放电数据
    np.random.seed(42)
    n_samples = 5000

    # 生成模拟相位和放电量
    phase = np.random.uniform(0, 360, n_samples)
    pd_magnitude = np.abs(np.random.exponential(100, n_samples))

    # 加上放电脉冲
    pulse_positions = np.random.choice(n_samples, size=200, replace=False)
    pd_magnitude[pulse_positions] += 500

    result = recognizer.run(pd_magnitude, phase)

    print("局部放电模式识别:")
    print(f"  放电类型: {result['pd_type_name']}")
    print(f"  置信度: {result['confidence']:.0%}")
    print(f"  严重程度: {result['severity']} ({result['severity_score']:.2f})")
    print(f"  总放电次数: {result['total_discharges']}")
    print(f"  相位峰值数: {result['phase_analysis']['n_peaks']}")
    print(f"  分布对称性: {result['phase_analysis']['symmetry']}")
    print(f"  放电量集中度: {result['charge_analysis']['concentration']:.2f}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§16 S-16 保护定值在线校核

16.1 功能描述

模型名称: 保护定值在线校核 英文名称: Protection Setting Online Verification 技术等级: L2a（需在线学习）

在线校核继电保护定值的合理性，基于电网实际运行状态和故障仿真。

16.2 核心算法公式

灵敏度校验:
    K_sens = I_f_min / I_set
    要求: K_sens ≥ 1.5 (主保护), K_sens ≥ 1.2 (后备保护)

选择性校验:
    K_sel = I_set_downstream / I_set_upstream
    要求: K_sel ≥ 1.2

可靠性校验:
    K_rel = I_set / I_load_max × K_safety
    要求: K_rel ≥ 1.1

瞬时速断倍数:
    K_inst = I_set_inst / I_load_inrush

16.3 参考代码

"""
S-16 保护定值在线校核
=====================
功能: 在线校核继电保护定值合理性
输入: 保护定值, 运行电流, 故障电流, 负荷电流
输出: 校验结果, 建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from collections import deque

class ProtectionSettingVerifier:
    """保护定值在线校核模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 protection_type: str = 'overcurrent',
                 primary_sensitivity: float = 1.5,
                 backup_sensitivity: float = 1.2,
                 selectivity_ratio: float = 1.2,
                 safety_factor: float = 1.1):
        """
        参数:
            protection_type: 保护类型 (overcurrent/distance/diff)
            primary_sensitivity: 主保护灵敏度要求
            backup_sensitivity: 后备保护灵敏度要求
            selectivity_ratio: 选择性配合系数
            safety_factor: 可靠性安全系数
        """
        self.protection_type = protection_type
        self.K_sens_primary = primary_sensitivity
        self.K_sens_backup = backup_sensitivity
        self.K_sel = selectivity_ratio
        self.K_safety = safety_factor

        # 校核历史
        self.verification_history = deque(maxlen=1000)

    def verify_sensitivity(self, I_f_min: float, I_set: float,
                          is_primary: bool = True) -> Dict:
        """
        灵敏度校验

        K_sens = I_f_min / I_set
        """
        K_sens = I_f_min / I_set if I_set > 0 else 0

        K_required = self.K_sens_primary if is_primary else self.K_sens_backup

        return {
            'K_sens': K_sens,
            'K_required': K_required,
            'passed': K_sens >= K_required,
            'margin_percent': (K_sens - K_required) / K_required * 100 if K_required > 0 else 0
        }

    def verify_selectivity(self, I_set_up: float, I_set_down: float) -> Dict:
        """
        选择性校验

        K_sel = I_set_down / I_set_up
        """
        K_sel = I_set_down / I_set_up if I_set_up > 0 else 0

        return {
            'K_sel': K_sel,
            'K_required': self.K_sel,
            'passed': K_sel >= self.K_sel,
            'margin_percent': (K_sel - self.K_sel) / self.K_sel * 100 if self.K_sel > 0 else 0
        }

    def verify_reliability(self, I_set: float, I_load_max: float) -> Dict:
        """
        可靠性校验

        K_rel = I_set / (I_load_max × K_safety)
        """
        denominator = I_load_max * self.K_safety
        K_rel = I_set / denominator if denominator > 0 else 0

        # 可靠性系数应该小于1，表示保护不会误动
        reliability_ok = K_rel < 1.0 and K_rel > 0.5

        return {
            'K_rel': K_rel,
            'passed': reliability_ok,
            'margin_percent': (1 - K_rel) * 100 if K_rel < 1 else 0
        }

    def verify_instantaneous(self, I_set_inst: float, I_load_inrush: float) -> Dict:
        """
        瞬时速断校验

        确保躲过最大涌流
        """
        K_inst = I_set_inst / I_load_inrush if I_load_inrush > 0 else float('inf')

        # 应该大于1.2倍涌流
        return {
            'K_inst': K_inst,
            'passed': K_inst >= 1.2,
            'margin_percent': (K_inst - 1.2) / 1.2 * 100 if K_inst > 0 else 0
        }

    def verify_time_coordination(self, t_up: float, t_down: float,
                               delta_t: float = 0.3) -> Dict:
        """
        时间配合校验

        t_up - t_down >= delta_t
        """
        coordination_ok = t_up - t_down >= delta_t

        return {
            'time_difference': t_up - t_down,
            'delta_t_required': delta_t,
            'passed': coordination_ok,
            'margin_s': (t_up - t_down) - delta_t if coordination_ok else 0
        }

    def run(self, I_set: float, I_set_inst: float,
           I_f_min: float, I_load_max: float,
           I_load_inrush: float = 0,
           I_set_upstream: float = None,
           is_primary: bool = True) -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数
        """
        # 灵敏度校验
        sens_result = self.verify_sensitivity(I_f_min, I_set, is_primary)

        # 可靠性校验
        rel_result = self.verify_reliability(I_set, I_load_max)

        # 瞬时速断校验
        inst_result = self.verify_instantaneous(I_set_inst, I_load_inrush)

        # 选择性校验
        sel_result = None
        if I_set_upstream is not None:
            sel_result = self.verify_selectivity(I_set_upstream, I_set)

        # 综合判定
        all_passed = sens_result['passed'] and rel_result['passed'] and inst_result['passed']
        if sel_result:
            all_passed = all_passed and sel_result['passed']

        # 问题诊断
        issues = []
        if not sens_result['passed']:
            issues.append(f"灵敏度不足 ({sens_result['K_sens']:.2f} < {sens_result['K_required']})")
        if not rel_result['passed']:
            issues.append(f"可靠性不足 ({rel_result['K_rel']:.2f})")
        if not inst_result['passed']:
            issues.append(f"瞬时速断不满足 ({inst_result['K_inst']:.2f} < 1.2)")
        if sel_result and not sel_result['passed']:
            issues.append(f"选择性不足 ({sel_result['K_sel']:.2f} < {sel_result['K_required']})")

        if not issues:
            status = 'PASS'
            action = '保护定值设置合理'
        elif len(issues) == 1:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = f'建议调整: {issues[0]}'
        else:
            status = 'FAIL'
            action = f'定值问题: {", ".join(issues)}'

        result = {
            'model_id': 'S-16',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'protection_type': self.protection_type,
            'sensitivity': sens_result,
            'reliability': rel_result,
            'instantaneous': inst_result,
            'selectivity': sel_result,
            'overall_pass': all_passed,
            'issues': issues,
            'status': status,
            'action': action
        }

        self.verification_history.append(result)
        return result


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    verifier = ProtectionSettingVerifier()

    # 模拟保护定值和电流数据
    result = verifier.run(
        I_set=100.0,          # 定时限电流定值 (A)
        I_set_inst=500.0,     # 瞬时速断定值 (A)
        I_f_min=200.0,        # 最小故障电流 (A)
        I_load_max=80.0,      # 最大负荷电流 (A)
        I_load_inrush=150.0,  # 最大涌流 (A)
        I_set_upstream=120.0, # 上级保护定值 (A)
        is_primary=True
    )

    print("保护定值在线校核:")
    print(f"  灵敏度: {result['sensitivity']['K_sens']:.2f} (要求≥{result['sensitivity']['K_required']})")
    print(f"    通过: {result['sensitivity']['passed']}, 裕度: {result['sensitivity']['margin_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  可靠性: {result['reliability']['K_rel']:.2f}")
    print(f"    通过: {result['reliability']['passed']}, 裕度: {result['reliability']['margin_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  瞬时速断: {result['instantaneous']['K_inst']:.2f}")
    print(f"    通过: {result['instantaneous']['passed']}")
    print(f"  选择性: {result['selectivity']['K_sel']:.2f} (要求≥{result['selectivity']['K_required']})")
    print(f"    通过: {result['selectivity']['passed']}")
    print(f"  综合结果: {result['overall_pass']}")
    print(f"  状态: {result['status']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§17 S-17 断路器机械特性监测

17.1 功能描述

模型名称: 断路器机械特性监测 英文名称: Circuit Breaker Mechanical Characteristic Monitoring 技术等级: L2b（需专业传感器）

监测断路器分合闸时间、速度、同步性等机械特性，评估机械状态。

17.2 核心算法公式

分闸时间:
    t_open = t_contact_separation - t_trip_command

合闸时间:
    t_close = t_contact_engagement - t_close_command

平均分闸速度:
    v_avg = stroke / t_open

分合闸同步性:
    Δt_sync = |t_A - t_B|  (三相不同期)

弹跳时间:
    t_bounce = t_recontact - t_first_contact

17.3 参考代码

"""
S-17 断路器机械特性监测
======================
功能: 监测断路器机械特性
输入: 分合闸电流波形, 位移传感器数据, 时间戳
输出: 机械特性参数, 状态评估
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from collections import deque

class BreakerMechanicalMonitor:
    """断路器机械特性监测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_stroke: float = 200.0,
                 rated_time_open: float = 0.06,
                 rated_time_close: float = 0.10,
                 rated_v_open: float = 4.0,
                 rated_v_close: float = 3.0,
                 sync_threshold: float = 0.005,
                 bounce_threshold: float = 0.002):
        """
        参数:
            rated_stroke: 额定行程 (mm)
            rated_time_open: 额定分闸时间 (s)
            rated_time_close: 额定合闸时间 (s)
            rated_v_open: 额定分闸速度 (m/s)
            rated_v_close: 额定合闸速度 (m/s)
            sync_threshold: 同步性阈值 (s)
            bounce_threshold: 弹跳时间阈值 (s)
        """
        self.stroke = rated_stroke
        self.t_open_rated = rated_time_open
        self.t_close_rated = rated_time_close
        self.v_open_rated = rated_v_open
        self.v_close_rated = rated_v_close
        self.sync_th = sync_threshold
        self.bounce_th = bounce_threshold

        # 特性历史
        self.characteristic_history = deque(maxlen=100)

    def extract_timing_from_current(self, current: np.ndarray, fs: float) -> float:
        """
        从分合闸电流提取动作时间

        检测电流突变点
        """
        # 计算电流变化率
        di = np.diff(current)

        # 找到最大变化率点
        max_di_idx = np.argmax(np.abs(di))

        # 对应时间
        t_action = max_di_idx / fs

        return t_action

    def extract_timing_from_displacement(self, displacement: np.ndarray, 
                                        fs: float, mode: str = 'open') -> Dict:
        """
        从位移曲线提取机械特性

        mode: 'open' 或 'close'
        """
        # 归一化位移
        d_norm = (displacement - displacement.min()) / (displacement.max() - displacement.min() + 1e-10)

        # 动作时间 (到达90%位置)
        threshold = 0.9 if mode == 'open' else 0.5  # 分闸用90%, 合闸用50%
        t_action_idx = np.searchsorted(d_norm, threshold)
        t_action = t_action_idx / fs

        # 总动作时间 (0%到100%)
        t_total_idx = len(d_norm) - np.searchsorted(d_norm[::-1], 0.1)
        t_total = t_total_idx / fs

        # 速度计算
        v_avg = self.stroke / 1000 / t_total  # m/s

        # 弹跳检测
        bounces = []
        for i in range(1, len(d_norm) - 1):
            if (d_norm[i] > d_norm[i-1] and d_norm[i] > d_norm[i+1] and
                d_norm[i] > 0.1 and d_norm[i] < 0.9):
                bounces.append(i / fs)

        t_bounce = 0
        if len(bounces) > 1:
            t_bounce = bounces[1] - bounces[0]

        return {
            't_action': t_action,
            't_total': t_total,
            'v_avg': v_avg,
            'n_bounces': len(bounces),
            't_bounce': t_bounce,
            'bounce_detected': t_bounce > self.bounce_th
        }

    def calculate_phase_sync(self, t_A: float, t_B: float, t_C: float) -> Dict:
        """
        计算三相不同期

        Δt_sync = max(|t_i - t_j|)
        """
        max_diff = max(abs(t_A - t_B), abs(t_B - t_C), abs(t_C - t_A))

        return {
            't_A': t_A,
            't_B': t_B,
            't_C': t_C,
            'max_diff': max_diff,
            'sync_ok': max_diff < self.sync_th,
            'worst_pair': self._get_worst_pair(t_A, t_B, t_C)
        }

    def _get_worst_pair(self, t_A: float, t_B: float, t_C: float) -> str:
        """找出最大不同期的相别"""
        diff_AB = abs(t_A - t_B)
        diff_BC = abs(t_B - t_C)
        diff_CA = abs(t_C - t_A)

        max_diff = max(diff_AB, diff_BC, diff_CA)

        if max_diff == diff_AB:
            return 'AB'
        elif max_diff == diff_BC:
            return 'BC'
        else:
            return 'CA'

    def assess_mechanical_condition(self, results: Dict) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """
        评估机械状态
        """
        issues = []

        # 时间检查
        if results['timing']['t_total'] > self.t_open_rated * 1.2:
            issues.append(f"动作时间过长 ({results['timing']['t_total']*1000:.1f}ms)")

        # 速度检查
        if results['timing']['v_avg'] < self.v_open_rated * 0.8:
            issues.append(f"动作速度偏低 ({results['timing']['v_avg']:.2f}m/s)")
        elif results['timing']['v_avg'] > self.v_open_rated * 1.2:
            issues.append(f"动作速度偏高 ({results['timing']['v_avg']:.2f}m/s)")

        # 弹跳检查
        if results['timing']['bounce_detected']:
            issues.append(f"存在弹跳 ({results['timing']['t_bounce']*1000:.1f}ms)")

        # 同步性检查
        if not results['sync']['sync_ok']:
            issues.append(f"三相不同期 ({results['sync']['max_diff']*1000:.1f}ms)")

        if not issues:
            return 'NORMAL', '机械特性正常'
        elif len(issues) == 1:
            return 'WARNING', issues[0]
        else:
            return 'CRITICAL', f"多项异常: {issues[0]}"

    def run(self, displacement_A: np.ndarray, displacement_B: np.ndarray,
           displacement_C: np.ndarray, fs: float = 10000.0,
           operation_type: str = 'open') -> Dict:
        """
        模型主运行函数

        参数:
            displacement_A/B/C: 三相位移数据
            fs: 采样频率
            operation_type: 操作类型 ('open' 或 'close')
        """
        # 提取三相特性
        char_A = self.extract_timing_from_displacement(displacement_A, fs, operation_type)
        char_B = self.extract_timing_from_displacement(displacement_B, fs, operation_type)
        char_C = self.extract_timing_from_displacement(displacement_C, fs, operation_type)

        # 计算同步性
        sync = self.calculate_phase_sync(
            char_A['t_action'], char_B['t_action'], char_C['t_action']
        )

        # 平均特性
        avg_t_total = (char_A['t_total'] + char_B['t_total'] + char_C['t_total']) / 3
        avg_v = (char_A['v_avg'] + char_B['v_avg'] + char_C['v_avg']) / 3

        # 评估状态
        results = {
            'timing': {
                'A': char_A,
                'B': char_B,
                'C': char_C,
                'avg_t_total': avg_t_total,
                'avg_v': avg_v
            },
            'sync': sync
        }

        level, desc = self.assess_mechanical_condition(results)

        if level == 'CRITICAL':
            action = '机械特性严重异常，建议停电检修'
        elif level == 'WARNING':
            action = f'机械问题: {desc}'
        else:
            action = '断路器机械状态良好'

        output = {
            'model_id': 'S-17',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'operation_type': operation_type,
            'phase_A': char_A,
            'phase_B': char_B,
            'phase_C': char_C,
            'synchronization': sync,
            'avg_operation_time_ms': avg_t_total * 1000,
            'avg_operation_speed_m_s': avg_v,
            'condition_level': level,
            'condition_desc': desc,
            'status': level,
            'action': action
        }

        self.characteristic_history.append(output)
        return output


def example_usage():
    """使用示例"""
    monitor = BreakerMechanicalMonitor()

    # 模拟分闸位移曲线
    fs = 10000
    t = np.linspace(0, 0.1, 1000)

    # A相: 正常分闸
    d_A = 200 * (1 - np.exp(-t / 0.015)) * (t < 0.06) + 200 * np.ones_like(t) * (t >= 0.06)
    # B相: 稍慢
    d_B = 200 * (1 - np.exp(-t / 0.016)) * (t < 0.065) + 200 * np.ones_like(t) * (t >= 0.065)
    # C相: 有弹跳
    d_C = 200 * (1 - np.exp(-t / 0.015)) * (t < 0.058)
    d_C[t >= 0.058] = 180 + 20 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 500 * (t[t >= 0.058] - 0.058)) * np.exp(-(t[t >= 0.058] - 0.058) / 0.005)
    d_C[t >= 0.065] = 200

    result = monitor.run(d_A, d_B, d_C, fs, 'open')

    print("断路器机械特性监测:")
    print(f"  操作类型: {result['operation_type']}")
    print(f"  A相动作时间: {result['phase_A']['t_total']*1000:.1f}ms, 速度: {result['phase_A']['v_avg']:.2f}m/s")
    print(f"  B相动作时间: {result['phase_B']['t_total']*1000:.1f}ms, 速度: {result['phase_B']['v_avg']:.2f}m/s")
    print(f"  C相动作时间: {result['phase_C']['t_total']*1000:.1f}ms, 速度: {result['phase_C']['v_avg']:.2f}m/s")
    print(f"  平均动作时间: {result['avg_operation_time_ms']:.1f}ms")
    print(f"  三相不同期: {result['synchronization']['max_diff']*1000:.2f}ms")
    print(f"  同步状态: {'正常' if result['synchronization']['sync_ok'] else '异常'}")
    print(f"  C相弹跳: {result['phase_C']['n_bounces']}次, {result['phase_C']['t_bounce']*1000:.2f}ms")
    print(f"  状态: {result['condition_level']}")
    print(f"  建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§18 S-18 涌流与内部故障区分

18.1 功能描述

模型名称: 涌流与内部故障区分 英文名称: Inrush Current vs Internal Fault Discrimination 技术等级: L2b（需离线训练模型）

区分变压器励磁涌流和内部故障电流，防止保护误动。

18.2 核心算法公式

二次谐波制动判据:
    K_2nd = I_2nd / I_fund × 100%
    涌流: K_2nd > 15-20%
    故障: K_2nd < 10%

波形对称度:
    α = |(I_pos - |I_neg|)| / I_pos × 100%
    涌流: α > 60°
    故障: α < 15°

间断角判据:
    θ_d = 间断角
    涌流: θ_d > 65°

18.3 参考代码

"""
S-18 涌流与内部故障区分
========================
功能: 区分励磁涌流和内部故障电流
输入: 电流波形
输出: 区分结果, 置信度
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Tuple
from scipy.fft import fft

class InrushFaultDiscriminator:
    """涌流与故障区分模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 harmonic2_threshold: float = 0.15,
                 harmonic5_threshold: float = 0.25,
                 symmetry_threshold: float = 60.0,
                间断角_threshold: float = 65.0):
        """
        参数:
            harmonic2_threshold: 二次谐波制动系数阈值
            harmonic5_threshold: 五次谐波阈值
            symmetry_threshold: 对称度阈值 (度)
            间断角_threshold: 间断角阈值 (度)
        """
        self.K_2nd_th = harmonic2_threshold
        self.K_5th_th = harmonic5_threshold
        self.sym_th = symmetry_threshold
        self.angle_th =间断角_threshold

    def extract_harmonics(self, current: np.ndarray, fs: float) -> Dict:
        """提取谐波分量"""
        N = len(current)
        yf = np.abs(fft(current))[:N//2] * 2 / N
        xf = fftfreq(N, 1/fs)[:N//2]

        # 基波频率
        f_fund = 50.0
        df = fs / N

        harmonics = {}
        for h in [2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]:
            freq = h * f_fund
            idx = int(freq / df)
            if idx < len(yf):
                harmonics[f'{h}rd'] = yf[idx]

        # 基波幅值
        fund_idx = int(f_fund / df)
        I_fund = yf[fund_idx] if fund_idx < len(yf) else 1e-6

        return {k: v / I_fund for k, v in harmonics.items()}

    def calculate_symmetry(self, current: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """计算波形对称度"""
        # 找到正负半波峰值
        I_pos = np.max(current)
        I_neg = np.min(current)

        # 对称角度
        # 涌流情况下，负半波是翻转的正半波，角度约为180度
        alpha = np.abs((I_pos - abs(I_neg)) / I_pos) * 90

        # 更精确的对称度计算
        N_half = len(current) // 2
        pos_half = current[:N_half]
        neg_half = -current[N_half:]

        # 相关性
        if len(pos_half) > 0:
            correlation = np.corrcoef(pos_half[:len(neg_half)], neg_half[:len(pos_half)])[0, 1]
        else:
            correlation = 0

        return alpha, correlation

    def calculate_dead_angle(self, current: np.ndarray, threshold: float = 0.1) -> float:
        """计算间断角"""
        I_peak = np.max(np.abs(current))
        I_threshold = I_peak * threshold

        # 找到电流小于阈值的区间
        below = np.abs(current) < I_threshold
        angles = np.where(below)[0] / len(current) * 360

        if len(angles) == 0:
            return 0

        # 计算最大连续间断角
        dead_angles = []
        start = angles[0]
        for i in range(1, len(angles)):
            if angles[i] - angles[i-1] > 5:  # 间断点
                dead_angles.append(angles[i-1] - start)
                start = angles[i]
        dead_angles.append(angles[-1] - start)

        return max(dead_angles) if dead_angles else 0

    def discriminate(self, harmonics: Dict, alpha: float, 
                   dead_angle: float) -> Dict:
        """区分涌流和故障"""
        K_2nd = harmonics.get('2nd', 0)
        K_5th = harmonics.get('5th', 0)

        # 判据1: 二次谐波制动
        is_inrush_h2 = K_2nd > self.K_2nd_th

        # 判据2: 五次谐波
        is_inrush_h5 = K_5th > self.K_5th_th

        # 判据3: 波形对称度
        is_inrush_sym = alpha > self.sym_th

        # 判据4: 间断角
        is_inrush_angle = dead_angle > self.angle_th

        # 综合判断
        inrush_score = sum([is_inrush_h2, is_inrush_h5, is_inrush_sym, is_inrush_angle])

        if inrush_score >= 3:
            result = 'INRUSH'
            confidence = inrush_score / 4
        elif inrush_score == 2:
            if is_inrush_h2 and is_inrush_sym:
                result = 'INRUSH'
                confidence = 0.6
            else:
                result = 'UNCERTAIN'
                confidence = 0.5
        else:
            result = 'FAULT'
            confidence = (4 - inrush_score) / 4

        return {
            'result': result,
            'confidence': confidence,
            'criteria': {
                'harmonic_2nd': K_2nd,
                'harmonic_5th': K_5th,
                'symmetry_deg': alpha,
                'dead_angle_deg': dead_angle,
                'is_inrush_h2': is_inrush_h2,
                'is_inrush_h5': is_inrush_h5,
                'is_inrush_sym': is_inrush_sym,
                'is_inrush_angle': is_inrush_angle
            }
        }

    def run(self, current: np.ndarray, fs: float = 5120.0) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        harmonics = self.extract_harmonics(current, fs)
        alpha, corr = self.calculate_symmetry(current)
        dead_angle = self.calculate_dead_angle(current)

        discrimination = self.discriminate(harmonics, alpha, dead_angle)

        if discrimination['result'] == 'INRUSH':
            action = '判定为励磁涌流，保护闭锁'
        elif discrimination['result'] == 'UNCERTAIN':
            action = '无法明确区分，建议延时动作'
        else:
            action = '判定为内部故障，保护动作'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-18',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'harmonics': harmonics,
            'symmetry_deg': alpha,
            'dead_angle_deg': dead_angle,
            'result': discrimination['result'],
            'confidence': discrimination['confidence'],
            'status': discrimination['result'],
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    discriminator = InrushFaultDiscriminator()
    t = np.linspace(0, 0.1, 512)
    i涌流 = 100 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 50 * t) + 20 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 100 * t)  # 含二次谐波
    result = discriminator.run(i涌流)
    print(f"区分结果: {result['result']}, 置信度: {result['confidence']:.0%}")
    print(f"二次谐波: {result['harmonics'].get('2nd', 0)*100:.1f}%")
    print(f"建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§19 S-19 直流系统绝缘监测

19.1 功能描述

模型名称: 直流系统绝缘监测 英文名称: DC System Insulation Monitoring 技术等级: L2a（需在线校准）

监测直流系统对地绝缘电阻，及时发现接地故障。

19.2 核心算法公式

绝缘电阻计算:
    R_ins = R_known × (U_known / U_f - 1)

平衡电桥法:
    R_pos = R_N × U_pos / U_neg

不平衡度:
    ΔU = |U_pos - U_neg| / U_total × 100%

19.3 参考代码

"""
S-19 直流系统绝缘监测
======================
功能: 监测直流系统绝缘状态
输入: 正负极对地电压, 已知电阻
输出: 绝缘电阻, 故障告警
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Tuple

class DCInsulationMonitor:
    """直流系统绝缘监测模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_voltage: float = 220.0,
                 alarm_resistance: float = 25.0,
                 critical_resistance: float = 10.0):
        """
        参数:
            rated_voltage: 额定电压 (V)
            alarm_resistance: 告警绝缘电阻 (kΩ)
            critical_resistance: 严重绝缘电阻 (kΩ)
        """
        self.U_rated = rated_voltage
        self.R_alarm = alarm_resistance
        self.R_critical = critical_resistance

    def calculate_insulation_resistance(self, U_pos: float, U_neg: float,
                                      R_known: float = 100.0) -> Dict:
        """计算绝缘电阻"""
        # 正极对地电压
        U_pos_measured = U_pos
        # 负极对地电压
        U_neg_measured = U_neg

        # 计算正负极对地电阻
        # 使用分压原理: U_pos = U_total * R_pos / (R_pos + R_neg)

        U_total = U_pos_measured + U_neg_measured

        if abs(U_total) < 1e-6:
            return {'R_pos': np.inf, 'R_neg': np.inf, 'U_total': 0}

        # 正极绝缘电阻
        R_pos = R_known * U_pos_measured / (U_total - U_pos_measured + 1e-6)

        # 负极绝缘电阻
        R_neg = R_known * U_neg_measured / (U_total - U_neg_measured + 1e-6)

        return {
            'R_pos': R_pos,
            'R_neg': R_neg,
            'U_total': U_total
        }

    def calculate_unbalance(self, U_pos: float, U_neg: float) -> float:
        """计算电压不平衡度"""
        U_total = U_pos + U_neg
        if abs(U_total) < 1e-6:
            return 0
        return abs(U_pos - U_neg) / U_total * 100

    def run(self, U_pos: float, U_neg: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        insulation = self.calculate_insulation_resistance(U_pos, U_neg)
        unbalance = self.calculate_unbalance(U_pos, U_neg)

        # 最小绝缘电阻
        R_min = min(insulation['R_pos'], insulation['R_neg'])

        # 状态判断
        if R_min < self.R_critical:
            status = 'CRITICAL'
            action = '绝缘严重下降，定位并处理接地故障'
        elif R_min < self.R_alarm:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '绝缘电阻低于告警值，加强监测'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '直流系统绝缘状态正常'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-19',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'U_pos_V': U_pos,
            'U_neg_V': U_neg,
            'R_pos_kohm': insulation['R_pos'],
            'R_neg_kohm': insulation['R_neg'],
            'R_min_kohm': R_min,
            'unbalance_percent': unbalance,
            'status': status,
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    monitor = DCInsulationMonitor()
    result = monitor.run(U_pos=115.0, U_neg=103.0)
    print(f"正极绝缘: {result['R_pos_kohm']:.1f}kΩ, 负极绝缘: {result['R_neg_kohm']:.1f}kΩ")
    print(f"状态: {result['status']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§20 S-20 雷击过电压波形捕获

20.1 功能描述

模型名称: 雷击过电压波形捕获 英文名称: Lightning Overvoltage Waveform Capture 技术等级: L3（需专用波形记录）

捕获和分析雷击过电压波形，评估防雷保护效果。

20.2 核心算法公式

波形参数提取:
    U_peak = max(|u(t)|)
    t_front = t_90% - t_10%
    t_tail = t_50%_falling

雷击类型识别:
    直击雷: U_peak > 10kV, t_front < 10μs
    感应雷: U_peak < 10kV, t_front > 10μs

能量计算:
    W = ∫ u²(t) dt / R

20.3 参考代码

"""
S-20 雷击过电压波形捕获
=======================
功能: 捕获和分析雷击波形
输入: 过电压波形数据
输出: 波形参数, 雷击类型, 能级评估
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Tuple, List

class LightningWaveformCapture:
    """雷击过电压波形捕获模型"""

    def __init__(self, fs: float = 1e6):
        self.fs = fs

    def extract_waveform_parameters(self, voltage: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """提取波形参数"""
        U_peak = np.max(np.abs(voltage))
        peak_idx = np.argmax(np.abs(voltage))

        # 找到过零点
        above_10 = np.where(np.abs(voltage) > 0.1 * U_peak)[0]
        if len(above_10) > 0:
            t_start = above_10[0] / self.fs
            t_end = above_10[-1] / self.fs
            duration = t_end - t_start
        else:
            duration = 0

        return {
            'U_peak': U_peak,
            'peak_idx': peak_idx,
            'duration_s': duration
        }

    def classify_lightning_type(self, params: Dict) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """雷击类型识别"""
        if params['U_peak'] > 10000:
            return 'DIRECT', '直击雷'
        elif params['duration_s'] > 1e-5:
            return 'INDUCED', '感应雷'
        else:
            return 'SWITCHING', '操作过电压'

    def run(self, voltage: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        params = self.extract_waveform_parameters(voltage)
        lightning_type, type_desc = self.classify_lightning_type(params)

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-20',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'U_peak_V': params['U_peak'],
            'duration_us': params['duration_s'] * 1e6,
            'lightning_type': lightning_type,
            'type_desc': type_desc,
            'status': 'CAPTURED'
        }


def example_usage():
    capture = LightningWaveformCapture()
    # 模拟雷击波形
    t = np.linspace(0, 0.001, 1000)
    voltage = 50000 * np.exp(-t / 0.0001) * (t > 0)
    result = capture.run(voltage)
    print(f"雷击类型: {result['type_desc']}, 峰值: {result['U_peak_V']/1000:.0f}kV")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§21 S-21 剩余电流保护器(RCD)状态自检

21.1 功能描述

模型名称: 剩余电流保护器(RCD)状态自检 英文名称: RCD Self-Test Status Monitoring 技术等级: L1（纯计算模型）

对RCD进行自检测试，评估其动作可靠性。

21.2 核心算法公式

剩余电流计算:
    I_Δn = |I_L1 + I_L2 + ... + I_Ln|

动作时间测试:
    t_trip = t_RCD_trip - t_test_injection

定期自检周期:
    T_self-test = 报警周期 - 提前预警时间

21.3 参考代码

"""
S-21 剩余电流保护器状态自检
===========================
功能: RCD自检和状态监测
输入: 泄漏电流, 测试电流注入
输出: 自检结果, 状态评估
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

class RCDSelfTestMonitor:
    """RCD状态自检模型"""

    def __init__(self,
                 rated_i_delta: float = 30.0,
                 max_trip_time: float = 0.3,
                 test_interval_days: int = 30):
        """
        参数:
            rated_i_delta: 额定剩余动作电流 (mA)
            max_trip_time: 最大分断时间 (s)
            test_interval_days: 建议测试周期 (天)
        """
        self.I_delta_rated = rated_i_delta
        self.t_max = max_trip_time
        self.test_interval = timedelta(days=test_interval_days)

    def calculate_residual_current(self, currents: list) -> float:
        """计算剩余电流"""
        return abs(sum(currents))

    def check_trip_time(self, t_trip: float) -> Dict:
        """检查分断时间"""
        if t_trip < self.t_max:
            return {'ok': True, 't_trip': t_trip, 'margin': (self.t_max - t_trip) / self.t_max}
        else:
            return {'ok': False, 't_trip': t_trip, 'exceeded': t_trip - self.t_max}

    def run(self, I_L1: float, I_L2: float, I_L3: float, I_N: float = 0,
           last_test_date: datetime = None) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        I_delta = self.calculate_residual_current([I_L1, I_L2, I_L3, I_N])

        # 判断是否超过额定值
        if I_delta > self.I_delta_rated:
            status = 'TRIP_REQUIRED'
            action = f'剩余电流{I_delta:.1f}mA超过额定值，应立即跳闸'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '剩余电流正常'

        # 自检周期检查
        need_test = False
        if last_test_date:
            days_since_test = (datetime.now() - last_test_date).days
            need_test = days_since_test >= self.test_interval.days

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-21',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'residual_current_mA': I_delta,
            'rated_current_mA': self.I_delta_rated,
            'status': status,
            'need_self_test': need_test,
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    monitor = RCDSelfTestMonitor()
    result = monitor.run(I_L1=10.0, I_L2=9.5, I_L3=0.0, I_N=20.0)
    print(f"剩余电流: {result['residual_current_mA']:.1f}mA")
    print(f"状态: {result['status']}")
    print(f"建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§22 S-22 电气火灾风险综合评级

22.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电气火灾风险综合评级 英文名称: Electrical Fire Risk Comprehensive Rating 技术等级: L2a（需多源数据融合）

综合评估电气系统火灾风险，给出风险等级和建议。

22.2 核心算法公式

风险指数:
    R = Σ(w_i × F_i)

风险因素:
    F_过载 = P / P_rated
    F_温升 = (T - T_ambient) / (T_max - T_ambient)
    F_老化 = 1 - R_ins / R_initial
    F_谐波 = THD / THD_limit

风险等级:
    R < 0.3: 低风险
    R < 0.6: 中风险
    R < 0.8: 高风险
    R >= 0.8: 极高风险

22.3 参考代码

"""
S-22 电气火灾风险综合评级
=========================
功能: 综合评估电气火灾风险
输入: 多维度电气参数
输出: 风险等级, 风险因素分析
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class FireRiskAssessor:
    """电气火灾风险综合评级模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        # 权重配置
        self.weights = {
            'overload': 0.25,
            'temperature': 0.25,
            'aging': 0.20,
            'harmonic': 0.15,
            'unbalance': 0.10,
            'leakage': 0.05
        }

    def calculate_overload_factor(self, P: float, P_rated: float) -> float:
        """过载风险因子"""
        return min(1.0, P / P_rated)

    def calculate_temperature_factor(self, T: float, T_amb: float, T_max: float) -> float:
        """温升风险因子"""
        if T_max <= T_amb:
            return 0
        return min(1.0, (T - T_amb) / (T_max - T_amb))

    def calculate_aging_factor(self, R_ins: float, R_init: float) -> float:
        """老化风险因子"""
        return min(1.0, 1 - R_ins / R_init)

    def calculate_harmonic_factor(self, THD: float, THD_limit: float = 0.30) -> float:
        """谐波风险因子"""
        return min(1.0, THD / THD_limit)

    def calculate_unbalance_factor(self, ub_percent: float) -> float:
        """不平衡风险因子"""
        return min(1.0, ub_percent / 10)

    def calculate_leakage_factor(self, I_leak: float, I_rated: float) -> float:
        """泄漏电流风险因子"""
        return min(1.0, I_leak / (0.001 * I_rated))

    def run(self, P: float, P_rated: float, T: float, T_amb: float,
           R_ins: float, R_init: float, THD: float,
           unbalance: float, I_leak: float, I_rated: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        # 计算各风险因子
        F_overload = self.calculate_overload_factor(P, P_rated)
        F_temp = self.calculate_temperature_factor(T, T_amb, T_max=120)
        F_aging = self.calculate_aging_factor(R_ins, R_init)
        F_harmonic = self.calculate_harmonic_factor(THD)
        F_unbalance = self.calculate_unbalance_factor(unbalance)
        F_leakage = self.calculate_leakage_factor(I_leak, I_rated)

        # 综合风险指数
        risk_index = (self.weights['overload'] * F_overload +
                     self.weights['temperature'] * F_temp +
                     self.weights['aging'] * F_aging +
                     self.weights['harmonic'] * F_harmonic +
                     self.weights['unbalance'] * F_unbalance +
                     self.weights['leakage'] * F_leakage)

        # 风险等级
        if risk_index >= 0.8:
            level = 'CRITICAL'
            action = '极高火灾风险，立即采取防范措施'
        elif risk_index >= 0.6:
            level = 'HIGH'
            action = '高火灾风险，加强监测和巡检'
        elif risk_index >= 0.3:
            level = 'MEDIUM'
            action = '中等风险，持续关注'
        else:
            level = 'LOW'
            action = '火灾风险较低'

        return {
            'model_id': 'S-22',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'risk_index': risk_index,
            'risk_level': level,
            'factors': {
                'overload': F_overload,
                'temperature': F_temp,
                'aging': F_aging,
                'harmonic': F_harmonic,
                'unbalance': F_unbalance,
                'leakage': F_leakage
            },
            'status': level,
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    assessor = FireRiskAssessor()
    result = assessor.run(
        P=85.0, P_rated=100.0,
        T=95.0, T_amb=35.0,
        R_ins=500.0, R_init=1000.0,
        THD=0.18,
        unbalance=3.5,
        I_leak=50.0, I_rated=100.0
    )
    print(f"火灾风险指数: {result['risk_index']:.2f}")
    print(f"风险等级: {result['risk_level']}")
    print(f"建议: {result['action']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

Q系列 - 电能质量 (18个模型)

§23 Q-01 谐波源责任划分

23.1 功能描述

模型名称: 谐波源责任划分 英文名称: Harmonic Source Responsibility Allocation 技术等级: L1（纯计算模型）

基于谐波电流方向和阻抗分离，划分用户与电网的谐波责任。

23.2 核心算法公式

谐波功率方向:
    P_h = 3 × U_h × I_h × cos(φ_h)

责任划分:
    Share_user = I_user_h / (I_user_h + I_grid_h) × 100%

阻抗分离:
    Z_user = (U_h - U_grid_h) / I_user_h

23.3 参考代码

"""
Q-01 谐波源责任划分
==================
功能: 划分谐波责任
输入: 谐波电压, 谐波电流, 相位角
输出: 责任比例
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class HarmonicResponsibilityAllocator:
    """谐波源责任划分模型"""

    def __init__(self, harmonic_orders: List[int] = None):
        self.harmonic_orders = harmonic_orders or [3, 5, 7, 11, 13, 17, 19]

    def calculate_harmonic_power(self, U_h: float, I_h: float, phi_h: float) -> float:
        """计算谐波功率"""
        return 3 * U_h * I_h * np.cos(np.deg2rad(phi_h))

    def allocate_responsibility(self, I_user: List[float], I_grid: List[float]) -> Dict:
        """责任划分"""
        total_user = sum(I_user)
        total_grid = sum(I_grid)
        total = total_user + total_grid

        if total < 1e-9:
            return {'user_share': 0, 'grid_share': 0}

        return {
            'user_share_percent': total_user / total * 100,
            'grid_share_percent': total_grid / total * 100
        }

    def run(self, harmonic_data: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        results = []
        for h, data in enumerate(harmonic_data):
            I_user = data.get('I_user', 0)
            I_grid = data.get('I_grid', 0)
            allocation = self.allocate_responsibility([I_user], [I_grid])
            results.append({
                'order': self.harmonic_orders[h],
                'I_user': I_user,
                'I_grid': I_grid,
                **allocation
            })

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-01',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'harmonic_details': results
        }


def example_usage():
    allocator = HarmonicResponsibilityAllocator()
    data = [{'I_user': 10, 'I_grid': 5}, {'I_user': 8, 'I_grid': 12}]
    result = allocator.run(data)
    print(f"3次谐波用户责任: {result['harmonic_details'][0]['user_share_percent']:.1f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§24 Q-02 并联谐振预警

24.1 功能描述

模型名称: 并联谐振预警 英文名称: Parallel Resonance Warning 技术等级: L2a

预测并联谐振发生的可能性和频率。

24.2 核心算法公式

并联谐振频率:
    f_r = 1 / (2π × √(L × C))

品质因数:
    Q = R × √(C/L)

谐振阻抗:
    Z_r = Q² × R

24.3 参考代码

"""
Q-02 并联谐振预警
=================
功能: 预测并联谐振
输入: 系统参数, 谐波电流
输出: 谐振频率, 风险等级
"""
