import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class ParallelResonancePredictor:
    """并联谐振预警模型"""

    def __init__(self, L: float = 0.001, C: float = 0.001, R: float = 10.0):
        self.L = L
        self.C = C
        self.R = R

    def calculate_resonance_frequency(self) -> float:
        """计算谐振频率"""
        return 1 / (2 * np.pi * np.sqrt(self.L * self.C))

    def calculate_Q_factor(self) -> float:
        """计算品质因数"""
        return self.R * np.sqrt(self.C / self.L)

    def check_resonance_risk(self, f_harmonic: float, f_resonance: float, Q: float) -> Dict:
        """评估谐振风险"""
        frequency_ratio = f_harmonic / f_resonance
        is_near = abs(frequency_ratio - 1) < 0.1

        return {
            'near_resonance': is_near,
            'frequency_ratio': frequency_ratio,
            'risk_level': 'HIGH' if is_near else 'LOW'
        }

    def run(self, harmonic_currents: Dict[int, float]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        f_resonance = self.calculate_resonance_frequency()
        Q = self.calculate_Q_factor()

        risks = []
        for h, I_h in harmonic_currents.items():
            f_h = h * 50
            risk = self.check_resonance_risk(f_h, f_resonance, Q)
            if risk['near_resonance']:
                risks.append({'harmonic': h, 'I': I_h, **risk})

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-02',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'resonance_frequency_Hz': f_resonance,
            'Q_factor': Q,
            'risks': risks,
            'status': 'WARNING' if risks else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    predictor = ParallelResonancePredictor(L=0.001, C=0.001, R=10)
    result = predictor.run({5: 20, 7: 15, 11: 10})
    print(f"谐振频率: {result['resonance_frequency_Hz']:.1f}Hz")
    print(f"Q值: {result['Q_factor']:.1f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§25 Q-03 串联谐振预警

25.1 功能描述

模型名称: 串联谐振预警 英文名称: Series Resonance Warning 技术等级: L2a

预测串联谐振发生的可能性。

25.2 核心算法公式

串联谐振频率:
    f_r = 1 / (2π × √(L × C))

串联阻抗:
    Z_r = R

25.3 参考代码

"""
Q-03 串联谐振预警
=================
功能: 预测串联谐振
输入: 系统参数
输出: 谐振频率, 风险评估
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class SeriesResonancePredictor:
    """串联谐振预警模型"""

    def __init__(self, L: float = 0.001, C: float = 0.001):
        self.L = L
        self.C = C

    def calculate_resonance_frequency(self) -> float:
        """计算谐振频率"""
        return 1 / (2 * np.pi * np.sqrt(self.L * self.C))

    def run(self, harmonic_voltages: Dict[int, float]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        f_resonance = self.calculate_resonance_frequency()

        risks = []
        for h, U_h in harmonic_voltages.items():
            f_h = h * 50
            if abs(f_h - f_resonance) < 20:
                risks.append({'harmonic': h, 'U': U_h})

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-03',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'resonance_frequency_Hz': f_resonance,
            'risks': risks,
            'status': 'WARNING' if risks else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    predictor = SeriesResonancePredictor()
    result = predictor.run({5: 50, 7: 30})
    print(f"串联谐振频率: {result['resonance_frequency_Hz']:.1f}Hz")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§26 Q-04 闪变源识别与定位

26.1 功能描述

模型名称: 闪变源识别与定位 英文名称: Flicker Source Identification and Location 技术等级: L2b

识别和定位电网中的闪变源。

26.2 核心算法公式

短时闪变值:
    P_st = 0.0324 × S^0.3 × F^0.8

闪变传递系数:
    T_f = ΔU_flicker / U_nominal × 100%

26.3 参考代码

"""
Q-04 闪变源识别与定位
=====================
功能: 识别闪变源
输入: 电压波动数据
输出: 闪变源位置, 贡献度
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class FlickerSourceIdentifier:
    """闪变源识别模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.flicker_threshold = 0.5

    def calculate_pst(self, voltage_fluctuation: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """计算短时闪变值"""
        S = np.std(voltage_fluctuation) * 100
        F = 1.0  # 简化假设
        return 0.0324 * (S ** 0.3) * (F ** 0.8)

    def run(self, measurement_points: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        pst_values = []
        for point in measurement_points:
            pst = self.calculate_pst(point.get('fluctuation', []))
            pst_values.append({
                'point_id': point.get('id'),
                'pst': pst,
                'contribution': pst
            })

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-04',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'measurement_points': pst_values,
            'max_pst': max([p['pst'] for p in pst_values]) if pst_values else 0,
            'status': 'WARNING' if any(p['pst'] > self.flicker_threshold for p in pst_values) else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    identifier = FlickerSourceIdentifier()
    points = [{'id': 'P1', 'fluctuation': np.random.randn(100) * 0.5}]
    result = identifier.run(points)
    print(f"最大Pst: {result['max_pst']:.2f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§27 Q-05 电压暂降原因分类

27.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电压暂降原因分类 英文名称: Voltage Sag Cause Classification 技术等级: L2b

基于暂降波形特征分类暂降原因（短路故障、电机启动、变压器励磁等）。

27.2 核心算法公式

暂降深度:
    Depth = (U_nominal - U_min) / U_nominal × 100%

持续时间:
    T_duration = t_end - t_start

残余电压:
    U_residual = U_min / U_nominal

27.3 参考代码

"""
Q-05 电压暂降原因分类
=====================
功能: 分类暂降原因
输入: 暂降事件数据
输出: 暂降类型, 置信度
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class VoltageSagClassifier:
    """电压暂降原因分类模型"""

    def classify(self, depth: float, duration: float, 
                 has_phase_shift: bool) -> Dict:
        """分类暂降原因"""
        # 规则判断
        if depth > 0.6 and duration < 0.1:
            cause = 'SHORT_CIRCUIT'
            confidence = 0.9
        elif depth < 0.3 and duration > 1.0:
            cause = 'MOTOR_START'
            confidence = 0.85
        elif depth < 0.2 and duration < 0.5:
            cause = 'TRANSFORMER_INRUSH'
            confidence = 0.8
        elif has_phase_shift:
            cause = 'DISTANCE_FAULT'
            confidence = 0.75
        else:
            cause = 'UNKNOWN'
            confidence = 0.5

        return {'cause': cause, 'confidence': confidence}

    def run(self, U_min: float, U_nominal: float, t_start: float, 
           t_end: float, phase_shift: bool = False) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        depth = (U_nominal - U_min) / U_nominal
        duration = t_end - t_start

        classification = self.classify(depth, duration, phase_shift)

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-05',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'depth_percent': depth * 100,
            'duration_s': duration,
            'cause': classification['cause'],
            'confidence': classification['confidence']
        }


def example_usage():
    classifier = VoltageSagClassifier()
    result = classifier.run(U_min=100, U_nominal=220, t_start=0, t_end=0.05)
    print(f"暂降类型: {result['cause']}, 置信度: {result['confidence']:.0%}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§28 Q-06 三相不平衡溯源

28.1 功能描述

模型名称: 三相不平衡溯源 英文名称: Three-Phase Unbalance Source Tracing 技术等级: L2a

识别三相不平衡的来源（电网侧或用户侧）。

28.2 核心算法公式

负序电流责任:
    I_2_user = |I_2_pointA - I_2_pointB|
    Share_user = I_2_user / I_2_total × 100%

28.3 参考代码

"""
Q-06 三相不平衡溯源
===================
功能: 溯源三相不平衡
输入: 负序电流数据
输出: 不平衡来源, 责任比例
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class UnbalanceSourceTracer:
    """三相不平衡溯源模型"""

    def run(self, I_2_upstream: float, I_2_downstream: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        I_2_total = abs(I_2_upstream) + abs(I_2_downstream)

        if I_2_total < 1e-9:
            return {
                'model_id': 'Q-06',
                'status': 'NORMAL',
                'unbalance_level': 0
            }

        user_share = abs(I_2_downstream) / I_2_total * 100
        grid_share = abs(I_2_upstream) / I_2_total * 100

        # 计算不平衡度
        unbalance_level = abs(I_2_upstream) / 100 * 100

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-06',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'user_share_percent': user_share,
            'grid_share_percent': grid_share,
            'main_source': 'USER' if user_share > grid_share else 'GRID',
            'unbalance_level_percent': unbalance_level,
            'status': 'WARNING' if unbalance_level > 2 else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    tracer = UnbalanceSourceTracer()
    result = tracer.run(I_2_upstream=5.0, I_2_downstream=3.0)
    print(f"用户责任: {result['user_share_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"主要来源: {result['main_source']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§29 Q-07 频率稳定性评估

29.1 功能描述

模型名称: 频率稳定性评估 英文名称: Frequency Stability Assessment 技术等级: L1

评估电网频率稳定性。

29.2 核心算法公式

频率偏差:
    Δf = f_measured - f_nominal

频率变化率:
    RoCoF = df/dt

稳定指数:
    S_f = 1 / (1 + |Δf| / f_nominal)

29.3 参考代码

"""
Q-07 频率稳定性评估
===================
功能: 评估频率稳定性
输入: 频率数据
输出: 稳定指数, 风险等级
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class FrequencyStabilityAssessor:
    """频率稳定性评估模型"""

    def __init__(self, f_nominal: float = 50.0, f_max_dev: float = 0.5):
        self.f_nom = f_nominal
        self.f_max_dev = f_max_dev

    def calculate_rocof(self, f_history: list, dt: float = 1.0) -> float:
        """计算频率变化率"""
        if len(f_history) < 2:
            return 0
        return (f_history[-1] - f_history[0]) / dt / len(f_history)

    def calculate_stability_index(self, f: float) -> float:
        """计算稳定指数"""
        delta_f = abs(f - self.f_nom)
        return 1 / (1 + delta_f / self.f_nom)

    def run(self, f_current: float, f_history: list = None) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        stability = self.calculate_stability_index(f_current)
        rocof = self.calculate_rocof(f_history) if f_history else 0

        if abs(f_current - self.f_nom) > self.f_max_dev:
            status = 'UNSTABLE'
        elif stability > 0.99:
            status = 'STABLE'
        else:
            status = 'MARGINAL'

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-07',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'frequency_Hz': f_current,
            'stability_index': stability,
            'rocof_Hz_s': rocof,
            'status': status
        }


def example_usage():
    assessor = FrequencyStabilityAssessor()
    result = assessor.run(f_current=50.02, f_history=[50.0, 50.01, 50.02])
    print(f"频率: {result['frequency_Hz']:.2f}Hz")
    print(f"稳定指数: {result['stability_index']:.4f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§30 Q-08 间谐波/次同步振荡监测

30.1 功能描述

模型名称: 间谐波/次同步振荡监测 英文名称: Interharmonic / Subsynchronous Oscillation Monitoring 技术等级: L2a

监测间谐波和次同步振荡。

30.2 核心算法公式

间谐波含量:
    I_ih = √(Σ I²_h+0.5) / I_fund × 100%

次同步振荡频率:
    f_sso = f_n - m × f_s

振荡阻尼:
    ζ = -ln(A_2 / A_1) / (2π × n)

30.3 参考代码

"""
Q-08 间谐波/次同步振荡监测
==========================
功能: 监测间谐波和次同步振荡
输入: 电流/电压频谱
输出: 振荡频率, 阻尼比
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class SSOMonitor:
    """次同步振荡监测模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.sso_band = (10, 45)  # 次同步频率范围

    def detect_interharmonics(self, spectrum: Dict[float, float]) -> List[Dict]:
        """检测间谐波"""
        interharmonics = []
        fundamental_freq = 50.0

        for freq, amplitude in spectrum.items():
            # 检查是否为间谐波（非整数倍）
            ratio = freq / fundamental_freq
            if ratio > 1 and abs(ratio - round(ratio)) > 0.1:
                interharmonics.append({'frequency': freq, 'amplitude': amplitude})

        return interharmonics

    def detect_sso(self, frequency_band: np.ndarray, 
                   amplitude_band: np.ndarray, n_cycles: int = 5) -> Dict:
        """检测次同步振荡"""
        if len(frequency_band) < 2:
            return {'detected': False, 'frequency': 0, 'damping_ratio': 0}

        # 计算阻尼比
        peaks = np.where((amplitude_band[1:-1] > amplitude_band[:-2]) & 
                        (amplitude_band[1:-1] > amplitude_band[2:]))[0] + 1

        if len(peaks) >= 2:
            A1 = amplitude_band[peaks[0]]
            A2 = amplitude_band[peaks[1]]
            if A1 > 0 and A2 > 0:
                damping = -np.log(A2 / A1) / (2 * np.pi * n_cycles)
            else:
                damping = 0
        else:
            damping = 0

        f_sso = np.mean(frequency_band)

        return {
            'detected': abs(damping) < 0.1 and np.max(amplitude_band) > 0.01,
            'frequency': f_sso,
            'damping_ratio': damping
        }

    def run(self, spectrum: Dict[float, float]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        interharmonics = self.detect_interharmonics(spectrum)

        # 简化的SSO检测
        sso_band_freqs = [f for f in spectrum.keys() if 10 < f < 45]
        sso_amps = [spectrum[f] for f in sso_band_freqs]

        sso_result = self.detect_sso(
            np.array(sso_band_freqs) if sso_band_freqs else np.array([0]),
            np.array(sso_amps) if sso_amps else np.array([0])
        )

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-08',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'interharmonics': interharmonics,
            'sso_detected': sso_result['detected'],
            'sso_frequency_Hz': sso_result['frequency'],
            'damping_ratio': sso_result['damping_ratio'],
            'status': 'WARNING' if sso_result['detected'] or len(interharmonics) > 3 else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    monitor = SSOMonitor()
    spectrum = {50: 100, 75: 2, 100: 3, 150: 2, 25: 5, 30: 8}  # 含间谐波和次同步
    result = monitor.run(spectrum)
    print(f"间谐波数: {len(result['interharmonics'])}")
    print(f"SSO检测: {result['sso_detected']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§31 Q-09 电压偏差与无功关系模型

31.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电压偏差与无功关系模型 英文名称: Voltage Deviation and Reactive Power Relationship 技术等级: L1

建立电压偏差与无功功率的关系模型。

31.2 核心算法公式

电压偏差:
    ΔU = U - U_nominal
    ΔU% = (U - U_nom) / U_nom × 100%

无功补偿量:
    Q_c = ΔU × U² / X

电压调节灵敏度:
    K_uv = ΔU / ΔQ

31.3 参考代码

"""
Q-09 电压偏差与无功关系模型
===========================
功能: 分析电压与无功的关系
输入: 电压, 无功功率
输出: 电压偏差, 灵敏度
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class VoltageReactivePowerModel:
    """电压-无功关系模型"""

    def __init__(self, U_nominal: float = 220.0, X: float = 10.0):
        self.U_nom = U_nominal
        self.X = X

    def calculate_voltage_deviation(self, U: float) -> Dict:
        """计算电压偏差"""
        delta_U = U - self.U_nom
        delta_U_percent = delta_U / self.U_nom * 100
        return {'delta_U_V': delta_U, 'delta_U_percent': delta_U_percent}

    def calculate_q_compensation(self, delta_U: float) -> float:
        """计算所需补偿无功"""
        return delta_U * self.U_nom ** 2 / self.X

    def calculate_sensitivity(self, U_history: list, Q_history: list) -> float:
        """计算电压调节灵敏度"""
        if len(U_history) < 2:
            return 0
        dU = np.mean(np.diff(U_history))
        dQ = np.mean(np.diff(Q_history))
        return dU / dQ if abs(dQ) > 1e-9 else 0

    def run(self, U: float, Q: float = 0, U_history: list = None, 
           Q_history: list = None) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        deviation = self.calculate_voltage_deviation(U)
        sensitivity = self.calculate_sensitivity(U_history or [U], 
                                                Q_history or [Q])

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-09',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'voltage_V': U,
            'voltage_deviation_V': deviation['delta_U_V'],
            'voltage_deviation_percent': deviation['delta_U_percent'],
            'sensitivity_kV_Mvar': sensitivity,
            'status': 'WARNING' if abs(deviation['delta_U_percent']) > 10 else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    model = VoltageReactivePowerModel()
    result = model.run(U=235, U_history=[220, 230, 235], Q_history=[0, 5, 10])
    print(f"电压偏差: {result['voltage_deviation_percent']:.2f}%")
    print(f"灵敏度: {result['sensitivity_kV_Mvar']:.4f}kV/Mvar")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§32 Q-10 分布式电源对电压影响量化

32.1 功能描述

模型名称: 分布式电源对电压影响量化 英文名称: DG Impact on Voltage Quantification 技术等级: L2a

量化分析分布式电源对配电网电压的影响。

32.2 核心算法公式

电压抬升:
    ΔU = P_DG × R + Q_DG × X / U

电压越限概率:
    P_over = P(U > U_max)

32.3 参考代码

"""
Q-10 分布式电源对电压影响量化
==============================
功能: 量化DG对电压的影响
输入: DG功率, 位置, 网络参数
输出: 电压影响, 越限风险
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class DGImpactQuantifier:
    """分布式电源影响量化模型"""

    def __init__(self, R: float = 0.5, X: float = 0.3, U_base: float = 220.0):
        self.R = R
        self.X = X
        self.U_base = U_base

    def calculate_voltage_rise(self, P_dg: float, Q_dg: float) -> float:
        """计算电压抬升"""
        return (P_dg * self.R + Q_dg * self.X) / self.U_base

    def run(self, P_dg: float, Q_dg: float, U_base: float = None,
           U_max: float = 242.0) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        voltage_rise = self.calculate_voltage_rise(P_dg, Q_dg)
        U_new = (U_base or self.U_base) + voltage_rise
        over_limit = U_new > U_max

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-10',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'voltage_rise_V': voltage_rise,
            'new_voltage_V': U_new,
            'over_limit': over_limit,
            'status': 'WARNING' if over_limit else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    quantifier = DGImpactQuantifier()
    result = quantifier.run(P_dg=50, Q_dg=20)
    print(f"电压抬升: {result['voltage_rise_V']:.2f}V")
    print(f"越限: {result['over_limit']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§33 Q-11 冲击性负荷扰动评估

33.1 功能描述

模型名称: 冲击性负荷扰动评估 英文名称: Impact Load Disturbance Assessment 技术等级: L2a

评估冲击性负荷对电网的影响。

33.2 核心算法公式

冲击功率:
    P_impact = max(P) - min(P_avg)

电压波动率:
    d = ΔU_max / U_nom × 100%

33.3 参考代码

"""
Q-11 冲击性负荷扰动评估
========================
功能: 评估冲击负荷影响
输入: 功率/电压数据
输出: 扰动程度, 风险等级
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class ImpactLoadAssessor:
    """冲击负荷评估模型"""

    def __init__(self, U_nominal: float = 220.0):
        self.U_nom = U_nominal

    def calculate_impact_power(self, P_history: list) -> float:
        """计算冲击功率"""
        P_max = max(P_history)
        P_avg = np.mean(P_history[:len(P_history)//3])
        return P_max - P_avg

    def calculate_voltage_fluctuation(self, U_history: list) -> float:
        """计算电压波动率"""
        dU = max(U_history) - min(U_history)
        return dU / self.U_nom * 100

    def run(self, P_history: list, U_history: list) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        impact_power = self.calculate_impact_power(P_history)
        voltage_fluct = self.calculate_voltage_fluctuation(U_history)

        severity = 'HIGH' if voltage_fluct > 5 else 'MEDIUM' if voltage_fluct > 2 else 'LOW'

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-11',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'impact_power_kW': impact_power,
            'voltage_fluctuation_percent': voltage_fluct,
            'severity': severity,
            'status': 'WARNING' if severity != 'LOW' else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    assessor = ImpactLoadAssessor()
    P = [100, 100, 100, 200, 180, 120, 100]
    U = [220, 220, 220, 215, 218, 220, 220]
    result = assessor.run(P, U)
    print(f"冲击功率: {result['impact_power_kW']:.1f}kW")
    print(f"电压波动: {result['voltage_fluctuation_percent']:.2f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§34 Q-12 三相电压不平衡度趋势

34.1 功能描述

模型名称: 三相电压不平衡度趋势 英文名称: Three-Phase Voltage Unbalance Trend 技术等级: L1

监测三相电压不平衡度变化趋势。

34.2 核心算法公式

电压不平衡度:
    ε_U2 = U_2 / U_1 × 100%

趋势斜率:
    k = Σ(t_i - t_avg)(ε_i - ε_avg) / Σ(t_i - t_avg)²

34.3 参考代码

"""
Q-12 三相电压不平衡度趋势
==========================
功能: 监测不平衡度趋势
输入: 三相电压
输出: 不平衡度, 趋势
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class VoltageUnbalanceTrendAnalyzer:
    """电压不平衡趋势分析模型"""

    def calculate_unbalance(self, U_a: float, U_b: float, U_c: float) -> float:
        """计算不平衡度"""
        U_avg = (U_a + U_b + U_c) / 3
        if U_avg < 1e-9:
            return 0

        delta_U = [U_a - U_avg, U_b - U_avg, U_c - U_avg]
        U_2 = np.sqrt(np.mean([d**2 for d in delta_U]))

        return U_2 / U_avg * 100

    def calculate_trend(self, unbalance_history: List[float], 
                        time_history: List[float]) -> float:
        """计算趋势斜率"""
        if len(unbalance_history) < 2:
            return 0

        n = len(unbalance_history)
        t_arr = np.array(time_history) - time_history[0]
        u_arr = np.array(unbalance_history)

        t_mean = np.mean(t_arr)
        u_mean = np.mean(u_arr)

        numerator = np.sum((t_arr - t_mean) * (u_arr - u_mean))
        denominator = np.sum((t_arr - t_mean) ** 2)

        return numerator / denominator if denominator > 1e-9 else 0

    def run(self, U_a: float, U_b: float, U_c: float,
           unbalance_history: List[float] = None,
           time_history: List[float] = None) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        unbalance = self.calculate_unbalance(U_a, U_b, U_c)
        trend = self.calculate_trend(unbalance_history or [unbalance],
                                   time_history or [0])

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-12',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'unbalance_percent': unbalance,
            'trend_per_hour': trend,
            'status': 'WARNING' if unbalance > 2 else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = VoltageUnbalanceTrendAnalyzer()
    result = analyzer.run(220, 215, 210, [1.5, 1.8, 2.0, 2.3], [0, 1, 2, 3])
    print(f"当前不平衡度: {result['unbalance_percent']:.2f}%")
    print(f"趋势: {result['trend_per_hour']:.3f}%/h")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§35 Q-13 微电网并离网切换质量

35.1 功能描述

模型名称: 微电网并离网切换质量 英文名称: Microgrid Islanding/Grid-Connected Transition Quality 技术等级: L2b

评估微电网并离网切换过程的电能质量。

35.2 核心算法公式

切换暂降深度:
    Depth = U_before / U_after - 1

切换时间:
    t_switch = t_deadend - t_trigger

35.3 参考代码

"""
Q-13 微电网并离网切换质量
==========================
功能: 评估切换质量
输入: 切换前后电压/频率数据
输出: 切换质量指数
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class TransitionQualityAssessor:
    """切换质量评估模型"""

    def __init__(self, U_nominal: float = 220.0, f_nominal: float = 50.0):
        self.U_nom = U_nominal
        self.f_nom = f_nominal

    def calculate_quality_index(self, U_before: float, U_after: float,
                               t_switch: float) -> float:
        """计算切换质量指数"""
        voltage_drop = abs(U_before - U_after) / self.U_nom
        time_penalty = min(t_switch / 0.02, 1)  # 20ms为基准

        quality = 1 - 0.5 * voltage_drop - 0.5 * time_penalty
        return max(0, min(1, quality))

    def run(self, U_before: float, U_after: float, t_switch: float,
           mode: str = 'island') -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        quality = self.calculate_quality_index(U_before, U_after, t_switch)

        level = 'EXCELLENT' if quality > 0.9 else 'GOOD' if quality > 0.7 else 'POOR'

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-13',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'transition_mode': mode,
            'quality_index': quality,
            'quality_level': level,
            'status': 'NORMAL' if quality > 0.7 else 'WARNING'
        }


def example_usage():
    assessor = TransitionQualityAssessor()
    result = assessor.run(U_before=220, U_after=215, t_switch=0.01, mode='island')
    print(f"切换质量指数: {result['quality_index']:.2f}")
    print(f"质量等级: {result['quality_level']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§36 Q-14 多逆变器并联谐振抑制

36.1 功能描述

模型名称: 多逆变器并联谐振抑制 英文名称: Multi-Inverter Parallel Resonance Suppression 技术等级: L2b

分析和抑制多逆变器并联运行时的谐振。

36.2 核心算法公式

等效谐振频率:
    f_r = 1 / (2π × √(L_eq × C_eq))

谐振阻抗:
    Z_r = Q_r × R_eq

36.3 参考代码

"""
Q-14 多逆变器并联谐振抑制
==========================
功能: 分析和抑制谐振
输入: 逆变器参数
输出: 谐振频率, 抑制建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class ResonanceSuppressionAdvisor:
    """谐振抑制建议模型"""

    def __init__(self, n_inverters: int = 4):
        self.n_inverters = n_inverters

    def calculate_equivalent_parameters(self, L_list: List[float], 
                                      C_list: List[float]) -> Dict:
        """计算等效参数"""
        L_eq = 1 / sum([1/l for l in L_list]) if all(l > 0 for l in L_list) else 0
        C_eq = sum(C_list)

        if L_eq * C_eq > 0:
            f_r = 1 / (2 * np.pi * np.sqrt(L_eq * C_eq))
        else:
            f_r = 0

        return {'L_eq': L_eq, 'C_eq': C_eq, 'f_resonance': f_r}

    def run(self, L_list: List[float], C_list: List[float],
           harmonic_currents: Dict[int, float]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        params = self.calculate_equivalent_parameters(L_list, C_list)

        # 检查谐振风险
        risks = []
        for h, I_h in harmonic_currents.items():
            f_h = h * 50
            if abs(f_h - params['f_resonance']) < 20:
                risks.append({'harmonic': h, 'current': I_h})

        suppression = []
        if params['f_resonance'] < 2000:
            suppression.append({'method': 'LCL滤波器调谐', 'frequency': params['f_resonance']})

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-14',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'equivalent_inductance_H': params['L_eq'],
            'equivalent_capacitance_F': params['C_eq'],
            'resonance_frequency_Hz': params['f_resonance'],
            'risks': risks,
            'suppression_recommendations': suppression,
            'status': 'WARNING' if risks else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    advisor = ResonanceSuppressionAdvisor()
    result = advisor.run(L_list=[0.001, 0.001], C_list=[0.0001, 0.0001],
                        harmonic_currents={5: 20, 7: 15})
    print(f"谐振频率: {result['resonance_frequency_Hz']:.1f}Hz")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§37 Q-15 电网背景谐波阻抗在线辨识

37.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电网背景谐波阻抗在线辨识 英文名称: Grid Harmonic Impedance Online Identification 技术等级: L2b

在线辨识电网背景谐波阻抗。

37.2 核心算法公式

阻抗计算:
    Z_h = U_h / I_h

阻尼系数:
    ζ = R / (2 × √(L/C))

37.3 参考代码

"""
Q-15 电网背景谐波阻抗在线辨识
==============================
功能: 在线辨识谐波阻抗
输入: 谐波电压电流
输出: 阻抗参数
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class HarmonicImpedanceIdentifier:
    """谐波阻抗辨识模型"""

    def identify_impedance(self, U_h: float, I_h: float) -> Dict:
        """辨识谐波阻抗"""
        if abs(I_h) < 1e-9:
            return {'Z_h': np.inf, 'R_h': 0, 'X_h': 0}

        Z_h = U_h / I_h
        # 简化假设: R_h = 0.1 * Z_h
        R_h = 0.1 * abs(Z_h)
        X_h = np.sqrt(Z_h ** 2 - R_h ** 2)

        return {'Z_h': abs(Z_h), 'R_h': R_h, 'X_h': X_h}

    def run(self, harmonic_data: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        impedance_results = []
        for data in harmonic_data:
            h = data.get('h', 0)
            U_h = data.get('U_h', 0)
            I_h = data.get('I_h', 0)

            z = self.identify_impedance(U_h, I_h)
            impedance_results.append({
                'harmonic_order': h,
                'Z_ohm': z['Z_h'],
                'R_ohm': z['R_h'],
                'X_ohm': z['X_h']
            })

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-15',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'impedance_results': impedance_results
        }


def example_usage():
    identifier = HarmonicImpedanceIdentifier()
    data = [{'h': 5, 'U_h': 10, 'I_h': 2}, {'h': 7, 'U_h': 8, 'I_h': 1.5}]
    result = identifier.run(data)
    print(f"5次谐波阻抗: {result['impedance_results'][0]['Z_ohm']:.2f}Ω")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§38 Q-16 电压波动与风机出力相关性

38.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电压波动与风机出力相关性 英文名称: Voltage Fluctuation vs Wind Power Correlation 技术等级: L2b

分析电压波动与风机出力的相关性。

38.2 核心算法公式

相关系数:
    r = Σ(P_i - P_mean)(U_i - U_mean) / √(Σ(P_i-P_mean)² × Σ(U_i-U_mean)²)

滞后相关:
    r_τ = correlation(P(t), U(t+τ))

38.3 参考代码

"""
Q-16 电压波动与风机出力相关性
=============================
功能: 分析电压与风机出力的相关性
输入: 功率和电压时间序列
输出: 相关系数, 滞后时间
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class CorrelationAnalyzer:
    """相关性分析模型"""

    def calculate_correlation(self, P: list, U: list) -> float:
        """计算相关系数"""
        if len(P) != len(U) or len(P) < 2:
            return 0

        P_arr = np.array(P)
        U_arr = np.array(U)

        if np.std(P_arr) < 1e-9 or np.std(U_arr) < 1e-9:
            return 0

        return np.corrcoef(P_arr, U_arr)[0, 1]

    def calculate_lagged_correlation(self, P: list, U: list, max_lag: int = 10) -> Dict:
        """计算滞后相关性"""
        correlations = []
        for lag in range(max_lag + 1):
            P_shifted = P[:-lag] if lag > 0 else P
            U_shifted = U[lag:] if lag > 0 else U

            if len(P_shifted) > 0:
                r = self.calculate_correlation(P_shifted, U_shifted)
                correlations.append({'lag': lag, 'r': r})

        best = max(correlations, key=lambda x: abs(x['r']))
        return best

    def run(self, P_history: list, U_history: list, max_lag: int = 10) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        direct_corr = self.calculate_correlation(P_history, U_history)
        lagged_best = self.calculate_lagged_correlation(P_history, U_history, max_lag)

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-16',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'direct_correlation': direct_corr,
            'best_lag': lagged_best['lag'],
            'best_lagged_correlation': lagged_best['r'],
            'status': 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = CorrelationAnalyzer()
    P = [10, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60, 70]
    U = [219, 220, 222, 223, 224, 225, 226]
    result = analyzer.run(P, U)
    print(f"相关系数: {result['direct_correlation']:.3f}")
    print(f"最佳滞后: {result['best_lag']}点")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§39 Q-17 电动汽车充电谐波叠加评估

39.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电动汽车充电谐波叠加评估 英文名称: EV Charging Harmonic Superposition Assessment 技术等级: L2a

评估多辆EV同时充电时的谐波叠加效应。

39.2 核心算法公式

N辆EV充电总谐波:
    I_h_total = √(Σ I_h_i²) × K_diversity

叠加因子:
    K_diversity = 1 / √N × (1 + α(N-1))

39.3 参考代码

"""
Q-17 电动汽车充电谐波叠加评估
=============================
功能: 评估EV充电谐波叠加
输入: 充电桩数量, 单桩谐波
输出: 总谐波, 评估结果
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class EVHarmonicAssessor:
    """EV充电谐波评估模型"""

    def __init__(self, alpha: float = 0.3):
        self.alpha = alpha

    def calculate_total_harmonic(self, I_h_single: float, N: int) -> float:
        """计算N辆车充电的总谐波"""
        k_diversity = 1 / np.sqrt(N) * (1 + self.alpha * (N - 1))
        return np.sqrt(N) * I_h_single * k_diversity

    def run(self, N_evs: int, harmonic_currents: Dict[int, float]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        results = {}
        total_thd = 0

        for h, I_h in harmonic_currents.items():
            I_h_total = self.calculate_total_harmonic(I_h, N_evs)
            results[h] = I_h_total

        # 简化的THD计算
        I_fund = harmonic_currents.get(1, 100)
        total_thd = np.sqrt(sum([(I_h / I_fund) ** 2 for h, I_h in results.items() if h > 1]))

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-17',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'n_evs': N_evs,
            'harmonic_currents': results,
            'estimated_THD_percent': total_thd * 100,
            'status': 'WARNING' if total_thd > 0.08 else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    assessor = EVHarmonicAssessor()
    harmonics = {1: 100, 3: 30, 5: 20, 7: 15}
    result = assessor.run(N_evs=10, harmonic_currents=harmonics)
    print(f"10辆EV充电估计THD: {result['estimated_THD_percent']:.2f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§40 Q-18 电能质量综合指数(PQI)评估

40.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电能质量综合指数(PQI)评估 英文名称: Power Quality Index Comprehensive Assessment 技术等级: L1

综合评估电能质量。

40.2 核心算法公式

PQI = √(w1×THD² + w2×σ²_U² + w3×σ²_f² + w4×ε² + ...)

权重: w = [0.3, 0.2, 0.2, 0.3]

40.3 参考代码

"""
Q-18 电能质量综合指数(PQI)
==========================
功能: 综合评估电能质量
输入: 多项电能质量指标
输出: PQI指数
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class PQICalculator:
    """电能质量综合指数计算模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.weights = {
            'THD': 0.3,
            'voltage_dev': 0.2,
            'frequency_dev': 0.2,
            'unbalance': 0.3
        }
        self.limits = {
            'THD': 0.05,
            'voltage_dev': 0.1,
            'frequency_dev': 0.01,
            'unbalance': 0.02
        }

    def normalize(self, value: float, limit: float) -> float:
        """归一化"""
        return min(1.0, value / limit)

    def calculate_pqi(self, THD: float, voltage_dev: float,
                    frequency_dev: float, unbalance: float) -> float:
        """计算PQI"""
        n_thd = self.normalize(THD, self.limits['THD'])
        n_vd = self.normalize(voltage_dev, self.limits['voltage_dev'])
        n_fd = self.normalize(frequency_dev, self.limits['frequency_dev'])
        n_ub = self.normalize(unbalance, self.limits['unbalance'])

        pqi = np.sqrt(
            self.weights['THD'] * n_thd ** 2 +
            self.weights['voltage_dev'] * n_vd ** 2 +
            self.weights['frequency_dev'] * n_fd ** 2 +
            self.weights['unbalance'] * n_ub ** 2
        )

        return min(1.0, pqi)

    def run(self, THD: float, voltage_dev: float, 
           frequency_dev: float, unbalance: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        pqi = self.calculate_pqi(THD, voltage_dev, frequency_dev, unbalance)

        level = 'EXCELLENT' if pqi < 0.3 else 'GOOD' if pqi < 0.5 else 'FAIR' if pqi < 0.7 else 'POOR'

        return {
            'model_id': 'Q-18',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'PQI': pqi,
            'quality_level': level,
            'THD_percent': THD * 100,
            'voltage_dev_percent': voltage_dev * 100,
            'status': 'NORMAL' if pqi < 0.5 else 'WARNING'
        }


def example_usage():
    calculator = PQICalculator()
    result = calculator.run(THD=0.03, voltage_dev=0.05, frequency_dev=0.002, unbalance=0.01)
    print(f"PQI: {result['PQI']:.3f}")
    print(f"质量等级: {result['quality_level']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

E系列 - 用能分析 (15个模型)

§41 E-01 非侵入式负荷分解(NILM)

41.1 功能描述

模型名称: 非侵入式负荷分解(NILM) 英文名称: Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring 技术等级: L2b（需深度学习模型）

从总负荷数据中分解出各设备用电量。

41.2 核心算法公式

负荷分解:
    P_total = Σ P_device_i + ε

特征匹配:
    similarity = cosine(F_device, F_total)

41.3 参考代码

"""
E-01 非侵入式负荷分解(NILM)
===========================
功能: 从总负荷分解设备用电
输入: 总功率波形
输出: 各设备用电量
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class NILMDecomposer:
    """非侵入式负荷分解模型"""

    def __init__(self, appliances: List[str] = None):
        self.appliances = appliances or ['空调', '冰箱', '照明', '热水器', '插座']
        # 简化特征库 (实际需要训练)
        self.signatures = {
            '空调': {'power_range': (800, 3000), 'cycle': True},
            '冰箱': {'power_range': (100, 200), 'cycle': True},
            '照明': {'power_range': (20, 200), 'cycle': False},
            '热水器': {'power_range': (1000, 2000), 'cycle': True},
            '插座': {'power_range': (50, 500), 'cycle': False}
        }

    def extract_features(self, power_segment: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """提取负荷特征"""
        return {
            'mean': np.mean(power_segment),
            'std': np.std(power_segment),
            'max': np.max(power_segment),
            'min': np.min(power_segment)
        }

    def match_appliance(self, features: Dict) -> str:
        """匹配设备"""
        mean_power = features['mean']

        for app, sig in self.signatures.items():
            p_min, p_max = sig['power_range']
            if p_min <= mean_power <= p_max:
                return app
        return '未知设备'

    def run(self, total_power: np.ndarray, time_window: int = 60) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        # 简化分解
        segments = [total_power[i:i+time_window] for i in range(0, len(total_power), time_window)]

        results = []
        for seg in segments:
            if len(seg) < 10:
                continue
            features = self.extract_features(seg)
            appliance = self.match_appliance(features)
            results.append({
                'appliance': appliance,
                'power_W': features['mean'],
                'duration_s': len(seg)
            })

        # 汇总
        summary = {}
        for r in results:
            app = r['appliance']
            if app not in summary:
                summary[app] = {'count': 0, 'total_power': 0, 'total_duration': 0}
            summary[app]['count'] += 1
            summary[app]['total_power'] += r['power_W']
            summary[app]['total_duration'] += r['duration_s']

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-01',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'appliance_summary': summary,
            'total_segments': len(results)
        }


def example_usage():
    decomposer = NILMDecomposer()
    # 模拟总功率
    power = np.concatenate([
        np.ones(60) * 100,      # 冰箱
        np.ones(60) * 1500,     # 空调
        np.ones(60) * 50         # 插座
    ])
    result = decomposer.run(power)
    print(f"检测到的设备: {list(result['appliance_summary'].keys())}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§42 E-02 清洁能源消纳率分析

42.1 功能描述

模型名称: 清洁能源消纳率分析 英文名称: Renewable Energy Utilization Rate Analysis 技术等级: L1

分析清洁能源的自用率和消纳率。

42.2 核心算法公式

自用率:
    Self_use = E_self / E_pv × 100%

消纳率:
    Utilization = (E_self + E_exported) / E_pv × 100%

弃电量:
    E_curtailed = E_pv - E_self - E_exported

42.3 参考代码

"""
E-02 清洁能源消纳率分析
========================
功能: 分析清洁能源消纳情况
输入: 光伏发电, 自用电量, 售电量
输出: 消纳率, 弃电率
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class RenewableUtilizationAnalyzer:
    """清洁能源消纳分析模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        pass

    def calculate_metrics(self, E_pv: float, E_self: float, 
                         E_exported: float) -> Dict:
        """计算消纳指标"""
        total = E_pv

        if total < 1e-9:
            return {
                'self_use_rate': 0, 'utilization_rate': 0, 'curtailment_rate': 0,
                'E_self': 0, 'E_exported': 0, 'E_curtailed': 0
            }

        self_use = min(E_self, E_pv)
        exported = min(E_exported, max(0, E_pv - self_use))
        curtailed = max(0, E_pv - self_use - exported)

        return {
            'self_use_rate': self_use / total * 100,
            'utilization_rate': (self_use + exported) / total * 100,
            'curtailment_rate': curtailed / total * 100,
            'E_self': self_use,
            'E_exported': exported,
            'E_curtailed': curtailed
        }

    def run(self, E_pv: float, E_self: float, E_exported: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        metrics = self.calculate_metrics(E_pv, E_self, E_exported)

        level = 'EXCELLENT' if metrics['utilization_rate'] > 95 else \
                'GOOD' if metrics['utilization_rate'] > 85 else \
                'FAIR' if metrics['utilization_rate'] > 70 else 'POOR'

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-02',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'PV_generation_kWh': E_pv,
            'self_use_kWh': metrics['E_self'],
            'exported_kWh': metrics['E_exported'],
            'curtailed_kWh': metrics['E_curtailed'],
            'self_use_rate_percent': metrics['self_use_rate'],
            'utilization_rate_percent': metrics['utilization_rate'],
            'curtailment_rate_percent': metrics['curtailment_rate'],
            'level': level,
            'status': 'NORMAL' if metrics['curtailment_rate'] < 10 else 'WARNING'
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = RenewableUtilizationAnalyzer()
    result = analyzer.run(E_pv=100, E_self=70, E_exported=25)
    print(f"消纳率: {result['utilization_rate_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"弃电率: {result['curtailment_rate_percent']:.1f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§43 E-03 光储协同优化策略

43.1 功能描述

模型名称: 光储协同优化策略 英文名称: PV-Storage Coordination Strategy 技术等级: L2b

制定光伏和储能系统的协同运行策略。

43.2 核心算法公式

最优充放电策略:
    P_charge = min(P_pv_available, P_battery_max)

最优放电策略:
    P_discharge = min(P_demand, P_battery_available, P_inverter_max)

43.3 参考代码

"""
E-03 光储协同优化策略
=====================
功能: 制定光储协同策略
输入: 光伏功率, 负荷功率, 储能状态
输出: 充放电计划
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class PVStorageOptimizer:
    """光储协同优化模型"""

    def __init__(self, battery_capacity: float = 100, 
                 P_inverter_max: float = 50):
        self.E_battery_max = battery_capacity  # kWh
        self.P_inverter_max = P_inverter_max    # kW

    def optimize_strategy(self, P_pv: float, P_load: float,
                        E_battery: float, P_charge_max: float = 20) -> Dict:
        """优化策略"""
        P_net = P_pv - P_load

        if P_net > 0:
            # 光伏过剩，充电
            P_charge = min(P_net, P_charge_max, self.P_inverter_max)
            P_discharge = 0
        else:
            # 负荷缺口，放电
            P_discharge = min(-P_net, E_battery, self.P_inverter_max)
            P_charge = 0

        E_delta = P_charge - P_discharge

        return {
            'P_charge_kW': P_charge,
            'P_discharge_kW': P_discharge,
            'E_delta_kWh': E_delta * 0.25,  # 假设1小时
            'P_net_kW': P_net
        }

    def run(self, P_pv: float, P_load: float, 
           E_battery: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        strategy = self.optimize_strategy(P_pv, P_load, E_battery)

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-03',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'PV_power_kW': P_pv,
            'load_power_kW': P_load,
            'battery_SOC_kWh': E_battery,
            'charge_power_kW': strategy['P_charge_kW'],
            'discharge_power_kW': strategy['P_discharge_kW'],
            'strategy': 'CHARGE' if strategy['P_charge_kW'] > 0 else 'DISCHARGE' if strategy['P_discharge_kW'] > 0 else 'IDLE'
        }


def example_usage():
    optimizer = PVStorageOptimizer()
    result = optimizer.run(P_pv=30, P_load=20, E_battery=50)
    print(f"策略: {result['strategy']}")
    print(f"充电功率: {result['charge_power_kW']}kW")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§44 E-04 最大需量预测与预警

44.1 功能描述

模型名称: 最大需量预测与预警 英文名称: Maximum Demand Forecasting and Warning 技术等级: L2a

预测最大需量并提前预警。

44.2 核心算法公式

移动最大需量:
    D_max_15min = max(P_15min_window)

预测:
    D_pred = α × D_hist + (1-α) × D_current

预警阈值:
    D_alarm = D_contract × 0.9

44.3 参考代码

"""
E-04 最大需量预测与预警
========================
功能: 预测和预警最大需量
输入: 实时功率数据
输出: 预测需量, 预警
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict
from collections import deque

class DemandForecaster:
    """最大需量预测模型"""

    def __init__(self, contract_demand: float = 500,
                 alpha: float = 0.7):
        self.D_contract = contract_demand
        self.alpha = alpha
        self.demand_history = deque(maxlen=100)

    def calculate_15min_demand(self, power_data: np.ndarray, interval: float = 1.0) -> float:
        """计算15分钟最大需量"""
        window_size = int(900 / interval)  # 15分钟
        if len(power_data) < window_size:
            return np.max(power_data)

        max_demand = 0
        for i in range(len(power_data) - window_size + 1):
            window = power_data[i:i+window_size]
            max_demand = max(max_demand, np.mean(window))

        return max_demand

    def forecast(self, current_demand: float) -> float:
        """预测"""
        if not self.demand_history:
            return current_demand

        hist_avg = np.mean(self.demand_history)
        return self.alpha * hist_avg + (1 - self.alpha) * current_demand

    def run(self, power_data: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        current_demand = self.calculate_15min_demand(power_data)
        self.demand_history.append(current_demand)

        forecast_demand = self.forecast(current_demand)

        alarm_threshold = self.D_contract * 0.9
        critical_threshold = self.D_contract * 0.95

        if forecast_demand > critical_threshold:
            status = 'CRITICAL'
            action = '最大需量将超契约容量，立即节能'
        elif forecast_demand > alarm_threshold:
            status = 'WARNING'
            action = '最大需量接近契约容量，注意节能'
        else:
            status = 'NORMAL'
            action = '需量在安全范围'

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-04',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'current_demand_kW': current_demand,
            'forecast_demand_kW': forecast_demand,
            'contract_demand_kW': self.D_contract,
            'alarm_threshold_kW': alarm_threshold,
            'forecast_vs_contract_percent': forecast_demand / self.D_contract * 100,
            'status': status,
            'action': action
        }


def example_usage():
    forecaster = DemandForecaster(contract_demand=500)
    # 模拟功率数据
    power = np.array([300, 350, 400, 420, 380, 360])
    result = forecaster.run(power)
    print(f"当前需量: {result['current_demand_kW']:.1f}kW")
    print(f"预测需量: {result['forecast_demand_kW']:.1f}kW")
    print(f"状态: {result['status']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§45 E-05 变压器经济运行分析

45.1 功能描述

模型名称: 变压器经济运行分析 英文名称: Transformer Economic Operation Analysis 技术等级: L1

分析变压器在不同负载率下的经济性。

45.2 核心算法公式

总有功损耗:
    P_loss = P_0 + P_k × β²

负载率:
    β = S / S_rated

经济负载率:
    β_eco = √(P_0 / P_k)

45.3 参考代码

"""
E-05 变压器经济运行分析
========================
功能: 分析变压器经济运行
输入: 变压器参数, 负载功率
输出: 损耗, 经济性评估
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class TransformerEconomicAnalyzer:
    """变压器经济运行分析模型"""

    def __init__(self, P_0: float = 1.0, P_k: float = 10.0, S_rated: float = 1000):
        """
        参数:
            P_0: 空载损耗 (kW)
            P_k: 负载损耗 (kW)
            S_rated: 额定容量 (kVA)
        """
        self.P_0 = P_0
        self.P_k = P_k
        self.S_rated = S_rated
        self.beta_eco = np.sqrt(P_0 / P_k)

    def calculate_losses(self, S_load: float) -> Dict:
        """计算损耗"""
        beta = S_load / self.S_rated
        P_loss = self.P_0 + self.P_k * beta ** 2

        return {
            'beta': beta,
            'P_loss_kW': P_loss,
            'efficiency_percent': S_load / (S_load + P_loss) * 100,
            'loss_rate_percent': P_loss / S_load * 100 if S_load > 0 else 0
        }

    def run(self, S_load: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        losses = self.calculate_losses(S_load)

        is_economic = abs(losses['beta'] - self.beta_eco) < 0.1

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-05',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'load_kVA': S_load,
            'load_rate_percent': losses['beta'] * 100,
            'economic_load_rate_percent': self.beta_eco * 100,
            'total_loss_kW': losses['P_loss_kW'],
            'efficiency_percent': losses['efficiency_percent'],
            'is_economic': is_economic,
            'status': 'NORMAL' if is_economic else 'INFO'
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = TransformerEconomicAnalyzer(P_0=1.0, P_k=10.0, S_rated=1000)
    result = analyzer.run(S_load=500)  # 500kVA负载
    print(f"负载率: {result['load_rate_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"经济负载率: {result['economic_load_rate_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"效率: {result['efficiency_percent']:.2f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§46 E-06 峰谷平电量结构分析

46.1 功能描述

模型名称: 峰谷平电量结构分析 英文名称: Peak-Valley-Flat Electricity Structure Analysis 技术等级: L1

分析峰谷平时段的电量结构。

46.2 核心算法公式

时段划分:
    峰时: 8:00-11:00, 18:00-23:00
    谷时: 23:00-7:00
    平时: 其余时段

峰谷比:
    Ratio = E_peak / E_valley

46.3 参考代码

"""
E-06 峰谷平电量结构分析
========================
功能: 分析峰谷平电量
输入: 分时电量数据
输出: 结构分析
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class PeakValleyAnalyzer:
    """峰谷平分析模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.peak_hours = [(8, 11), (18, 23)]
        self.valley_hours = [(23, 7)]

    def get_period(self, hour: int) -> str:
        """判断时段"""
        for start, end in self.peak_hours:
            if start <= hour < end:
                return 'peak'
        for start, end in self.valley_hours:
            if hour >= start or hour < end:
                return 'valley'
        return 'flat'

    def analyze(self, hourly_data: Dict[int, float]) -> Dict:
        """分析"""
        E_peak = sum(hourly_data.get(h, 0) for h in range(24) if self.get_period(h) == 'peak')
        E_valley = sum(hourly_data.get(h, 0) for h in range(24) if self.get_period(h) == 'valley')
        E_flat = sum(hourly_data.get(h, 0) for h in range(24) if self.get_period(h) == 'flat')
        E_total = E_peak + E_valley + E_flat

        if E_total < 1e-9:
            return {'peak_ratio': 0, 'valley_ratio': 0, 'flat_ratio': 0}

        return {
            'E_peak': E_peak, 'E_valley': E_valley, 'E_flat': E_flat,
            'peak_ratio_percent': E_peak / E_total * 100,
            'valley_ratio_percent': E_valley / E_total * 100,
            'flat_ratio_percent': E_flat / E_total * 100,
            'peak_valley_ratio': E_peak / E_valley if E_valley > 0 else float('inf')
        }

    def run(self, hourly_data: Dict[int, float]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        analysis = self.analyze(hourly_data)

        level = 'GOOD' if analysis['valley_ratio_percent'] > 30 else 'FAIR' if analysis['valley_ratio_percent'] > 20 else 'POOR'

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-06',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'total_kWh': analysis['E_peak'] + analysis['E_valley'] + analysis['E_flat'],
            **analysis,
            'level': level,
            'status': 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = PeakValleyAnalyzer()
    hourly = {h: 100 for h in range(24)}
    hourly.update({h: 200 for h in [8,9,10,18,19,20,21,22]})  # 峰时加倍
    result = analyzer.run(hourly)
    print(f"峰时比例: {result['peak_ratio_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"谷时比例: {result['valley_ratio_percent']:.1f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§47 E-07 待机功耗/基础负荷挖掘

47.1 功能描述

模型名称: 待机功耗/基础负荷挖掘 英文名称: Standby Power / Base Load Mining 技术等级: L1

挖掘待机功耗和异常基础负荷。

47.2 核心算法公式

待机功耗识别:
    P_standby = min(P_daily)

异常检测:
    P_base_avg = mean(P_night)
    P_anomaly = P_base > threshold × P_standby

47.3 参考代码

"""
E-07 待机功耗/基础负荷挖掘
==========================
功能: 挖掘待机功耗
输入: 24小时功率数据
输出: 待机功耗, 异常识别
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class StandbyPowerAnalyzer:
    """待机功耗分析模型"""

    def __init__(self, standby_threshold: float = 1.5):
        self.threshold = standby_threshold

    def calculate_standby(self, daily_power: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """计算待机功耗"""
        return np.min(daily_power)

    def detect_anomaly(self, power: np.ndarray, standby: float) -> bool:
        """检测异常基础负荷"""
        night_power = power[:6]  # 凌晨时段
        avg_night = np.mean(night_power)
        return avg_night > standby * self.threshold

    def run(self, hourly_power: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        standby = self.calculate_standby(hourly_power)
        has_anomaly = self.detect_anomaly(hourly_power, standby)

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-07',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'standby_power_W': standby,
            'max_power_W': np.max(hourly_power),
            'avg_power_W': np.mean(hourly_power),
            'anomaly_detected': has_anomaly,
            'status': 'WARNING' if has_anomaly else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = StandbyPowerAnalyzer()
    # 模拟24小时功率
    power = np.array([50, 48, 45, 50, 200, 150, 300, 500, 600, 550, 500, 450,
                     400, 450, 500, 550, 600, 650, 700, 650, 550, 400, 200, 80])
    result = analyzer.run(power)
    print(f"待机功耗: {result['standby_power_W']:.1f}W")
    print(f"异常检测: {result['anomaly_detected']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§48 E-08 用电行为聚类分析

48.1 功能描述

模型名称: 用电行为聚类分析 英文名称: Electricity Consumption Behavior Clustering 技术等级: L2a

对用户用电行为进行聚类分析。

48.2 核心算法公式

特征向量:
    F = [P_avg, P_max, P_min, T_peak, E_peak, E_valley]

距离度量:
    d = √(Σ(F_i - F_j)²)

聚类中心:
    C_k = mean(points_in_cluster_k)

48.3 参考代码

"""
E-08 用电行为聚类分析
======================
功能: 聚类分析用电行为
输入: 用电特征数据
输出: 聚类结果
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class ConsumptionClusteringAnalyzer:
    """用电行为聚类分析模型"""

    def __init__(self, n_clusters: int = 3):
        self.n_clusters = n_clusters

    def extract_features(self, daily_power: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """提取特征"""
        return np.array([
            np.mean(daily_power),
            np.max(daily_power),
            np.min(daily_power),
            np.argmax(daily_power) / 24,  # 峰值时间比例
            np.sum(daily_power > np.mean(daily_power))  # 峰值时长
        ])

    def simple_clustering(self, features: List[np.ndarray]) -> List[int]:
        """简化聚类"""
        # 基于均值排序，简单分组
        sorted_idx = np.argsort([f[0] for f in features])
        clusters = [0] * len(features)

        for i, idx in enumerate(sorted_idx):
            clusters[idx] = i % self.n_clusters

        return clusters

    def run(self, daily_profiles: List[np.ndarray]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        features = [self.extract_features(p) for p in daily_profiles]
        clusters = self.simple_clustering(features)

        cluster_stats = {}
        for i, c in enumerate(clusters):
            if c not in cluster_stats:
                cluster_stats[c] = []
            cluster_stats[c].append(features[i])

        summary = {c: np.mean(stats, axis=0).tolist() for c, stats in cluster_stats.items()}

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-08',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'n_profiles': len(daily_profiles),
            'n_clusters': self.n_clusters,
            'cluster_assignments': clusters,
            'cluster_centers': summary
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = ConsumptionClusteringAnalyzer(n_clusters=3)
    profiles = [np.random.rand(24) * 500 + 100 for _ in range(30)]
    result = analyzer.run(profiles)
    print(f"聚类数: {result['n_clusters']}")
    print(f"聚类结果: {result['cluster_assignments'][:10]}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§49 E-09 碳足迹实时追踪

49.1 功能描述

模型名称: 碳足迹实时追踪 英文名称: Carbon Footprint Real-Time Tracking 技术等级: L1

实时计算和追踪碳排放。

49.2 核心算法公式

实时碳排放:
    CO2 = Σ(P_i × EF_i × T_i) / 1000

碳排放因子:
    EF_grid = 0.5283 kgCO2/kWh (华南电网)
    EF_pv = 0 kgCO2/kWh

49.3 参考代码

"""
E-09 碳足迹实时追踪
====================
功能: 实时追踪碳排放
输入: 分源功率数据
输出: 碳排放量
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class CarbonFootprintTracker:
    """碳足迹追踪模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.emission_factors = {
            'grid': 0.5283,  # kgCO2/kWh
            'pv': 0,
            'storage': 0,
            'diesel': 0.7
        }

    def calculate_emission(self, power_sources: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict:
        """计算碳排放"""
        total_co2 = 0
        breakdown = {}

        for source, power in power_sources.items():
            ef = self.emission_factors.get(source, 0)
            co2 = power * ef  # kW × kg/kWh = kgCO2
            breakdown[source] = co2
            total_co2 += co2

        return {
            'total_kgCO2': total_co2,
            'breakdown_kgCO2': breakdown,
            'total_tCO2': total_co2 / 1000
        }

    def run(self, power_sources: Dict[str, float], 
           time_interval: float = 1.0) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        emission = self.calculate_emission(power_sources)

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-09',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'emission_rate_kgCO2_h': emission['total_kgCO2'],
            'emission_rate_tCO2_h': emission['total_tCO2'],
            'breakdown': emission['breakdown_kgCO2'],
            'status': 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    tracker = CarbonFootprintTracker()
    sources = {'grid': 500, 'pv': 200}
    result = tracker.run(sources)
    print(f"碳排放率: {result['emission_rate_kgCO2_h']:.2f} kgCO2/h")
    print(f"等效植树: {result['emission_rate_kgCO2_h'] / 5:.1f} 棵/年")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§50 E-10 虚拟电厂(VPP)可调容量评估

50.1 功能描述

模型名称: 虚拟电厂可调容量评估 英文名称: VPP Adjustable Capacity Assessment 技术等级: L2b

评估虚拟电厂的可调容量。

50.2 核心算法公式

可调容量:
    P_adjustable = P_available - P_reserved

上调能力:
    P_up = min(P_max_i - P_current_i)

下调能力:
    P_down = max(P_current_i - P_min_i)

50.3 参考代码

"""
E-10 虚拟电厂(VPP)可调容量评估
==============================
功能: 评估VPP可调容量
输入: 分布式资源数据
输出: 可调容量
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class VPPCapacityAssessor:
    """VPP可调容量评估模型"""

    def assess(self, resources: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """评估可调容量"""
        P_up_total = 0
        P_down_total = 0
        P_available_total = 0

        for r in resources:
            P_max = r.get('P_max', 0)
            P_min = r.get('P_min', 0)
            P_current = r.get('P_current', (P_max + P_min) / 2)
            P_available = r.get('P_available', P_max - P_min)

            P_up = max(0, P_max - P_current)
            P_down = max(0, P_current - P_min)

            P_up_total += P_up
            P_down_total += P_down
            P_available_total += P_available

        return {
            'P_up_kW': P_up_total,
            'P_down_kW': P_down_total,
            'P_available_kW': P_available_total
        }

    def run(self, resources: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        capacity = self.assess(resources)

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-10',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'total_up_capacity_kW': capacity['P_up_kW'],
            'total_down_capacity_kW': capacity['P_down_kW'],
            'total_available_kW': capacity['P_available_kW'],
            'n_resources': len(resources),
            'status': 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    assessor = VPPCapacityAssessor()
    resources = [
        {'P_max': 100, 'P_min': 20, 'P_current': 60},
        {'P_max': 50, 'P_min': 10, 'P_current': 40}
    ]
    result = assessor.run(resources)
    print(f"上调能力: {result['total_up_capacity_kW']:.1f}kW")
    print(f"下调能力: {result['total_down_capacity_kW']:.1f}kW")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§51 E-11 绿电溯源与消纳证明

51.1 功能描述

模型名称: 绿电溯源与消纳证明 英文名称: Green Power Traceability and Consumption Certification 技术等级: L1

提供绿电溯源和消纳证明。

51.2 核心算法公式

绿电消纳率:
    Green_ratio = E_green_used / E_total × 100%

溯源匹配:
    Match = min(E_pv, E_load_green)

51.3 参考代码

"""
E-11 绿电溯源与消纳证明
========================
功能: 绿电溯源和消纳证明
输入: 绿电和负荷数据
输出: 消纳证明
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class GreenPowerCertifier:
    """绿电溯源证明模型"""

    def certify(self, E_pv: float, E_load_green: float, E_total: float) -> Dict:
        """生成消纳证明"""
        green_used = min(E_pv, E_load_green)
        green_ratio = green_used / E_total * 100 if E_total > 0 else 0
        match_ratio = green_used / E_pv * 100 if E_pv > 0 else 0

        return {
            'green_used_kWh': green_used,
            'green_ratio_percent': green_ratio,
            'pv_match_ratio_percent': match_ratio,
            'certification_id': f"GP-{pd.Timestamp.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}"
        }

    def run(self, E_pv: float, E_load_green: float, E_total: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        cert = self.certify(E_pv, E_load_green, E_total)

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-11',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'pv_generation_kWh': E_pv,
            'green_load_kWh': E_load_green,
            **cert,
            'status': 'CERTIFIED'
        }


def example_usage():
    certifier = GreenPowerCertifier()
    result = certifier.run(E_pv=1000, E_load_green=800, E_total=5000)
    print(f"绿电消纳率: {result['green_ratio_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"证书ID: {result['certification_id']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§52 E-12 储能电池健康度(SOH)在线估计

52.1 功能描述

模型名称: 储能电池健康度(SOH)在线估计 英文名称: Battery State of Health (SOH) Online Estimation 技术等级: L2b

在线估计储能电池的健康状态。

52.2 核心算法公式

SOH估计:
    SOH = C_current / C_initial × 100%

内阻法:
    R_internal = ΔU / ΔI
    SOH = (R_ref / R_current) × 100%

52.3 参考代码

"""
E-12 储能电池健康度(SOH)在线估计
================================
功能: 在线估计SOH
输入: 电池运行数据
输出: SOH值
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class BatterySOHEstimator:
    """电池SOH估计模型"""

    def __init__(self, C_initial: float = 100, R_initial: float = 0.01):
        self.C_initial = C_initial  # Ah
        self.R_initial = R_initial  # Ohm

    def estimate_soh_capacity(self, C_current: float) -> float:
        """容量法SOH"""
        return C_current / self.C_initial * 100

    def estimate_soh_resistance(self, R_internal: float) -> float:
        """内阻法SOH"""
        return (self.R_initial / R_internal) * 100

    def run(self, C_current: float = None, R_internal: float = None) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        if C_current is not None:
            soh = self.estimate_soh_capacity(C_current)
        elif R_internal is not None:
            soh = self.estimate_soh_resistance(R_internal)
        else:
            soh = 100

        level = 'GOOD' if soh > 80 else 'FAIR' if soh > 60 else 'REPLACE_SOON'

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-12',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'SOH_percent': soh,
            'level': level,
            'C_initial_Ah': self.C_initial,
            'status': 'WARNING' if soh < 80 else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    estimator = BatterySOHEstimator(C_initial=100)
    result = estimator.run(C_current=85)
    print(f"电池SOH: {result['SOH_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"状态: {result['level']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§53 E-13 光伏组串衰减与阴影遮挡检测

53.1 功能描述

模型名称: 光伏组串衰减与阴影遮挡检测 英文名称: PV String Degradation and Shading Detection 技术等级: L2b

检测光伏组串的功率衰减和阴影遮挡。

53.2 核心算法公式

功率衰减率:
    Degradation = (P_rated - P_actual) / P_rated × 100%

阴影指数:
    Shading = P_min_string / P_avg_string

53.3 参考代码

"""
E-13 光伏组串衰减与阴影遮挡检测
===============================
功能: 检测组串衰减和阴影
输入: 组串功率数据
输出: 衰减率, 阴影指数
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class PVStringAnalyzer:
    """光伏组串分析模型"""

    def __init__(self, P_rated: float = 500):
        self.P_rated = P_rated

    def analyze(self, string_powers: List[float]) -> Dict:
        """分析组串"""
        P_avg = np.mean(string_powers)
        P_min = np.min(string_powers)
        P_max = np.max(string_powers)

        degradation = (self.P_rated - P_avg) / self.P_rated * 100
        shading_index = P_min / P_avg if P_avg > 0 else 1

        return {
            'degradation_percent': max(0, degradation),
            'shading_index': shading_index,
            'string_count': len(string_powers),
            'avg_power_W': P_avg,
            'min_power_W': P_min,
            'max_power_W': P_max
        }

    def run(self, string_powers: List[float], 
           irradiance: float = 1000) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        analysis = self.analyze(string_powers)

        # 考虑辐照度修正
        corrected_degradation = analysis['degradation_percent'] / (irradiance / 1000) if irradiance > 800 else analysis['degradation_percent']

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-13',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'degradation_percent': corrected_degradation,
            'shading_index': analysis['shading_index'],
            'irradiance_W_m2': irradiance,
            'status': 'WARNING' if analysis['shading_index'] < 0.7 or corrected_degradation > 20 else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = PVStringAnalyzer(P_rated=500)
    powers = [480, 450, 350, 470, 460]  # 一个组串偏低(阴影)
    result = analyzer.run(powers, irradiance=900)
    print(f"衰减率: {result['degradation_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"阴影指数: {result['shading_index']:.2f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§54 E-14 园区级多能互补调度

54.1 功能描述

模型名称: 园区级多能互补调度 英文名称: Park-Level Multi-Energy Complementary Dispatch 技术等级: L3

制定园区多能互补调度策略。

54.2 核心算法公式

最优调度:
    min Σ(C_i × P_i)
    s.t. ΣP_i = P_demand
         P_i_min ≤ P_i ≤ P_i_max

54.3 参考代码

"""
E-14 园区级多能互补调度
========================
功能: 多能互补调度优化
输入: 能源价格, 可用容量, 需求
输出: 调度计划
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class MultiEnergyOptimizer:
    """多能互补优化模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.costs = {'grid': 0.8, 'pv': 0.1, 'storage': 0.5, 'diesel': 1.2}

    def optimize(self, demand: float, sources: Dict[str, Dict]) -> Dict:
        """优化调度"""
        allocation = {}
        remaining = demand

        # 按成本排序
        sorted_sources = sorted(self.costs.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])

        for source, cost in sorted_sources:
            if source in sources and remaining > 0:
                available = sources[source].get('available', 0)
                allocated = min(remaining, available)
                allocation[source] = allocated
                remaining -= allocated

        total_cost = sum(allocation.get(s, 0) * self.costs.get(s, 0) for s in allocation)

        return {
            'allocation': allocation,
            'remaining_unserved': max(0, remaining),
            'total_cost_yuan': total_cost
        }

    def run(self, demand: float, sources: Dict[str, Dict]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        result = self.optimize(demand, sources)

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-14',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'demand_kW': demand,
            'allocation': result['allocation'],
            'remaining_kW': result['remaining_unserved'],
            'estimated_cost_yuan_per_h': result['total_cost_yuan'],
            'status': 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    optimizer = MultiEnergyOptimizer()
    demand = 500
    sources = {
        'pv': {'available': 300},
        'storage': {'available': 100},
        'grid': {'available': 500}
    }
    result = optimizer.run(demand, sources)
    print(f"调度方案: {result['allocation']}")
    print(f"预计成本: ¥{result['estimated_cost_yuan_per_h']:.1f}/h")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§55 E-15 碳资产管理与交易策略

55.1 功能描述

模型名称: 碳资产管理与交易策略 英文名称: Carbon Asset Management and Trading Strategy 技术等级: L3

管理碳资产并制定交易策略。

55.2 核心算法公式

碳配额:
    Allowance = Benchmark × Activity

碳信用:
    Credit = Emission_reduced × Price_CER

交易策略:
    Buy: P_CER < P_allowance × 0.8
    Sell: P_CER > P_allowance × 1.2

55.3 参考代码

"""
E-15 碳资产管理与交易策略
==========================
功能: 碳资产管理和交易
输入: 排放数据, 碳价格
输出: 交易策略
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class CarbonAssetManager:
    """碳资产管理模型"""

    def __init__(self, benchmark: float = 0.8):
        self.benchmark = benchmark  # tCO2/unit

    def calculate_allowance(self, activity: float) -> float:
        """计算配额"""
        return self.benchmark * activity

    def calculate_credit(self, emission_actual: float, emission_baseline: float) -> float:
        """计算碳信用"""
        return max(0, emission_baseline - emission_actual)

    def generate_strategy(self, P_allowance: float, P_cer: float,
                         net_position: float) -> str:
        """生成交易策略"""
        if P_cer < P_allowance * 0.8 and net_position < 0:
            return 'BUY_CER'
        elif P_cer > P_allowance * 1.2 and net_position > 0:
            return 'SELL_CER'
        elif net_position < -100:
            return 'BUY_ALLOWANCE'
        else:
            return 'HOLD'

    def run(self, activity: float, emission_actual: float,
           P_allowance: float, P_cer: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        allowance = self.calculate_allowance(activity)
        credit = self.calculate_credit(emission_actual, allowance)
        net_position = allowance - emission_actual
        strategy = self.generate_strategy(P_allowance, P_cer, net_position)

        return {
            'model_id': 'E-15',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'allowance_tCO2': allowance,
            'emission_tCO2': emission_actual,
            'credit_tCO2': credit,
            'net_position_tCO2': net_position,
            'carbon_price_allowance': P_allowance,
            'carbon_price_cer': P_cer,
            'strategy': strategy,
            'estimated_value_yuan': net_position * P_allowance,
            'status': 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    manager = CarbonAssetManager(benchmark=0.8)
    result = manager.run(activity=1000, emission_actual=700, 
                        P_allowance=50, P_cer=35)
    print(f"碳配额: {result['allowance_tCO2']:.0f}t")
    print(f"净头寸: {result['net_position_tCO2']:.0f}t")
    print(f"交易策略: {result['strategy']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

C系列 - 节能对策 (12个模型)

§56 C-01 无功补偿优化建议

56.1 功能描述

模型名称: 无功补偿优化建议 英文名称: Reactive Power Compensation Optimization 技术等级: L1

提供无功补偿优化建议。

56.2 核心算法公式

目标功率因数:
    cos(φ_target) ≥ 0.9

补偿容量:
    Q_c = P × (tan(φ_1) - tan(φ_2))

56.3 参考代码

"""
C-01 无功补偿优化建议
=====================
功能: 提供无功补偿建议
输入: 视在功率, 有功功率, 目标功率因数
输出: 补偿建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class ReactiveCompensationAdvisor:
    """无功补偿优化模型"""

    def __init__(self, target_pf: float = 0.95):
        self.target_pf = target_pf

    def calculate_pf(self, P: float, Q: float) -> float:
        """计算功率因数"""
        S = np.sqrt(P**2 + Q**2)
        return P / S if S > 0 else 1

    def calculate_compensation(self, P: float, Q: float) -> Dict:
        """计算补偿"""
        pf_current = self.calculate_pf(P, Q)

        if pf_current >= self.target_pf:
            return {'Q_compensation': 0, 'pf_after': pf_current}

        phi_current = np.arccos(pf_current)
        phi_target = np.arccos(self.target_pf)

        Q_needed = P * (np.tan(phi_current) - np.tan(phi_target))

        return {'Q_compensation': max(0, Q_needed), 'pf_after': self.target_pf}

    def run(self, P: float, Q: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        compensation = self.calculate_compensation(P, Q)
        pf_current = self.calculate_pf(P, Q)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-01',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'current_pf': pf_current,
            'target_pf': self.target_pf,
            'reactive_power_kvar': Q,
            'compensation_kvar': compensation['Q_compensation'],
            'pf_after_compensation': compensation['pf_after'],
            'status': 'RECOMMEND' if compensation['Q_compensation'] > 10 else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    advisor = ReactiveCompensationAdvisor(target_pf=0.95)
    result = advisor.run(P=100, Q=60)
    print(f"当前功率因数: {result['current_pf']:.3f}")
    print(f"建议补偿: {result['compensation_kvar']:.1f}kvar")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§57 C-02 谐波治理经济性评估

57.1 功能描述

模型名称: 谐波治理经济性评估 英文名称: Harmonic Treatment Economic Evaluation 技术等级: L1

评估谐波治理的经济性。

57.2 核心算法公式

谐波治理收益:
    Benefit = Loss_reduction + Penalty_avoidance

年化成本:
    Cost = C_investment / N_years + C_operation

投资回收期:
    Payback = C_investment / Annual_benefit

57.3 参考代码

"""
C-02 谐波治理经济性评估
========================
功能: 评估谐波治理经济性
输入: 谐波数据, 投资成本
输出: 经济性评估
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class HarmonicEconomicEvaluator:
    """谐波经济性评估模型"""

    def __init__(self, penalty_per_percent: float = 1000):
        self.penalty_rate = penalty_per_percent

    def evaluate(self, THD_before: float, THD_after: float,
                loss_before: float, loss_after: float,
                investment: float, years: int = 10,
                electricity_price: float = 0.8) -> Dict:
        """经济性评估"""
        penalty_before = max(0, THD_before - 5) * self.penalty_rate
        penalty_after = max(0, THD_after - 5) * self.penalty_rate

        loss_reduction_kW = (loss_before - loss_after) / 1000
        annual_benefit = loss_reduction_kW * 8760 * electricity_price + (penalty_before - penalty_after)

        annual_cost = investment / years

        payback = investment / annual_benefit if annual_benefit > 0 else float('inf')
        roi = (annual_benefit - annual_cost) / investment * 100 if investment > 0 else 0

        return {
            'annual_benefit_yuan': annual_benefit,
            'annual_cost_yuan': annual_cost,
            'payback_years': payback,
            'roi_percent': roi,
            'THD_reduction_percent': THD_before - THD_after
        }

    def run(self, THD_before: float, THD_after: float,
           loss_before: float, loss_after: float,
           investment: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        result = self.evaluate(THD_before, THD_after, loss_before, loss_after, investment)

        level = 'RECOMMENDED' if result['payback_years'] < 5 else 'CONSIDER' if result['payback_years'] < 10 else 'NOT_RECOMMENDED'

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-02',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'THD_before_percent': THD_before,
            'THD_after_percent': THD_after,
            **result,
            'recommendation': level,
            'status': 'RECOMMENDED' if result['payback_years'] < 10 else 'INFO'
        }


def example_usage():
    evaluator = HarmonicEconomicEvaluator()
    result = evaluator.run(THD_before=15, THD_after=5, loss_before=5000, loss_after=2000, investment=50000)
    print(f"投资回收期: {result['payback_years']:.1f}年")
    print(f"ROI: {result['roi_percent']:.1f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§58 C-03 电机能效评估与替换建议

58.1 功能描述

模型名称: 电机能效评估与替换建议 英文名称: Motor Energy Efficiency Assessment 技术等级: L2a

评估电机能效并给出替换建议。

58.2 核心算法公式

能效等级:
    IE3: η ≥ 91.5% (2极), 92.5% (4极)
    IE4: η ≥ 93.5% (2极), 94.5% (4极)

年节能:
    Saving = (η_new - η_old) / η_new × P_rated × T × Price

58.3 参考代码

"""
C-03 电机能效评估与替换建议
============================
功能: 评估电机能效
输入: 电机参数
输出: 能效等级, 替换建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class MotorEfficiencyEvaluator:
    """电机能效评估模型"""

    IE3_4P = {0.75: 0.875, 1.5: 0.895, 2.2: 0.905, 4: 0.915, 7.5: 0.927, 11: 0.935, 15: 0.940, 22: 0.945}
    IE4_4P = {k: v + 0.02 for k, v in IE3_4P.items()}

    def get_efficiency(self, power: float, poles: int = 4) -> Dict:
        """获取效率等级"""
        rated_power = min(self.IE3_4P.keys(), key=lambda x: abs(x - power))
        ie3 = self.IE3_4P.get(rated_power, 0.9)
        ie4 = self.IE4_4P.get(rated_power, 0.92)

        return {'IE3': ie3, 'IE4': ie4}

    def evaluate_replacement(self, current_eff: float, target_eff: float,
                           P_rated: float, hours: int = 8760,
                           price: float = 0.8, new_motor_cost: float = 0) -> Dict:
        """评估替换"""
        saving_kW = P_rated * (1/current_eff - 1/target_eff)
        annual_saving_kWh = saving_kW * hours
        annual_saving_yuan = annual_saving_kWh * price

        payback = new_motor_cost / annual_saving_yuan if annual_saving_yuan > 0 else float('inf')

        return {
            'saving_kW': saving_kW,
            'annual_saving_kWh': annual_saving_kWh,
            'annual_saving_yuan': annual_saving_yuan,
            'payback_years': payback
        }

    def run(self, P_rated: float, current_efficiency: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        eff_standards = self.get_efficiency(P_rated)

        target_eff = eff_standards['IE3']
        recommendation = 'REPLACE_TO_IE3' if current_efficiency < eff_standards['IE3'] else 'GOOD'

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-03',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'motor_power_kW': P_rated,
            'current_efficiency_percent': current_efficiency * 100,
            'IE3_efficiency_percent': eff_standards['IE3'] * 100,
            'IE4_efficiency_percent': eff_standards['IE4'] * 100,
            'recommendation': recommendation,
            'status': 'WARNING' if recommendation == 'REPLACE_TO_IE3' else 'NORMAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    evaluator = MotorEfficiencyEvaluator()
    result = evaluator.run(P_rated=11, current_efficiency=0.90)
    print(f"当前效率: {result['current_efficiency_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"IE3标准: {result['IE3_efficiency_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"建议: {result['recommendation']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§59 C-04 照明/空调智能控制策略

59.1 功能描述

模型名称: 照明/空调智能控制策略 英文名称: Lighting/HVAC Smart Control Strategy 技术等级: L2b

制定智能控制策略。

59.2 核心算法公式

照明调光:
    Dimming = (E_required - E_natural) / E_total × 100%

空调设定:
    T_set = f(Outdoor_T, Occupancy, Time)

59.3 参考代码

"""
C-04 照明/空调智能控制策略
===========================
功能: 智能控制策略
输入: 环境参数
输出: 控制策略
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class SmartControlStrategy:
    """智能控制策略模型"""

    def strategy_lighting(self, natural_light: float, target_lux: float = 500) -> float:
        """照明策略"""
        if natural_light >= target_lux:
            return 0
        return min(100, (target_lux - natural_light) / target_lux * 100)

    def strategy_hvac(self, outdoor_temp: float, occupancy: int, 
                     hour: int, setpoint_base: float = 26) -> float:
        """空调策略"""
        if occupancy < 2:
            return setpoint_base + 2  # 节能模式

        if hour < 9 or hour > 18:
            return setpoint_base + 1  # 非工作时间

        if outdoor_temp > 35:
            return setpoint_base - 1  # 高温补偿

        return setpoint_base

    def run(self, natural_light: float, outdoor_temp: float,
           occupancy: int, hour: int) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        dimming = self.strategy_lighting(natural_light)
        temp_setpoint = self.strategy_hvac(outdoor_temp, occupancy, hour)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-04',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'natural_light_lux': natural_light,
            'lighting_dimming_percent': dimming,
            'outdoor_temp_C': outdoor_temp,
            'hvac_temp_setpoint_C': temp_setpoint,
            'occupancy': occupancy,
            'estimated_saving_percent': min(30, (dimming/3 + (temp_setpoint-24)*2))
        }


def example_usage():
    strategy = SmartControlStrategy()
    result = strategy.run(natural_light=200, outdoor_temp=33, occupancy=20, hour=14)
    print(f"照明调光: {result['lighting_dimming_percent']:.0f}%")
    print(f"空调设定: {result['hvac_temp_setpoint_C']:.0f}°C")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§60 C-05 错峰用电生产排程建议

60.1 功能描述

模型名称: 错峰用电生产排程建议 英文名称: Peak Shifting Production Scheduling 技术等级: L2b

提供错峰用电排程建议。

60.2 核心算法公式

峰谷电价比:
    Ratio = Price_peak / Price_valley

最优排程:
    max Σ(P_shifted × Price_reduction)

60.3 参考代码

"""
C-05 错峰用电生产排程建议
==========================
功能: 提供错峰排程建议
输入: 生产计划, 电价数据
输出: 排程建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class PeakShiftingScheduler:
    """错峰排程模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.peak_hours = [9, 10, 11, 18, 19, 20, 21, 22]
        self.valley_hours = [23, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]

    def suggest_schedule(self, production_plan: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
        """排程建议"""
        schedule = []

        for task in production_plan:
            if task.get('flexible', False):
                schedule.append({
                    **task,
                    'suggested_hour': self._find_best_hour(task),
                    'original_hour': task.get('hour', 0),
                    'saving_yuan': self._calculate_saving(task)
                })

        return schedule

    def _find_best_hour(self, task: Dict) -> int:
        """找最佳时段"""
        return 3  # 简化假设凌晨3点

    def _calculate_saving(self, task: Dict) -> float:
        """计算节省"""
        return task.get('power', 0) * 0.5 * 8  # 简化估算

    def run(self, production_plan: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        schedule = self.suggest_schedule(production_plan)
        total_saving = sum(s.get('saving_yuan', 0) for s in schedule)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-05',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'suggested_schedule': schedule,
