            'total_daily_saving_yuan': total_saving,
            'n_tasks_scheduled': len(schedule)
        }


def example_usage():
    scheduler = PeakShiftingScheduler()
    plan = [
        {'task': 'A', 'power': 100, 'hour': 10, 'flexible': True},
        {'task': 'B', 'power': 200, 'hour': 14, 'flexible': False}
    ]
    result = scheduler.run(plan)
    print(f"建议排程任务数: {result['n_tasks_scheduled']}")
    print(f"预计节省: ¥{result['total_daily_saving_yuan']:.1f}/天")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§61 C-06 分布式光伏自发自用最大化

61.1 功能描述

模型名称: 分布式光伏自发自用最大化 英文名称: Maximize Self-Consumption of Distributed PV 技术等级: L2a

最大化光伏自发自用。

61.2 核心算法公式

自发自用率:
    Self_use = E_self / E_pv × 100%

消纳优化:
    Storage_charge = E_excess * efficiency

61.3 参考代码

"""
C-06 分布式光伏自发自用最大化
==============================
功能: 最大化光伏自用
输入: 光伏, 负荷, 储能
输出: 优化方案
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class SelfConsumptionOptimizer:
    """自发自用优化模型"""

    def __init__(self, storage_capacity: float = 100, storage_eff: float = 0.9):
        self.E_storage_max = storage_capacity
        self.efficiency = storage_eff

    def optimize(self, E_pv: float, E_load: float, E_storage: float) -> Dict:
        """优化"""
        E_excess = max(0, E_pv - E_load)

        E_to_storage = min(E_excess * self.efficiency, self.E_storage_max - E_storage)
        E_stored_used = min(E_storage * self.efficiency, max(0, E_load - E_pv))

        E_self = min(E_pv, E_load) + E_stored_used
        self_consumption_rate = E_self / E_pv * 100 if E_pv > 0 else 0

        return {
            'E_self_kWh': E_self,
            'E_to_storage_kWh': E_to_storage,
            'E_from_storage_kWh': E_stored_used,
            'self_consumption_rate_percent': self_consumption_rate
        }

    def run(self, E_pv: float, E_load: float, E_storage: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        result = self.optimize(E_pv, E_load, E_storage)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-06',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'PV_generation_kWh': E_pv,
            'load_kWh': E_load,
            'storage_SOC_kWh': E_storage,
            **result,
            'status': 'OPTIMIZED'
        }


def example_usage():
    optimizer = SelfConsumptionOptimizer()
    result = optimizer.run(E_pv=100, E_load=60, E_storage=30)
    print(f"自发自用率: {result['self_consumption_rate_percent']:.1f}%")
    print(f"储电: {result['E_to_storage_kWh']:.1f}kWh")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§62 C-07 中央空调系统节能优化

62.1 功能描述

模型名称: 中央空调系统节能优化 英文名称: Central HVAC System Optimization 技术等级: L2b

优化中央空调系统运行。

62.2 核心算法公式

能效比:
    COP = Q_cooling / P_electrical

最佳供水温度:
    T_supply_opt = f(Outdoor_WB, Load)

62.3 参考代码

"""
C-07 中央空调系统节能优化
==========================
功能: 优化空调运行
输入: 负荷, 环境参数
输出: 优化建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class HVACOptimizer:
    """空调优化模型"""

    def calculate_cop(self, T_outdoor: float, T_supply: float) -> float:
        """计算COP"""
        return 300 / (T_outdoor - T_supply + 10)

    def suggest_supply_temp(self, T_outdoor: float, load_percent: float) -> float:
        """建议供水温度"""
        base_temp = 7
        adjustment = (T_outdoor - 35) * 0.1 + (load_percent - 0.5) * 2
        return max(5, min(12, base_temp + adjustment))

    def run(self, T_outdoor: float, load_percent: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        cop = self.calculate_cop(T_outdoor, 7)
        T_supply = self.suggest_supply_temp(T_outdoor, load_percent)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-07',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'outdoor_temp_C': T_outdoor,
            'load_percent': load_percent * 100,
            'current_COP': cop,
            'suggested_supply_temp_C': T_supply,
            'estimated_cop_improvement': (self.calculate_cop(T_outdoor, T_supply) - cop) / cop * 100
        }


def example_usage():
    optimizer = HVACOptimizer()
    result = optimizer.run(T_outdoor=33, load_percent=0.7)
    print(f"建议供水温度: {result['suggested_supply_temp_C']:.1f}°C")
    print(f"预计COP改善: {result['estimated_cop_improvement']:.1f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§63 C-08 空压机群联控优化

63.1 功能描述

模型名称: 空压机群联控优化 英文名称: Compressor Group Coordination Optimization 技术等级: L2a

优化空压机群运行。

63.2 核心算法公式

最优组合:
    min Σ(P_i × N_i) / Σ(Q_i × N_i)

加载台数:
    N_opt = ceil(Q_demand / Q_avg_unit)

63.3 参考代码

"""
C-08 空压机群联控优化
======================
功能: 优化空压机运行
输入: 需求, 机组参数
输出: 运行方案
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, List

class CompressorOptimizer:
    """空压机优化模型"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.units = [{'P': 150, 'Q': 20}, {'P': 100, 'Q': 15}, {'P': 50, 'Q': 8}]

    def optimize(self, Q_demand: float) -> Dict:
        """优化"""
        units_sorted = sorted(self.units, key=lambda x: x['P']/x['Q'])

        selected = []
        Q_total = 0

        for unit in units_sorted:
            if Q_total < Q_demand:
                selected.append(unit)
                Q_total += unit['Q']

        P_total = sum(u['P'] for u in selected)
        efficiency = Q_total / P_total if P_total > 0 else 0

        return {
            'selected_units': len(selected),
            'total_flow_m3_min': Q_total,
            'total_power_kW': P_total,
            'specific_power': 1/efficiency if efficiency > 0 else 0
        }

    def run(self, Q_demand: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        result = self.optimize(Q_demand)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-08',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'demand_m3_min': Q_demand,
            **result,
            'status': 'OPTIMIZED'
        }


def example_usage():
    optimizer = CompressorOptimizer()
    result = optimizer.run(Q_demand=35)
    print(f"建议台数: {result['selected_units']}")
    print(f"总功率: {result['total_power_kW']}kW")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§64 C-09 风机水泵变频调速策略

64.1 功能描述

模型名称: 风机水泵变频调速策略 英文名称: VFD Speed Control for Fan/Pump 技术等级: L1

制定变频调速策略。

64.2 核心算法公式

相似定律:
    Q2/Q1 = n2/n1
    P2/P1 = (n2/n1)³

64.3 参考代码

"""
C-09 风机水泵变频调速策略
==========================
功能: 变频调速优化
输入: 当前流量, 需求流量
输出: 调速建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class VFDSpeedOptimizer:
    """变频调速优化模型"""

    def calculate_speed_ratio(self, Q_current: float, Q_demand: float) -> float:
        """计算调速比"""
        if Q_current < 1e-9:
            return 1.0
        return min(1.0, Q_demand / Q_current)

    def calculate_power_saving(self, speed_ratio: float, P_rated: float) -> Dict:
        """计算节能"""
        P_new = P_rated * (speed_ratio ** 3)
        saving = P_rated - P_new

        return {
            'speed_ratio_percent': speed_ratio * 100,
            'power_kW': P_new,
            'saving_kW': saving,
            'saving_rate_percent': saving / P_rated * 100 if P_rated > 0 else 0
        }

    def run(self, Q_current: float, Q_demand: float, P_rated: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        ratio = self.calculate_speed_ratio(Q_current, Q_demand)
        saving = self.calculate_power_saving(ratio, P_rated)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-09',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'current_flow_m3_h': Q_current,
            'demand_flow_m3_h': Q_demand,
            **saving,
            'status': 'OPTIMIZED'
        }


def example_usage():
    optimizer = VFDSpeedOptimizer()
    result = optimizer.run(Q_current=100, Q_demand=70, P_rated=50)
    print(f"建议转速: {result['speed_ratio_percent']:.0f}%")
    print(f"节能率: {result['saving_rate_percent']:.1f}%")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§65 C-10 余热回收发电潜力评估

65.1 功能描述

模型名称: 余热回收发电潜力评估 英文名称: Waste Heat Recovery Power Potential Assessment 技术等级: L2a

评估余热回收发电潜力。

65.2 核心算法公式

回收功率:
    P_ORC = Q_waste × η_ORC × C_OPEX

有机朗肯循环效率:
    η_ORC = 0.15 ~ 0.25

65.3 参考代码

"""
C-10 余热回收发电潜力评估
==========================
功能: 评估余热回收
输入: 余热参数
输出: 发电潜力
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class WasteHeatRecoveryAssessor:
    """余热回收评估模型"""

    def __init__(self, eta_orc: float = 0.18):
        self.eta_orc = eta_orc

    def assess(self, Q_waste_kW: float, temp_C: float,
              efficiency_factor: float = 0.7) -> Dict:
        """评估"""
        P_recoverable = Q_waste_kW * self.eta_orc * efficiency_factor
        annual_kWh = P_recoverable * 8760
        annual_saving = annual_kWh * 0.8  # 电价

        return {
            'recoverable_power_kW': P_recoverable,
            'annual_energy_kWh': annual_kWh,
            'annual_saving_yuan': annual_saving,
            'temp_C': temp_C,
            'investment_estimate_yuan': P_recoverable * 5000
        }

    def run(self, Q_waste_kW: float, temp_C: float) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        result = self.assess(Q_waste_kW, temp_C)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-10',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'waste_heat_kW': Q_waste_kW,
            **result,
            'status': 'POTENTIAL'
        }


def example_usage():
    assessor = WasteHeatRecoveryAssessor()
    result = assessor.run(Q_waste_kW=500, temp_C=300)
    print(f"可回收功率: {result['recoverable_power_kW']:.1f}kW")
    print(f"年发电量: {result['annual_energy_kWh']:.0f}kWh")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§66 C-11 照明系统逐日光节能

66.1 功能描述

模型名称: 照明系统逐日光节能 英文名称: Daylight-Based Lighting Energy Saving 技术等级: L1

基于自然光的照明节能。

66.2 核心算法公式

日光利用因子:
    Daylight_factor = E_natural / E_design

节能率:
    Saving = 1 - (E_artificial / E_without_control)

66.3 参考代码

"""
C-11 照明系统逐日光节能
========================
功能: 照明节能优化
输入: 自然光数据
输出: 节能建议
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class DaylightControlOptimizer:
    """逐日光节能模型"""

    def __init__(self, design_lux: float = 500):
        self.design_lux = design_lux

    def calculate_dimming(self, natural_lux: float) -> float:
        """计算调光"""
        if natural_lux >= self.design_lux:
            return 0  # 全关
        return 1 - (natural_lux / self.design_lux)

    def calculate_saving(self, dimming: float, hours: float, 
                        P_lamps: float) -> Dict:
        """计算节能"""
        avg_dimming = (1 + dimming) / 2
        energy_used = P_lamps * hours * avg_dimming
        energy_without = P_lamps * hours
        saving = energy_without - energy_used

        return {
            'dimming_percent': dimming * 100,
            'daily_energy_used_kWh': energy_used,
            'daily_saving_kWh': saving,
            'annual_saving_kWh': saving * 365,
            'annual_saving_yuan': saving * 365 * 0.8
        }

    def run(self, natural_lux: float, P_lamps: float = 10, 
           operating_hours: float = 10) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        dimming = self.calculate_dimming(natural_lux)
        saving = self.calculate_saving(dimming, operating_hours, P_lamps)

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-11',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'natural_light_lux': natural_lux,
            'lamp_power_kW': P_lamps,
            **saving,
            'status': 'OPTIMIZED'
        }


def example_usage():
    optimizer = DaylightControlOptimizer()
    result = optimizer.run(natural_lux=300, P_lamps=5, operating_hours=10)
    print(f"调光比例: {result['dimming_percent']:.0f}%")
    print(f"年节省: ¥{result['annual_saving_yuan']:.0f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

§67 C-12 变压器更换经济性分析

67.1 功能描述

模型名称: 变压器更换经济性分析 英文名称: Transformer Replacement Economic Analysis 技术等级: L1

评估变压器更换的经济性。

67.2 核心算法公式

年运维成本节约:
    Saving = ΔP_0 × T × Price + ΔP_k × β² × T × Price

投资回收期:
    Payback = C_new - C_old / Annual_saving

67.3 参考代码

"""
C-12 变压器更换经济性分析
===========================
功能: 评估变压器更换
输入: 新旧变压器参数
输出: 经济性分析
"""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict

class TransformerReplacementAnalyzer:
    """变压器更换经济性分析模型"""

    def analyze(self, P0_old: float, Pk_old: float, 
               P0_new: float, Pk_new: float,
               load_factor: float, hours: int = 8760,
               price: float = 0.8, C_old: float = 0, C_new: float = 100000) -> Dict:
        """分析"""
        loss_old = P0_old + Pk_old * load_factor**2
        loss_new = P0_new + Pk_new * load_factor**2
        loss_saving = loss_old - loss_new

        annual_saving_kWh = loss_saving * hours
        annual_saving_yuan = annual_saving_kWh * price

        payback = (C_new - C_old) / annual_saving_yuan if annual_saving_yuan > 0 else float('inf')

        return {
            'annual_loss_saving_kW': loss_saving,
            'annual_energy_saving_kWh': annual_saving_kWh,
            'annual_saving_yuan': annual_saving_yuan,
            'payback_years': payback,
            'ROI_percent': annual_saving_yuan / (C_new - C_old) * 100 if C_new > C_old else 0
        }

    def run(self, S_rated: float, beta: float = 0.7,
           P0_old: float = 2.0, Pk_old: float = 12.0,
           P0_new: float = 1.0, Pk_new: float = 8.0) -> Dict:
        """模型主运行函数"""
        result = self.analyze(P0_old, Pk_old, P0_new, Pk_new, beta)

        level = 'RECOMMENDED' if result['payback_years'] < 5 else \
                'CONSIDER' if result['payback_years'] < 8 else 'NOT_RECOMMENDED'

        return {
            'model_id': 'C-12',
            'timestamp': pd.Timestamp.now(),
            'rated_power_kVA': S_rated,
            'load_factor': beta * 100,
            **result,
            'recommendation': level,
            'status': 'RECOMMENDED' if result['payback_years'] < 8 else 'INFO'
        }


def example_usage():
    analyzer = TransformerReplacementAnalyzer()
    result = analyzer.run(S_rated=1000, beta=0.7)
    print(f"年节省: ¥{result['annual_saving_yuan']:.0f}")
    print(f"回收期: {result['payback_years']:.1f}年")
    print(f"建议: {result['recommendation']}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    example_usage()

技术等级说明

等级 说明 特征
L1 即插即用 基于纯计算或规则，无需训练，精度高
L2a 少量调优 需1-4周在线学习或参数校准
L2b 中等复杂度 需离线训练模型，历史数据积累
L3 研究级 依赖外部传感器或长期数据
